# Dra Thal Gyur — working English translation

Adzom W1KG11703, volume 1. All 5,466 e-text units are represented below, with separate scan-only material. This is an annotated working draft: retained source words and flagged constructions remain unresolved. It is not an independently reviewed or publication-cleared edition.

Unit IDs lead to the exact Tibetan and character offsets. Source headings, source glosses, and translator supplies remain distinguishable. The [review notes](REVIEW-NOTES.md) and [handoff](HANDOFF.md) state limitations.

## Chapter 1

[U00001](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00001) ༅།  
[U00002](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00002) Here is the text named the Jewel-Producing Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word. [N-001](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-001)  
[U00003](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00003) ༄༅།  
[U00004](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00004) Here is the Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word, Which Brings Forth Jewels. [N-T01](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t01)  
[U00005](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00005) gaṃgarbamatagā. [N-002](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-002) In the language of India:  
[U00006](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00006) Ratnakaraśabdamahāprasaṅgatantra nāma—‘The Jewel-Producing Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word.’ [N-001](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-001)  
[U00007](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00007) In the language of Tibet:  
[U00008](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00008) The Continuum of the Great All-Penetrating Word, Which Brings Forth Jewels.  

**S0001 — scan-only material, PDF page 2:** The first Ground of all lineages—
to the teacher Samantabhadra I bow. [N-017](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-017)

[U00009](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00009) To the Blessed One, the great naturally arisen, the supreme teacher,  
[U00010](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00010) glorious Samantabhadra, I pay homage.  

[U00011](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00011) [Source heading: Setting out the uncommon introductory setting.] [N-016](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-016)  
[U00012](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00012) Thus did I hear at one time:  
[U00013](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00013) At the beginning of samsara and what is beyond it,  
[U00014](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00014) from the naturally arisen and unmade,  
[U00015](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00015) there appeared an aggregate of gathered elements:  
[U00016](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00016) earth, water, fire, and wind, the four elements;  
[U00017](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00017) at the center, wind-mind, causes and conditions.  
[U00018](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00018) The immeasurable palace, gathered and generated,  
[U00019](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00019) is not contrived by naturally arisen awareness. [N-003](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-003)  
[U00020](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00020) Where delusion is entirely pure,  
[U00021](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00021) the retinue of methods in which great bliss is gathered—  
[U00022](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00022) embodiments and primordial knowing, discerning knowing, and wind—  
[U00023](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00023) is indivisible, with no diverse objects of focus.  
[U00024](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00024) The teaching of great bliss through the methods of channels and winds  
[U00025](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00025) is explained in accordance with each one's experience. [N-003](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-003)  
[U00026](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00026) There is no past, future, or present;  
[U00027](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00027) there is no division at all in the indivisible.  
[U00028](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00028) All is naturally arising primordial knowing.  

[U00029](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00029) [Source heading: Setting out the common introductory setting.] [N-016](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-016)  
[U00030](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00030) Thus did I hear at one time:  
[U00031](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00031) Endowed with Joy, Heaped with Merit—  
[U00032](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00032) the earliest realm of beings. [N-004](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-004)  
[U00033](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00033) Earth and water, fire and wind—  
[U00034](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00034) the four great elements did not appear.  
[U00035](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00035) There was no form assembled from flesh and blood  
[U00036](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00036) in that realm of greatly pure self-appearance.  
[U00037](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00037) In the utterly beautiful and expanded  
[U00038](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00038) full number of a lotus's stamens,  
[U00039](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00039) at the central site endowed with an upper part, [N-004](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-004)  
[U00040](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00040) with all qualities complete,  
[U00041](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00041) a form embodiment ripened by four conditions—  
[U00042](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00042) a miraculously born body—was assumed  
[U00043](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00043) by the teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,  
[U00044](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00044) who emanated in the beautiful form of an eight-year-old.  
[U00045](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00045) From the three gazes, the teacher's embodiment  
[U00046](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00046) appeared with inconceivable light.  
[U00047](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00047) The activity of emanation first arose.  
[U00048](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00048) In the initial setting for the arising of the continuum,  
[U00049](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00049) the seeds of the five were held. [N-005](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-005)  
[U00050](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00050) As for the miraculously born beings to be trained,  
[U00051](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00051) white in color and free of fleshly form,  
[U00052](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00052) with stainless embodiments of unimpeded penetration,  
[U00053](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00053) their coarse afflictions were self-purified;  
[U00054](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00054) they were not lying dormant,  
[U00055](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00055) and the two kinds of manifest circulation were absent. [N-005](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-005)  
[U00056](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00056) The five faculties were self-clear;  
[U00057](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00057) their distinguishing marks subsided within.  
[U00058](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00058) Coming and going dependent on breath were exhausted.  
[U00059](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00059) Having taken up abiding in the open sky,  
[U00060](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00060) they turned about and rose, back and forth.  
[U00061](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00061) Their walking, standing, and lying down,  
[U00062](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00062) their movements of stirring and running,  
[U00063](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00063) were nowhere other than in the open sky itself.  
[U00064](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00064) Light arose from themselves and appeared;  
[U00065](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00065) the light of the sun, moon, and so forth did not appear.  
[U00066](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00066) Unclothed, with a gentle manner,  
[U00067](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00067) beautiful and graceful,  
[U00068](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00068) at ease, with consciousness unimpaired,  
[U00069](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00069) as though abiding in the first meditative absorption,  
[U00070](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00070) they were free of manifest doing and agency.  
[U00071](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00071) Like the transformative agency of formless karma,  
[U00072](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00072) they did not appear through constituents together with deterioration. [N-006](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-006)  
[U00073](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00073) In particular, from the lotus of the core,  
[U00074](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00074) the forms, one thousand and one in number,  
[U00075](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00075) like rainbows and illusions,  
[U00076](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00076) were the form embodiments of the buddhas.  
[U00077](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00077) Distinct among them was the teacher's form,  
[U00078](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00078) with immeasurable patterned rays of light.  
[U00079](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00079) I, the divine youth Joy-Maker,  
[U00080](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00080) had assumed a body complete in mastery.  
[U00081](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00081) In the abode of the light rays of the teacher's speech,  
[U00082](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00082) I found bliss through intrinsic nature. [N-T05](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t05)  
[U00083](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00083) Then, through the blessing of his activity,  
[U00084](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00084) sixty-four stars of light  
[U00085](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00085) appeared as an array of forms in the open sky,  
[U00086](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00086) without having been made by anyone.  
[U00087](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00087) In particular, the seventeen utmost refinements, [N-007](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-007)  
[U00088](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00088) for the completion of the sacred teaching—  
[U00089](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00089) the actual teaching of embodiment, speech, and awakened mind, [N-T04](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t04)  
[U00090](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00090) together with vajras and embodied forms—  
[U00091](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00091) had not been made by anyone.  
[U00092](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00092) As self-appearance from the primordial outset,  
[U00093](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00093) in accordance with each one's karma and fortune,  
[U00094](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00094) they appeared in the very golden core,  
[U00095](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00095) not simultaneously to everyone.  
[U00096](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00096) Thus, like the heart, they are secret.  
[U00097](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00097) Since the ultimate supreme accomplishments  
[U00098](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00098) do not involve exertion and effort,  
[U00099](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00099) whoever encounters this is certain to be liberated.  
[U00100](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00100) Thus the Great Perfection is free from doing and effort. [N-T08](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t08)  
[U00101](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00101) It does not arise from other exertion.  
[U00102](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00102) Buddhas beyond expression  
[U00103](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00103) have paid homage from the primordial outset  
[U00104](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00104) to the dharma embodiment, in which it was certainly transmitted.  
[U00105](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00105) To make it appear to those with the appropriate karma,  
[U00106](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00106) the seed of emanation arises.  
[U00107](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00107) From that actual teaching,  
[U00108](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00108) this magical display of reflected forms,  
[U00109](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00109) which no one can express,  
[U00110](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00110) [was proclaimed] from the sky, undivided from space: [N-008](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-008)  
[U00111](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00111) even before all phrases,  
[U00112](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00112) from still above the ‘nine, two, and four,’ [N-008](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-008)  
[U00113](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00113) it was proclaimed in the melody of Great Brahmā.  
[U00114](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00114) Then, from the space of the nature of phenomena,  
[U00115](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00115) through the speech of the Great All-Pervader,  
[U00116](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00116) blessing displayed it as its own sound. [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U00117](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00117) Then, from space free of directions,  
[U00118](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00118) from the melodious sound of the kalaviṅka,  
[U00119](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00119) which gathers the quintessence of all teachings,  
[U00120](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00120) this very king of continua,  
[U00121](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00121) even before all phrases,  
[U00122](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00122) was drawn from three hundred and sixty  
[U00123](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00123) sounds and spoken by the teacher.  
[U00124](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00124) Then, the sounds of the individual elements,  
[U00125](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00125) from the agencies of heat and cold—  
[U00126](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00126) eight thousand individual melodious sounds—  
[U00127](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00127) appeared blessed by the teacher.  
[U00128](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00128) In particular, in the speech that distinguishes,  
[U00129](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00129) the Ground from which words and phrases arise [N-T09](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t09)  
[U00130](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00130) was taught in sixty limbs by the teacher,  
[U00131](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00131) who spoke to the gathered retinue of self-appearance.  
[U00132](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00132) What was the enlightened intent of this?  
[U00133](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00133) The manner of speaking had three enlightened intents:  
[U00134](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00134) antidote, condensation, and the meaning for the faculties. [N-009](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-009)  
[U00135](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00135) The phrases were condensed and made to appear.  
[U00136](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00136) No other phrases expressing their subject matter  
[U00137](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00137) are spoken by the elements of the three times.  
[U00138](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00138) Words and phrases were definitively condensed into names;  
[U00139](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00139) even the kalaviṅka's sounds,  
[U00140](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00140) for drawing in, engaging, and being pleasing,  
[U00141](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00141) were condensed from twelve clarifiers of word and meaning  
[U00142](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00142) into four or five expressive phrases. [N-010](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-010)  
[U00143](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00143) Definitively condensed into the teacher's speech,  
[U00144](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00144) there were a thousand and five in Brahmā's melody,  
[U00145](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00145) a hundred and three definite expressive forms,  
[U00146](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00146) definitively condensed into thirty lengtheners.  
[U00147](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00147) There were sixteen clarifiers among them,  
[U00148](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00148) sixty-two limbs of ‘realization-clearing,’  
[U00149](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00149) and forty-two limbs free from conceptual elaborations. [N-010](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-010)  
[U00150](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00150) The two classes specially condensed  
[U00151](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00151) are the foundation of words and phrases.  
[U00152](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00152) What is the enlightened intent of that?  
[U00153](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00153) Through each one's karmic magic,  
[U00154](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00154) the roots of training—‘fourteen, two’— [N-011](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-011)  
[U00155](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00155) those skilled in designs  
[U00156](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00156) bring forth emanations and so on  
[U00157](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00157) and know the meanings of constant display. [N-011](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-011)  
[U00158](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00158) In the magical display of what was thus spoken,  
[U00159](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00159) even before all continua,  
[U00160](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00160) from the root words and phrases of the All-Penetrating,  
[U00161](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00161) all certainly arose and proliferated as teaching.  
[U00162](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00162) The sources of the sacred teaching were established;  
[U00163](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00163) by its being spoken, samsara is made to subside.  
[U00164](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00164) Its qualities are beyond thought.  
[U00165](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00165) At the beginning of the emanated teaching,  
[U00166](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00166) it increased through the arising of this.  
[U00167](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00167) At the outset, when all bodies were miraculously born,  
[U00168](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00168) in the age of immeasurable lifespans,  
[U00169](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00169) for miraculously born beings,  
[U00170](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00170) intrinsic nature was undivided and perfectly pure.  
[U00171](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00171) All was expressed as indivisible in essence;  
[U00172](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00172) words and phrases were bliss-awareness.  
[U00173](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00173) The dharma embodiment too was without cause;  
[U00174](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00174) the complete enjoyment embodiment spread out, all-pervading; [N-T07](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t07)  
[U00175](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00175) the Ground for the emanation embodiment's elaboration was complete.  
[U00176](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00176) Without division, they entered balanced union,  
[U00177](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00177) and in the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature  
[U00178](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00178) abided together in a single maṇḍala.  
[U00179](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00179) Thus they were indivisible, of a single condition.  
[U00180](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00180) Then, one with this and not separate, [Small-print source gloss: ‘Compiler/questioner; the naturally arisen array.’] [N-012](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-012)  
[U00181](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00181) From among the retinue whose intrinsic nature was pure,  
[U00182](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00182) the Lord of Gods made this request:  
[U00183](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00183) ‘Ema! Listen, listen, Blessed One!  
[U00184](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00184) This sacred appearance,  
[U00185](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00185) this inconceivable gathering—  
[U00186](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00186) the intrinsic nature of its phrases is thus:  
[U00187](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00187) What are the enlightened intent, purpose, and arising of the four elements? [N-013](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-013)  
[U00188](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00188) How are all the meanings of the limbs pursued?  
[U00189](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00189) What is the meaning of its enlightened intent?  
[U00190](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00190) How extensive is the expression of words?  
[U00191](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00191) How many sites of penetration are there?  
[U00192](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00192) [Source insertion: At this point, the questions corresponding to the two passages below, beginning ‘one's own and others' assertions’ and ‘the projecting is wind,’ have not occurred.] What are transformation and its agents? [N-013](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-013) [N-T20](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t20)  
[U00193](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00193) What clearly expresses the chapters?  
[U00194](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00194) Who desires this perfection?  
[U00195](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00195) What is the meaning of the arising of the introductory setting?  
[U00196](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00196) How many remaining teachings are there?  
[U00197](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00197) From what does the cause of destruction arise?  
[U00198](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00198) After destruction, from where does abiding arise?  
[U00199](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00199) Who maintains it in the interval?  
[U00200](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00200) Finally, into what does this very thing turn?  
[U00201](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00201) What are the body and its limbs like?  
[U00202](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00202) What do the elements desire?  
[U00203](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00203) What are the successive ages of sentient beings?  
[U00204](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00204) What are the outer, the inner, and the meaning?  
[U00205](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00205) What are the transitions of time?  
[U00206](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00206) What are days, months, and years?  
[U00207](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00207) What sustains life and vitality?  
[U00208](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00208) What is the endpoint of dispersal, penetration, and balance? [N-014](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-014)  
[U00209](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00209) What cuts the continuum of samsara's path?  
[U00210](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00210) What accomplishes the teacher's qualities?  
[U00211](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00211) How should one train in the essence of the three embodiments?  
[U00212](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00212) What is the scope of common activity?  
[U00213](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00213) How are the individual embodiments accomplished?  
[U00214](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00214) What are sleep and dreams like?  
[U00215](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00215) To what should cultivation for taming ordinary mind be applied?  
[U00216](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00216) What is the enlightened intent of what is called wind?  
[U00217](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00217) How many qualities do the channels have?  
[U00218](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00218) Likewise, what are the aspects of the spheres?  
[U00219](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00219) What is the scope of the ḍākinīs' activity?  
[U00220](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00220) What is the activity belonging to the aspects of transference?  
[U00221](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00221) Of what is dependent connection the cause?  
[U00222](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00222) By what are illness and suffering liberated?  
[U00223](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00223) What is the manner of the elements' transference?  
[U00224](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00224) Which elements belong to the crucial points?  
[U00225](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00225) What limbs of activity belong to calculation and conjunction?  
[U00226](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00226) By what is the bliss of the elements accomplished?  
[U00227](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00227) By what is the activity of relocation carried out?  
[U00228](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00228) What distinguishes the aware from the insentient?  
[U00229](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00229) What method reverses the crucial points themselves?  
[U00230](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00230) By what is the enlightened intent of the tenets established?  
[U00231](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00231) What is the enlightened intent concerning the ultimate?  
[U00232](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00232) How many stages of accomplishment are there?  
[U00233](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00233) How do the natural states abide?  
[U00234](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00234) How should the reckonings of delusion be held?  
[U00235](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00235) By what are elaborations about objects of knowledge cut?  
[U00236](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00236) What is the method of relying on a lama?  
[U00237](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00237) How are the outer elemental signs discerned?  
[U00238](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00238) What are the junctures of time and teaching?  
[U00239](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00239) What is the rite of conferring empowerment?  
[U00240](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00240) What are the pledges and vows like?  
[U00241](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00241) What subject matter is taught through phrases?  
[U00242](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00242) Beyond phrases, by what are elaborations cut?  
[U00243](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00243) How is this applied to the crucial point of direct perception?  
[U00244](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00244) By what are samsara and nirvana separated? [N-T11](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t11)  
[U00245](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00245) How many aspects of activity are there in retaining wind?  
[U00246](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00246) How many stages are there in training to hold ordinary mind? [N-T15](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t15)  
[U00247](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00247) What are the outer, inner, and secret signs of death?  
[U00248](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00248) By what is viewing brought to its endpoint?  
[U00249](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00249) What are the life-pillars of cultivation?  
[U00250](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00250) How is activity applied?  
[U00251](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00251) How extensive is the site of attaining the result?  
[U00252](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00252) By what is samsara's flowing stream cut?  
[U00253](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00253) What is the taking of nourishment from vital essences? [N-T16](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t16)  
[U00254](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00254) By what is clothing accomplished?  
[U00255](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00255) How is the flow of the elements cut? [N-T17](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t17)  
[U00256](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00256) By what are the tastes of the four balanced?  
[U00257](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00257) How do qualities arise in the treasury?  
[U00258](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00258) By what are the noble ones' miraculous powers accomplished?  
[U00259](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00259) How should one enjoy the treasures?  
[U00260](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00260) By what are the five Grounds brought under control?  
[U00261](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00261) I have not previously known these things.  
[U00262](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00262) Lord of appearance, speak to me!  
[U00263](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00263) By clearly distinguishing their sequence for me,  
[U00264](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00264) in the times to come,  
[U00265](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00265) all who have faith in and aspire to this  
[U00266](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00266) will come to trust this Great Perfection.  
[U00267](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00267) The simple, free from doing, will find great bliss. [N-019](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-019)  
[U00268](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00268) When samsara is exhausted to its very end,  
[U00269](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00269) they will find the entrance to the path beyond sorrow.  
[U00270](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00270) Therefore, whatever I have asked,  
[U00271](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00271) clearly distinguish its sequence and speak!’  
[U00272](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00272) Then—[Source heading: The array setting out the greatness of the continuum]—the all-pervading lord, the hero, [N-020](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-020)  
[U00273](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00273) though words and phrases were not proclaimed as names,  
[U00274](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00274) in space whose intrinsic nature is uncontrived,  
[U00275](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00275) without being written or arranged,  
[U00276](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00276) possessed a melody with sixty limbs.  
[U00277](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00277) Like a mere unwritten design,  
[U00278](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00278) to the faculties endowed with luster,  
[U00279](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00279) it certainly appeared in direct perception.  
[U00280](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00280) Also in the sound of Great Brahmā:  
[U00281](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00281) ‘Indra, sovereign of the gods, listen!  
[U00282](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00282) Having thoroughly disciplined body, speech, and mental faculty, [N-021](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-021)  
[U00283](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00283) abandon wrong activity and thinking.  
[U00284](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00284) Having purified the faculties and so forth,  
[U00285](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00285) listen with ordinary mind from which stains have been abandoned.  
[U00286](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00286) Ema! How wonderful and astonishing!  
[U00287](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00287) This, not spoken by the buddhas,  
[U00288](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00288) surpasses other continua.  
[U00289](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00289) So that there may be no return to abiding in the three realms,  
[U00290](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00290) this most secret, definitive command,  
[U00291](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00291) for increasing the quintessence of the teaching,  
[U00292](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00292) is the very highest summit of the vehicles.  
[U00293](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00293) It gathers, through meaning, the mirror of the teachings;  
[U00294](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00294) it is the commentary on the command, the heart of the pith instructions,  
[U00295](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00295) the great perfection whose intrinsic nature is secret,  
[U00296](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00296) the heart-commentary, the eye of authoritative transmission,  
[U00297](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00297) the most secret key, the inner depth of the view,  
[U00298](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00298) the training ground of activity, the crucial point of cultivation,  
[U00299](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00299) the cutter of the succession of wombs, the sweeper of the lower destinies. [N-022](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-022)  
[U00300](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00300) I will explain the supreme secret continuum;  
[U00301](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00301) retinue who also hold the vajra, listen!  
[U00302](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00302) In the phrases I elaborate,  
[U00303](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00303) there is not a particle of the dust of delusion.  
[U00304](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00304) The teachings taught from this point onward  
[U00305](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00305) are certain to be accomplished and to bring forth meaning;  
[U00306](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00306) without doubt they yield the result.  
[U00307](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00307) Therefore I teach the quintessence of the teaching.  
[U00308](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00308) Listen, listen, Joy-Maker—take hold of this!’  
[U00309](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00309) As the phrases of the questions were clearly distinguished,  
[U00310](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00310) from the All-Pervader's voice,  
[U00311](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00311) emanations of letters such as these,  
[U00312](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00312) in space whose intrinsic nature is uncontrived,  
[U00313](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00313) unarranged phrases, naturally arisen,  
[U00314](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00314) appeared in the manner of questions and replies.  
[U00315](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00315) Accomplished through the settling of the basic condition, [N-023](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-023)  
[U00316](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00316) they appeared thus in the Ground from which phrases arise:  
[U00317](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00317) [Source note: Though the questions are said to number seventy, there are sixty-six.] [N-024](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-024)  

[U00318](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00318) [Source heading: First reply.]  
[U00319](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00319) The purposes of the elements are as follows:  
[U00320](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00320) Water gathers the refined part  
[U00321](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00321) and performs the activity of separating the dregs  
[U00322](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00322) in each individual body.  
[U00323](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00323) Recognize its purpose, measure, and number.  
[U00324](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00324) Earth supports the foundation  
[U00325](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00325) and performs the activities of forming and establishing.  
[U00326](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00326) Through ripening, it generates channels and so forth.  
[U00327](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00327) Know its purpose in the conventional sphere.  
[U00328](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00328) Fire clears away the elements [Source variant note: ‘increases’ also occurs], [N-025](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-025)  
[U00329](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00329) and performs the activities of ripening and clarifying.  
[U00330](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00330) By blazing and spreading, it generates the eyes and so forth.  
[U00331](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00331) It makes one know its purpose in the forms of warmth.  
[U00332](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00332) Wind supports and lifts,  
[U00333](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00333) and performs the activities of stirring and swaying.  
[U00334](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00334) By vibrating and fluttering, it sustains the body.  
[U00335](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00335) Know its purpose in the abiding of ordinary mind.  
[U00336](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00336) The great maṇḍala of these four elements  
[U00337](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00337) acts as the cause of the formation of the body and so forth.  
[U00338](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00338) In the interval, it brings about abiding and maintenance;  
[U00339](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00339) at the end, it performs all the activities of destruction.  
[U00340](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00340) The great elements, having a single cause,  
[U00341](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00341) appear as the masters of diverse functions.  
[U00342](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00342) Thus their purposes, for self and other,  
[U00343](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00343) are skilled in the meaning of completing what is to be done.  

[U00344](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00344) [Source heading: Second reply.]  
[U00345](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00345) The meaning of the limbs is as follows:  
[U00346](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00346) The clear sequence of phrases expressing  
[U00347](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00347) the knowing of the buddhas  
[U00348](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00348) is explained through the presentation of the title and through the text,  
[U00349](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00349) so that conceptual minds of the gradual and simultaneous types  
[U00350](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00350) may realize it. [N-T18](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t18)  
[U00351](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00351) The text is taught in two aspects:  
[U00352](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00352) the establishment of the body itself,  
[U00353](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00353) and its extensive division through the meanings of the limbs.  
[U00354](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00354) The body is the two aspects of the introductory setting.  
[U00355](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00355) In order to surpass others in particular,  
[U00356](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00356) it is applied to the uncommon body.  
[U00357](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00357) In order to accord in approach with other vehicles,  
[U00358](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00358) the common introductory setting  
[U00359](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00359) is taught to inspire trust  
[U00360](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00360) in the compiler's own retinue.  
[U00361](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00361) Arising from the Ground of training as that which trains,  
[U00362](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00362) it makes the teaching abide.  
[U00363](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00363) The limbs are examples and the meanings of phrases.  
[U00364](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00364) Through the six chapters of the text itself,  
[U00365](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00365) the meanings of the phrases, just as they abide,  
[U00366](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00366) are shown like opening the interior of a body  
[U00367](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00367) and revealing its solid and hollow organs and so forth.  
[U00368](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00368) The meanings of the phrases have two aspects,  
[U00369](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00369) spoken as questions and replies.  
[U00370](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00370) The replies are explained in two ways: briefly and extensively.  
[U00371](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00371) To suit the inclinations of the individuals who are to be trained,  
[U00372](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00372) diverse elaborations are the meaning.  

[U00373](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00373) [Source heading: Third reply.]  
[U00374](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00374) Ema! In the definite arising of the continua,  
[U00375](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00375) there are two aspects of enlightened intent.  
[U00376](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00376) For those whose conceptual mind concerns awareness as self-appearance, [N-026](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-026)  
[U00377](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00377) the meaning of the continuum's title is distinguished.  
[U00378](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00378) Through the three—sign, word, and meaning—  
[U00379](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00379) direct perception of the nature of phenomena is made the path.  
[U00380](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00380) Once the flow of samsara in the three realms has been cut,  
[U00381](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00381) because there is no turning back again and again,  
[U00382](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00382) one knows the defining characteristic of exhaustion.  
[U00383](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00383) For conceptual minds that focus on objects,  
[U00384](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00384) the texts of the continua are distinguished.  
[U00385](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00385) The definitive crucial point corresponding to each inclination  
[U00386](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00386) is applied to each one individually.  
[U00387](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00387) If it is fully realized without separation,  
[U00388](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00388) one does not abide in samsara.  
[U00389](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00389) People who delight in elaboration itself,  
[U00390](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00390) having trained in diverse methods,  
[U00391](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00391) also take pride in their respective accomplishments. [N-026](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-026)  
[U00392](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00392) The pith instructions on the meaning of the crucial points of enlightened intent  
[U00393](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00393) should be known in their respective contexts.  

[U00394](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00394) [Source heading: Fourth reply.]  
[U00395](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00395) Through this very king of continua,  
[U00396](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00396) the varieties of its definitive title  
[U00397](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00397) are taught; as for the pith instructions on the meaning of perfection,  
[U00398](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00398) the phrases alone are examined and distinguished with certainty.  
[U00399](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00399) The syllables, too, in accord with their meanings,  
[U00400](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00400) are brought to the face of meaning in this way:  
[U00401](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00401) This manner of expressing sound [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U00402](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00402) has not been explained in other continua.  
[U00403](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00403) Ema! How greatly wonderful!  
[U00404](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00404) I will elaborate it; listen to me.  
[U00405](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00405) The occasions of sound have five aspects:  
[U00406](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00406) Brahmā, the All-Pervader, the kalaviṅka,  
[U00407](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00407) the sounds of the elements, and the teacher's speech.  
[U00408](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00408) Through these five, in the phenomena that arise,  
[U00409](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00409) one engages freely in great play.  
[U00410](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00410) For the five sites of Brahmā,  
[U00411](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00411) there are two hundred for each,  
[U00412](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00412) and one acting as a further site for each.  
[U00413](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00413) Since there are a thousand and five further sites,  
[U00414](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00414) merely by knowing this  
[U00415](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00415) one is brought into contact with the Blessed One.  
[U00416](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00416) As for the All-Pervader, from further sites,  
[U00417](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00417) by two and five and ‘six, three,’ [N-027](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-027)  
[U00418](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00418) the cause of the activity of parting the side-locks—  
[U00419](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00419) when the grouping with its marks is complete,  
[U00420](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00420) there are a hundred for each of these. [N-027](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-027)  
[U00421](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00421) From the aspects of the magic that definitely distinguishes,  
[U00422](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00422) if one knows how to express each of those numbers,  
[U00423](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00423) higher knowing is accomplished in one's continuum.  
[U00424](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00424) Through the melodious sounds of the kalaviṅka,  
[U00425](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00425) there are a hundred and twenty for the body,  
[U00426](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00426) and likewise for speech and ordinary mind—  
[U00427](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00427) the same number as above is spoken in definite expression.  
[U00428](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00428) When the worldly gods,  
[U00429](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00429) likewise the gods beyond the world,  
[U00430](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00430) and the ḍākinīs gather,  
[U00431](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00431) if one knows the distinctions of their sounds,  
[U00432](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00432) one knows their languages.  
[U00433](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00433) When one definitely engages in the six kinds of higher knowing, [N-T19](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t19)  
[U00434](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00434) one knows the meanings of the Bliss-Gone One's commands.  
[U00435](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00435) Through the number of verses,  
[U00436](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00436) one is definitively connected with the individual sites.  
[U00437](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00437) Then, in the sound of water too,  
[U00438](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00438) eloquent speech is inconceivable.  
[U00439](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00439) For the skilled, the rite is complete.  
[U00440](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00440) As for the word and meaning of the elements,  
[U00441](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00441) through the hero and the hero-goer, [N-028](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-028)  
[U00442](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00442) in each of the four—earth, water, fire, and wind—  
[U00443](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00443) there are two apiece.  
[U00444](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00444) Since the neutral one is empty,  
[U00445](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00445) from the distinction between male and female  
[U00446](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00446) there are the causal agencies of heat and cold.  
[U00447](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00447) There are two thousand for each;  
[U00448](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00448) if a yogin knows how to express  
[U00449](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00449) the sounds of expression and designation,  
[U00450](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00450) then in the languages of all of these,  
[U00451](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00451) one delights in listening with ease and well-being.  
[U00452](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00452) Whoever has definitely mastered this  
[U00453](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00453) is definitively connected with the six classes;  
[U00454](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00454) they appear directly, together with their times.  
[U00455](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00455) The teachers of the past, future, and present,  
[U00456](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00456) the buddhas, the Blessed Ones,  
[U00457](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00457) with a single melody, in the maṇḍala,  
[U00458](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00458) for the appearance of the fortunate,  
[U00459](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00459) through the complete purity of the six faculties,  
[U00460](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00460) in tens apiece, from the three embodiments, [N-029](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-029)  
[U00461](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00461) proliferate in particular from the emanation embodiment.  
[U00462](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00462) Merely through a ray of that proliferation,  
[U00463](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00463) the yogin's stream of conceptual thought ceases.  
[U00464](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00464) The sites, distinctions, and domains of their vital cores [N-029](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-029)  
[U00465](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00465) are attained, and one assuredly enters seeing and realization.  
[U00466](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00466) Thus the purposes of sound  
[U00467](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00467) should be known in two aspects:  
[U00468](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00468) supreme and common—  
[U00469](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00469) the individually distinguishing magic for those with the appropriate karma.  
[U00470](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00470) For the supreme, the five embodiments are manifestly accomplished.  
[U00471](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00471) Through the ten signs of such accomplishment,  
[U00472](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00472) one equals the body of Vajradhara.  
[U00473](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00473) Its qualities cannot be exhausted in words.  
[U00474](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00474) In phenomena and the basic space of phenomena,  
[U00475](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00475) in the Ground, path, and result,  
[U00476](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00476) one plays in bliss.  
[U00477](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00477) The common purposes are these:  
[U00478](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00478) treatises on language and valid cognition,  
[U00479](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00479) crafts and various magical skills,  
[U00480](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00480) archery and activities not previously trained in are accomplished. [N-030](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-030)  
[U00481](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00481) One can act upon the five elements,  
[U00482](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00482) prevails over weapons and poison,  
[U00483](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00483) and becomes skilled in the sounds of the insentient and the aware,  
[U00484](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00484) in signs and conventions.  
[U00485](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00485) In brief, one comes to know the treatises on purposes  
[U00486](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00486) and the defining characteristics of the commands.  
[U00487](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00487) One distinguishes the varieties of medicines and elixir preparations,  
[U00488](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00488) brings ‘penetrating letters’—thal-yig—to perfection, [N-030](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-030)  
[U00489](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00489) and worldly applications are spontaneously accomplished.  
[U00490](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00490) Thus the purposes of word and meaning are accomplished.  
[U00491](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00491) The distinguishing magic definitive for the continuum is set apart.  

[U00492](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00492) [Source heading: Fifth reply.]  
[U00493](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00493) There are eight aspects of the sites of penetration:  
[U00494](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00494) world systems and buddha-fields,  
[U00495](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00495) faults in assertions and the sites of faults,  
[U00496](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00496) the activity of projection and application,  
[U00497](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00497) what is acknowledged and the crucial point of going—eight in all. [N-031](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-031) [N-T21](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t21)  
[U00498](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00498) These are held to be the sites of penetration.  
[U00499](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00499) Regarding a world system: the times of its formation,  
[U00500](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00500) abiding, and destruction;  
[U00501](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00501) emptiness, causes and conditions;  
[U00502](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00502) its manner of establishment and its measure of extension. [N-032](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-032)  
[U00503](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00503) The buddha-fields have thirteen aspects:  
[U00504](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00504) beneath the world here  
[U00505](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00505) is the realm called Melody of Penetration,  
[U00506](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00506) its extent difficult to measure, utterly beautiful.  
[U00507](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00507) Its causes, conditions, and intrinsic nature,  
[U00508](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00508) the teacher's teaching and its retinue,  
[U00509](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00509) above, below, and in the directions and intermediate directions—  
[U00510](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00510) its length and breadth are pleasing, well-arrayed and adorned.  
[U00511](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00511) Then, six worlds below,  
[U00512](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00512) that called Holder of Penetration  
[U00513](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00513) is wheel-shaped, with unimpeded penetration.  
[U00514](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00514) The teacher's teaching is truth itself;  
[U00515](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00515) well-adorned in its array, it is the second.  
[U00516](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00516) Three worlds to the right of this,  
[U00517](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00517) that called Protector of Penetration  
[U00518](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00518) is entirely adorned with emeralds.  
[U00519](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00519) Its light and colors are definitely clear;  
[U00520](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00520) it is a field where the teacher's teaching trains beings,  
[U00521](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00521) each with their own karma and characteristics.  
[U00522](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00522) Then, six worlds above,  
[U00523](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00523) that called Spreading Penetration  
[U00524](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00524) resembles the shape of an opened lotus,  
[U00525](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00525) very extensive and level.  
[U00526](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00526) Diverse light blazes; its colors  
[U00527](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00527) appear white and red, intermingling.  
[U00528](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00528) Coarse reflected forms do not appear.  
[U00529](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00529) The sources of the teacher's teaching expand;  
[U00530](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00530) with the age complete, it is called Penetration.  
[U00531](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00531) Above this world,  
[U00532](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00532) still higher, beyond nineteen worlds,  
[U00533](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00533) is that called Club of Penetration.  
[U00534](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00534) Its region is utterly complete and delightful [Source variant note: ‘pleasant’ also occurs]. [N-033](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-033)  
[U00535](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00535) With diverse small jewels,  
[U00536](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00536) it is definitively adorned in the manner of heaps.  
[U00537](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00537) The teacher's teaching possesses the two completions;  
[U00538](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00538) it is an array of garlands of skulls.  
[U00539](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00539) In the world's northeastern intermediate direction,  
[U00540](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00540) that called Flow of Penetration  
[U00541](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00541) resembles a stūpa in shape, with beautiful tiers,  
[U00542](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00542) entirely adorned with five arrays. [N-033](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-033)  
[U00543](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00543) It is the Ground from which diverse sounds and melodies arise.  
[U00544](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00544) The teacher's teaching certainly arises at its time.  
[U00545](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00545) Twenty-one worlds above it,  
[U00546](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00546) is that called Fully Pervaded by Penetration.  
[U00547](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00547) In the intrinsic nature of a pure object of focus,  
[U00548](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00548) it appears with unimpeded penetration, without outside or inside.  
[U00549](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00549) The teacher's teaching displays arising;  
[U00550](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00550) its active causes and conditions: the All-Pervader. [N-032](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-032)  
[U00551](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00551) To the left, in this world's southeast,  
[U00552](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00552) beyond six world systems,  
[U00553](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00553) is that called Sound of Penetration,  
[U00554](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00554) thickly covered with moving pennants.  
[U00555](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00555) Victory banners, parasols, and so forth are raised.  
[U00556](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00556) With showers of scattered flowers and fragrance,  
[U00557](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00557) the teacher and the teaching  
[U00558](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00558) are surrounded by hosts of beautifying reflected forms.  
[U00559](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00559) The play of bliss arises.  
[U00560](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00560) To the right, in this world's southwest,  
[U00561](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00561) six worlds away and below, is Wind of Penetration.  
[U00562](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00562) Swaying and active in the open sky,  
[U00563](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00563) moving back and forth and floating,  
[U00564](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00564) it brings forth sounds through movement.  
[U00565](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00565) The teacher's teaching is stirred by wind.  
[U00566](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00566) There are three engagements endowed with five. [N-032](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-032)  
[U00567](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00567) Thirteen worlds stacked above it,  
[U00568](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00568) is that called Jewel Penetration,  
[U00569](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00569) adorned with auspicious suns,  
[U00570](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00570) ornamented with the qualities of desire,  
[U00571](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00571) beautiful with magical designs and letters.  
[U00572](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00572) The teacher's teaching is complete in reflected forms,  
[U00573](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00573) beautiful with ‘lda-ldi’ lotus eyes. [N-034](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-034)  
[U00574](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00574) Above this world's northwestern intermediate direction,  
[U00575](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00575) in the sixth world system,  
[U00576](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00576) is that called Gathering Penetration,  
[U00577](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00577) with goddesses whose offerings are complete,  
[U00578](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00578) filled with light and the fragrance of sandalwood.  
[U00579](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00579) The teacher's teaching is equal in its parts,  
[U00580](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00580) surrounded by beautiful forms, sixteen years of age,  
[U00581](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00581) utterly youthful.  
[U00582](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00582) Nine worlds below it,  
[U00583](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00583) is that called Conch-Endowed Deity of Penetration,  
[U00584](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00584) faithful and intoxicated by sweet fragrance,  
[U00585](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00585) with flashing, intensely blazing light,  
[U00586](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00586) densely filled with intensely tremulous brightness. [N-034](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-034)  
[U00587](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00587) The teacher and the teaching,  
[U00588](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00588) its retinue and enjoyments, are complete.  
[U00589](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00589) In the sixth world from here,  
[U00590](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00590) is that called Star Penetration.  
[U00591](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00591) Its form of stacked wheels  
[U00592](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00592) is completely piled up in fives and nines.  
[U00593](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00593) It is adorned with awnings and hanging garlands. [N-034](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-034)  
[U00594](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00594) Thunder roars and red tongues of lightning issue forth.  
[U00595](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00595) The teacher's teaching is surrounded by authoritative transmissions.  
[U00596](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00596) Garlands of arising light and the faces of nāgas [Source variant notes: glu and gdung also occur] [N-035](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-035)  
[U00597](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00597) illuminate all directions and intermediate directions.  

[U00598](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00598) [Source heading: Sixth reply.]  
[U00599](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00599) The phrases of one's own and others' assertions  
[U00600](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00600) are decisively resolved through one's own phrases. [N-036](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-036)  
[U00601](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00601) To bring one's own position to a definite conclusion,  
[U00602](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00602) through authoritative transmission, reasoning, and pith instructions,  
[U00603](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00603) remove faults, establish it, and abandon contradictions.  
[U00604](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00604) Abandon the three faults and dispel doubt.  
[U00605](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00605) Take up the four logical reasons and an assertion.  
[U00606](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00606) Seize the fortress of abiding and flee to the side. [N-036](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-036)  
[U00607](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00607) Draw a wheel in the center of the four directions.  
[U00608](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00608) Establish a logical reason free from taking sides.  
[U00609](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00609) Against others, debate the definitive application of a consequence. [N-T21](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t21)  
[U00610](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00610) Surround assertion, what is to be proved, and logical reason  
[U00611](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00611) with projections, three by three.  
[U00612](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00612) The skilled apply these with their defining characteristics complete.  
[U00613](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00613) This is the subject matter established for oneself and others.  
[U00614](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00614) Faults are of two kinds: one's own and others'.  
[U00615](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00615) Know one's own faults oneself.  
[U00616](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00616) In assertion, logical reason, and what is to be proved,  
[U00617](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00617) identify each individual fault  
[U00618](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00618) and bring the qualities to completion.  
[U00619](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00619) Know the other's qualities;  
[U00620](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00620) let the skilled examine that person's faults.  
[U00621](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00621) From the mere phrases expressing word and meaning,  
[U00622](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00622) through the defining characteristics of connection and nonconnection,  
[U00623](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00623) reckon the consequences for the pervaded meanings. [N-036](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-036)  

[U00624](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00624) [Source heading: Seventh reply.]  
[U00625](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00625) Projection is wind, the activity of transference.  
[U00626](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00626) It mounts phrases and angles [Source variant note: gzhog also occurs]. [N-037](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-037)  
[U00627](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00627) Above and below the wheel of logical reasons,  
[U00628](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00628) it penetrates the reverse and the angles.  
[U00629](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00629) Through the modes of subject and property, [N-T20](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t20)  
[U00630](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00630) engaging two by two, it projects.  
[U00631](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00631) Wind also projects the afflictions.  
[U00632](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00632) Through the impure [wind]—primordial knowing. [Source variant note: ni also occurs.] [N-038](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-038)  
[U00633](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00633) With an object of focus for application, maintain one's own position.  
[U00634](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00634) Subject, assertion, and logical reason,  
[U00635](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00635) indicated by an example, form one application.  
[U00636](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00636) Know the subject's connection through the object.  
[U00637](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00637) Examine the faults of an assertion before making it.  
[U00638](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00638) Through logical reasons, reckon the meaning of the connection.  
[U00639](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00639) Through an example, establish the phenomena that appear.  
[U00640](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00640) Tenets depend on the conceptual mind,  
[U00641](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00641) through the expressiveness of knowing in oneself and others.  
[U00642](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00642) From what is acknowledged, consequences follow. [N-T21](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t21)  
[U00643](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00643) The activities of doing and of analysis,  
[U00644](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00644) differences between phrases and realization,  
[U00645](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00645) the various junctures at which causes and conditions ripen,  
[U00646](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00646) wishing to go and having gone,  
[U00647](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00647) with different particular modes of connection—  
[U00648](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00648) what is called ‘gone’: the objects,  
[U00649](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00649) paths and sites, phrases and engagement,  
[U00650](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00650) mindfulness, the actual, and what is not an entity, [N-039](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-039)  
[U00651](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00651) abiding, arising, and acting [Source variant note: ‘done’ also occurs], [N-039](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-039)  
[U00652](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00652) paths with reversal and paths without reversal,  
[U00653](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00653) what has been accomplished and the act of accomplishing—  
[U00654](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00654) these are the causes of what is done and what does it.  

[U00655](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00655) [Source heading: Eighth reply.]  
[U00656](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00656) To draw the definitive reasons together at their conclusion:  
[U00657](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00657) transformation occurs through the magical display of the elements.  
[U00658](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00658) Because seasons change through time,  
[U00659](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00659) the yogin's experience and knowing also change.  
[U00660](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00660) Let the skilled discern the times of change.  
[U00661](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00661) Through the course of wind, fire, earth, and water,  
[U00662](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00662) fire, wind, earth, and water revolve in ‘four, two.’ [N-040](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-040)  
[U00663](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00663) Earth, wind, water, and fire—two agents of change.  
[U00664](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00664) Wind, earth, fire, and water, from five parts,  
[U00665](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00665) transform and act as the body's Ground.  
[U00666](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00666) Sites of emptiness—there are eight.  
[U00667](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00667) The expressiveness of knowing that brings transformation:  
[U00668](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00668) proliferation, coiling, and spreading,  
[U00669](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00669) gathering and signs—regarding these,  
[U00670](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00670) drawing in, abiding, and transforming,  
[U00671](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00671) wind, wind, earth, earth, fire, fire, water—  
[U00672](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00672) from each one's sixteen functions,  
[U00673](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00673) pervading, entering, causing abiding, and guarding.  
[U00674](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00674) ‘Transmission-pairing,’ single-sided pairing, and one common grouping [N-040](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-040)  
[U00675](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00675) are the agents of change that make one heroic.  

[U00676](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00676) [Source heading: Ninth reply.]  
[U00677](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00677) Clearly distinguishing the two—chapters and their expression— [N-041](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-041)  
[U00678](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00678) the Array of Diversity, the abode of pith instructions,  
[U00679](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00679) is the first: definite arising.  
[U00680](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00680) It is the Ground, definitely showing what is yet to come.  
[U00681](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00681) Taking up the Ground together with the introductory setting,  
[U00682](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00682) the Array of the Gathered Crucial Points, the support of ordinary mind,  
[U00683](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00683) is the second: bringing [it] down upon itself. [N-041](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-041)  
[U00684](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00684) It shows what certainly arises from the secret command and the extent of change.  
[U00685](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00685) The third, the Array of Primordial Knowing,  
[U00686](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00686) shows the foundation for indication by words and signs.  
[U00687](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00687) The fourth, the Array of the Nature of Phenomena,  
[U00688](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00688) is the transmission on the nature of ordinary mind, complete in qualities. [N-T22](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t22)  
[U00689](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00689) The fifth is the Array of View and Cultivation,  
[U00690](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00690) the crucial point of basic space, complete in freedom from doing.  
[U00691](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00691) The sixth is the Array of Embodiments,  
[U00692](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00692) the secret Ground, complete in qualities.  
[U00693](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00693) Thus, through these six,  
[U00694](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00694) the result of the three embodiments is attained.  
[U00695](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00695) Know the chapter divisions that show their scope.  
[U00696](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00696) The treasure from which glorious enlightened intent arises,  
[U00697](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00697) also the embodiment of the method of bliss,  
[U00698](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00698) prophecy, guidance, and the crucial point of ordinary mind,  
[U00699](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00699) protecting beings and oneself, and the wind deity—  
[U00700](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00700) the remainder is just that much. [N-041](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-041)  

[U00701](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00701) [Source heading: Tenth reply.]  
[U00702](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00702) Regarding the explanation of ‘perfection’:  
[U00703](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00703) phrases of the teaching in which clinging is self-liberated,  
[U00704](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00704) a text with neither additions nor omissions,  
[U00705](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00705) and completeness without delusion in the meaning.  
[U00706](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00706) The explanation is free from conventions and phrases.  
[U00707](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00707) From the complete aspects of class-formation, [N-042](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-042)  
[U00708](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00708) the languages of the six classes of beings  
[U00709](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00709) and all engagements through signs appear.  
[U00710](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00710) Whatever is transformed by each one's capacity [Source variant note: ‘applied by capacity’ also occurs; N-042],  
[U00711](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00711) is complete in intrinsic nature; thus it is the Great Perfection.  
[U00712](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00712) The perfection distinguishing appearance and emptiness:  
[U00713](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00713) having clearly distinguished the text's phrases and meanings,  
[U00714](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00714) it is entirely complete, with nothing remaining.  
[U00715](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00715) The Ground itself is complete without change.  
[U00716](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00716) The path is straight and fully complete.  
[U00717](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00717) The result is complete in the condition in which nothing is to be done.  
[U00718](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00718) View and cultivation are complete in great freedom from doing.  
[U00719](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00719) Empowerment, pledges, and vows are complete.  
[U00720](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00720) In brief, everything is complete.  

[U00721](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00721) [Source heading: Eleventh reply.]  
[U00722](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00722) True phrases in the introductory setting:  
[U00723](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00723) for every individual being trained,  
[U00724](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00724) the Ground of training is the five aggregates.  
[U00725](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00725) The introductory setting is what trains that Ground.  
[U00726](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00726) ‘Introducing’ refers to expression in phrases.  
[U00727](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00727) ‘Ground’ has five aspects.  
[U00728](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00728) The place is held to have three aspects.  
[U00729](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00729) ‘Phun’ means gathered together; [N-043](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-043)  
[U00730](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00730) ‘Gsum’ is the number [three], that which brings appearance;  
[U00731](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00731) ‘Tshogs’ is the completeness of qualities.  
[U00732](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00732) The teacher too has three aspects:  
[U00733](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00733) ‘Phun’ means complete as embodiment;  
[U00734](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00734) ‘Gsum’ makes the three embodiments appear;  
[U00735](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00735) ‘Tshogs’ gathers the retinues.  
[U00736](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00736) The retinue too has three aspects:  
[U00737](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00737) ‘Phun’ means arrayed as forms;  
[U00738](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00738) ‘Gsum’ is the number [three], with diversity gathered;  
[U00739](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00739) ‘Tshogs’ means having faith and gathering.  
[U00740](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00740) The teaching too has three aspects:  
[U00741](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00741) ‘Phun’ means the quintessence of the innermost core;  
[U00742](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00742) ‘Gsum’ is the precious collection of scriptures;  
[U00743](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00743) ‘Tshogs’ liberates individuals.  
[U00744](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00744) Time too is three, in which the four are gathered:  
[U00745](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00745) ‘Phun’ means all vehicles are complete;  
[U00746](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00746) ‘Gsum’ is past, present, and future;  
[U00747](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00747) ‘Tshogs’ is definite completeness.  
[U00748](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00748) Being uncommon, it is exceptional:  
[U00749](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00749) the sign of surpassing all vehicles,  
[U00750](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00750) the quintessences gathered into the core,  
[U00751](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00751) revealed by the teacher's speech to the retinue,  
[U00752](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00752) the foremost teaching, gathering the supremely wondrous.  
[U00753](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00753) As for the causes and conditions of the common introductory setting,  
[U00754](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00754) because it accords with others merely in the form of transmission,  
[U00755](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00755) it is known as ‘common.’  

[U00756](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00756) [Source heading: Twelfth reply.]  
[U00757](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00757) Thus, regarding this very teaching,  
[U00758](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00758) the remaining teaching is as follows:  
[U00759](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00759) the definite divisions of time are explained—  
[U00760](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00760) Endowed with the Core, Endowed with Perfection,  
[U00761](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00761) Great Endowment with Perfection,  
[U00762](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00762) likewise Endowed with Three and Endowed with Two,  
[U00763](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00763) Endowed with Conflict and Great Endowment with Conflict. [N-044](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-044)  
[U00764](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00764) Between each of these,  
[U00765](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00765) in sixty ages of darkness,  
[U00766](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00766) the shoots of the teaching will shift elsewhere.  
[U00767](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00767) In the north, at Endowed with Movement,  
[U00768](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00768) the lamp of the teaching will shine clearly.  
[U00769](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00769) A monk called Glorious Protector  
[U00770](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00770) will uphold it.  
[U00771](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00771) His patron, a king  
[U00772](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00772) called Jinamitra,  
[U00773](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00773) will provide the basis for restoring the teaching.  
[U00774](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00774) It will remain for a thousand and three hundred years.  
[U00775](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00775) Then an age of darkness  
[U00776](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00776) will remain for sixty [years]. [N-044](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-044)  
[U00777](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00777) Then, in the south, at Protector of the Grove,  
[U00778](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00778) on an island spacious and beautiful,  
[U00779](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00779) the life-pillar of the teaching will descend.  
[U00780](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00780) One called Glory of Joy,  
[U00781](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00781) of the class of those with long locks,  
[U00782](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00782) will uphold this teaching.  
[U00783](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00783) His patron, of the householder class,  
[U00784](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00784) called Accomplishment of Virtue,  
[U00785](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00785) will make this very sacred teaching  
[U00786](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00786) remain for a hundred years.  
[U00787](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00787) Then the age of darkness  
[U00788](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00788) will last a hundred and twenty years.  
[U00789](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00789) Then, in the east, at Wind of Bliss,  
[U00790](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00790) on the peak of Vajra-Arising Mountain,  
[U00791](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00791) this sacred teaching will descend.  
[U00792](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00792) A king called Sūtrade  
[U00793](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00793) will uphold the teaching.  
[U00794](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00794) His patron, a brahmin  
[U00795](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00795) called Jaya Ākar,  
[U00796](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00796) will sustain it for a thousand and four hundred years. [N-045](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-045)  
[U00797](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00797) After that, the age of darkness  
[U00798](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00798) will last five thousand years.  
[U00799](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00799) Then, in the west, on the Island of Loving Care,  
[U00800](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00800) at a place called Bharabhati Sāli [Source variant note: sa yi also occurs], [N-045](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-045)  
[U00801](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00801) among the class of fortunate beings,  
[U00802](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00802) the three—innermost, quintessence, and core—will descend. [N-045](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-045)  
[U00803](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00803) The monk Protector of Bliss  
[U00804](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00804) will uphold this sacred teaching.  
[U00805](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00805) His patron, Holder of Heroes,  
[U00806](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00806) of the outcaste class, will for a thousand years  
[U00807](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00807) make this remain and increase.  
[U00808](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00808) After that, darkness itself  
[U00809](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00809) will remain for five thousand years.  
[U00810](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00810) Then it will descend here again,  
[U00811](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00811) at the place known as the Vajra Seat.  
[U00812](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00812) It will increase and blaze with light  
[U00813](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00813) for five hundred and fifty years.  
[U00814](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00814) Excellent Intelligence will uphold it.  
[U00815](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00815) His patron will be a ḍākinī  
[U00816](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00816) called Pure Cool-Maker.  
[U00817](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00817) After that, in this world,  
[U00818](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00818) earth, water, fire, and wind will clash together.  
[U00819](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00819) It will not remain, but become a great emptiness.  
[U00820](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00820) What is called an age, from its beginning,  
[U00821](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00821) will finally reach the time of destruction.  

[U00822](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00822) [Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]  
[U00823](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00823) The immediate cause of the teaching's destruction  
[U00824](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00824) in each of those places  
[U00825](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00825) will be an individual called one of perverse craving.  
[U00826](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00826) Each such individual will be  
[U00827](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00827) the immediate cause of destroying the entire teaching.  
[U00828](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00828) In the first time of darkness here,  
[U00829](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00829) in the west of Jambudvīpa,  
[U00830](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00830) a king will have six fingers on his hand,  
[U00831](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00831) with an extra blue tooth at the front.  
[U00832](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00832) On the elbow of his right arm  
[U00833](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00833) there will be a black mole in the shape of a scorpion,  
[U00834](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00834) extremely terrifying.  
[U00835](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00835) Through him, the buddha's teaching will become dark.  
[U00836](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00836) This will last sixty years.  
[U00837](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00837) After that, the first Glory-Holder will uphold it.  
[U00838](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00838) After a thousand and three hundred years,  
[U00839](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00839) again, in the north of Jambudvīpa,  
[U00840](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00840) on the summit of Majestic Mountain,  
[U00841](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00841) one in the form of a monk, lacking a loving mind,  
[U00842](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00842) will have two ears on his right side  
[U00843](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00843) and triangular eyes covered from below upward. [N-046](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-046)  
[U00844](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00844) He and a group of non-Buddhist proponents will dispute.  
[U00845](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00845) For sixty years there will be darkness.  
[U00846](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00846) After that, one called Deity of Bliss  
[U00847](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00847) will make it increase for a hundred years.  
[U00848](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00848) After that, in the northeast of Jambudvīpa,  
[U00849](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00849) beside the flowing Unfordable River,  
[U00850](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00850) one with a king's body, endowed with merit,  
[U00851](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00851) will have only one eye,  
[U00852](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00852) coarse limbs and bodily strength,  
[U00853](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00853) and be adorned with archery and magical skills.  
[U00854](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00854) He will suppress those who practice the teaching.  
[U00855](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00855) For sixty years, the teaching itself  
[U00856](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00856) will set here and move elsewhere.  
[U00857](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00857) After that, one in the form of a solitary buddha  
[U00858](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00858) will appear west of the Vajra Seat. [N-046](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-046)  
[U00859](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00859) He will make the sacred teaching itself  
[U00860](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00860) remain for a hundred and twenty years.  
[U00861](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00861) After that, in central Jambudvīpa,  
[U00862](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00862) at a place called the Golden City,  
[U00863](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00863) one with an elder's body will have a two-tipped tongue,  
[U00864](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00864) a small frame and short limbs.  
[U00865](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00865) He will quarrel with groups and take up weapons.  
[U00866](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00866) With his appearance, darkness will again  
[U00867](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00867) last sixty years.  
[U00868](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00868) After that, in eastern Jambudvīpa,  
[U00869](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00869) on the river called Lokita,
