[U00870](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00870) on its right bank,  
[U00871](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00871) one called Glorious Lion  
[U00872](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00872) will sustain it for a thousand and four hundred years.  
[U00873](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00873) Then again, in central Jambudvīpa,  
[U00874](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00874) on the bank of the great river Gaṅgā,  
[U00875](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00875) a king's son, on his arm,  
[U00876](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00876) will have something emerald-colored,  
[U00877](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00877) projecting prominently to a measure of four finger-widths. [N-046](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-046)  
[U00878](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00878) With his appearance, darkness will again  
[U00879](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00879) last sixty years.  
[U00880](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00880) After that, at the temple called  
[U00881](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00881) Kamalaśīla,  
[U00882](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00882) its master, Protector of Youth,  
[U00883](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00883) will sustain it for a thousand years.  
[U00884](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00884) After that, in southern Jambudvīpa,  
[U00885](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00885) in the town called Brahmā's Island,  
[U00886](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00886) one of the commoner class will have a lion's head.  
[U00887](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00887) Three months after his birth,  
[U00888](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00888) darkness will last for sixty [years]. [N-044](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-044)  
[U00889](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00889) After that, on Vulture Peak,  
[U00890](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00890) the monk Beautifying Youth  
[U00891](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00891) will sustain it for five hundred and fifty years.  
[U00892](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00892) After that, on the borders of Jambudvīpa,  
[U00893](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00893) in the land called Kashmir,  
[U00894](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00894) in the City Endowed with Sound,  
[U00895](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00895) one with a bird's body and a human face  
[U00896](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00896) will be born among those of low lineage.  
[U00897](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00897) Ten years after his birth,  
[U00898](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00898) darkness will last for sixty [years].  
[U00899](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00899) After that, in Jambudvīpa,  
[U00900](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00900) five yojanas east of its center,  
[U00901](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00901) through the monk Śākya Dzīka,  
[U00902](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00902) for ten years from then,  
[U00903](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00903) it will be like a blazing lamp. [N-045](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-045)  
[U00904](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00904) Thus, through seven successive stages,  
[U00905](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00905) it will increase and diminish in Jambudvīpa.  
[U00906](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00906) Then it will definitely rise  
[U00907](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00907) into the field of Beautiful Array.  
[U00908](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00908) When lifespans fall below ten years,  
[U00909](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00909) it will not remain here, but appear there.  

[U00910](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00910) [Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]  
[U00911](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00911) When its flourishing and decline are thus complete,  
[U00912](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00912) this core of the sacred teaching  
[U00913](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00913) will be in the world of Spacious Protection.  
[U00914](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00914) To make the buddha's teaching remain,  
[U00915](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00915) the teacher called Beautiful Utpala Flower,  
[U00916](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00916) the buddha, the Blessed One,  
[U00917](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00917) will be in the palace of Stacked Bliss-Making,  
[U00918](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00918) with a retinue of five thousand.  
[U00919](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00919) All will be great bodhisattvas,  
[U00920](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00920) having attained acceptance of non-arising phenomena,  
[U00921](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00921) and only those with virtuous minds will appear.  
[U00922](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00922) With the coming of that very teacher,  
[U00923](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00923) those of perverse craving will be definitively tamed.  
[U00924](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00924) This very sacred teaching,  
[U00925](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00925) for five thousand thousands of years,  
[U00926](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00926) will be made to remain as the core of the teaching. [N-047](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-047)  
[U00927](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00927) Then again, as before,  
[U00928](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00928) it will increase and diminish through seven successive stages.  
[U00929](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00929) After that, in the spacious world system  
[U00930](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00930) of Melodious Crest,  
[U00931](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00931) the teacher will be Infinite Destroyer of Samsara.  
[U00932](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00932) His retinue will be seven and a half hundreds,  
[U00933](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00933) all arhats whose contaminations are exhausted,  
[U00934](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00934) who have done what was to be done and laid down the burden.  
[U00935](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00935) He too will sustain the core of the teaching itself  
[U00936](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00936) for seven hundred thousand years.  
[U00937](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00937) Following the course of the earlier sequence,  
[U00938](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00938) after seven such periods of abiding,  
[U00939](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00939) for eighty-five ages,  
[U00940](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00940) the teachings will dissolve into the buddha's embodiment,  
[U00941](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00941) and in no world system whatever  
[U00942](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00942) will the buddha's teaching remain.  
[U00943](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00943) Then, in the lower world,  
[U00944](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00944) for ‘a million, six hundred thousand’ ages, [N-047](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-047)  
[U00945](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00945) the buddha's teaching will remain for a long time.  
[U00946](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00946) There will be no increase or decline in it.  
[U00947](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00947) It will bring beings to maturity.  

[U00948](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00948) [Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]  
[U00949](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00949) The teaching of a perfectly awakened buddha  
[U00950](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00950) is held to have two aspects:  
[U00951](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00951) the actual teaching and its reflected form.  
[U00952](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00952) Its reflected form has no fixed pattern:  
[U00953](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00953) this and that will appear in a mixed way. [N-048](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-048)  
[U00954](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00954) The sun makes [it] fully appear.  
[U00955](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00955) The three actual presences of embodiment, speech, and awakened mind—  
[U00956](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00956) whoever receives them will, without effort,  
[U00957](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00957) unfailingly attain buddhahood.  
[U00958](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00958) Therefore, the crucial point of blessing:  
[U00959](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00959) in the emanation of speech as well,  
[U00960](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00960) having carefully painted the teacher's colors,  
[U00961](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00961) fasten it beneath the left armpit.  
[U00962](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00962) If one reads and retains it at the proper times,  
[U00963](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00963) one will certainly, unfailingly awaken. [N-048](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-048)  
[U00964](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00964) Whoever constantly reads this continuum  
[U00965](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00965) will become as described above.  
[U00966](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00966) The seventeen definitive secret commands,  
[U00967](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00967) together with the nails of the teaching and the magical letters,  
[U00968](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00968) in the earliest field of the emanation embodiment,  
[U00969](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00969) were spoken by the teacher Samantabhadra  
[U00970](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00970) at the place of Dense Array.  
[U00971](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00971) Then, in the abode of Those with Long Locks,  
[U00972](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00972) the All-Seeing Vairocana  
[U00973](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00973) held this, bringing the five families to maturity.  
[U00974](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00974) After that, through my holding it,  
[U00975](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00975) as the sacred life-pillar of the teaching,  
[U00976](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00976) a thousand and two buddhas  
[U00977](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00977) will definitely arise in reliance on this.  
[U00978](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00978) Sentient beings born miraculously or from eggs,  
[U00979](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00979) from warmth or from wombs,  
[U00980](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00980) will be brought to maturity.  
[U00981](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00981) From this very teaching that trains beings,  
[U00982](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00982) twelve wheels of teaching will arise.  
[U00983](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00983) Its thirty-six deeds,  
[U00984](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00984) in dependence on embodiment, speech, and awakened mind,  
[U00985](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00985) will appear to the sentient beings who are to be trained.  
[U00986](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00986) After I have passed beyond sorrow,  
[U00987](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00987) in the western land of Uḍḍiyāna,  
[U00988](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00988) to the divine lady of Dhanakośa,  
[U00989](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00989) a fatherless son, Vajrahe, [N-049](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-049)  
[U00990](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00990) will uphold the sacred teaching.  
[U00991](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00991) Under his guidance, in the west,  
[U00992](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00992) a fortunate brahmin boy,  
[U00993](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00993) called Mañjuśrīpati,  
[U00994](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00994) will uphold it for seventy-five years.  
[U00995](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00995) After him, a fortunate one,  
[U00996](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00996) in the place called Sośa Island,  
[U00997](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00997) of the householder class, called Śrīsiddha,  
[U00998](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00998) will uphold it.  
[U00999](alignment/chapter-01.md#u00999) After him, one of the outcaste class will uphold it—  
[U01000](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01000) Jñānasūtra will uphold this very teaching. [N-049](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-049)  
[U01001](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01001) After him, one of the householder class—  
[U01002](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01002) Vast Seal—will uphold it. [N-050](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-050)  
[U01003](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01003) After him, one of the royal class,  
[U01004](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01004) an emanation together with an attendant, will uphold it. [N-050](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-050)  
[U01005](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01005) After him, in the form of an ascetic,  
[U01006](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01006) Spontaneous Victory Banner will do so.  
[U01007](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01007) After him, Sedheśvara will uphold it.  
[U01008](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01008) After him, those abiding on the grounds—  
[U01009](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01009) bodhisattvas—will uphold it.  
[U01010](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01010) After that, an emanation of Vajrapāṇi,  
[U01011](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01011) Vajraphala, will uphold it.  
[U01012](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01012) After that, the king Lion—  
[U01013](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01013) the Good One—will uphold it. [N-050](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-050)  
[U01014](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01014) After that, Glorious Word-Specialist will uphold it.  
[U01015](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01015) After that, Yogi Prabha will uphold it.  
[U01016](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01016) After that, King Throne-Light will do so.  
[U01017](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01017) After that, the monk Endowed with Glory;  
[U01018](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01018) then Supreme Intelligence will uphold it.  
[U01019](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01019) Then the ḍākinī Glory-Holder,  
[U01020](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01020) having upheld this very teaching,  
[U01021](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01021) will spread it when the lifespan is ten years.  
[U01022](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01022) Thereafter, its essence is non-abiding. [N-050](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-050)  

[U01023](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01023) [Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]  
[U01024](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01024) This secret heart-sphere of the teaching: [N-051](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-051) [N-T24](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t24)  
[U01025](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01025) after the seven teachings have passed in this way,  
[U01026](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01026) and after its definite course through two fields,  
[U01027](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01027) when the seven have thus passed, [N-051](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-051)  
[U01028](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01028) on the summit of the Vajra Seat,  
[U01029](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01029) the speech-token of all the teachings—[N-051](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-051)  
[U01030](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01030) the letters of the Great Naturally Arisen—  
[U01031](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01031) will descend together with sound. [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U01032](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01032) Ten million ages from now,  
[U01033](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01033) to the Blessed One Wheel-Garland of Lightning  
[U01034](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01034) it will appear. [N-051](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-051)  
[U01035](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01035) Then, after three hundred years—  
[U01036](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01036) after those three [hundreds] have thus passed—  
[U01037](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01037) the vajra of the teaching's awakened mind, too, [N-T04](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t04)  
[U01038](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01038) will appear to that teacher.  
[U01039](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01039) Then, after five hundred years,  
[U01040](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01040) the teaching's embodiment, too, will descend there.  
[U01041](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01041) When the actual teaching has descended in this way,  
[U01042](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01042) the embers of the teaching will blaze up again.  
[U01043](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01043) Also among that teacher's retinue,  
[U01044](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01044) one called Noble Brahmā-Light,  
[U01045](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01045) endowed with miraculous power, toward perfection  
[U01046](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01046) will have intense faith and aspiration;  
[U01047](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01047) he will definitely uphold [the teaching] and become manifest. [N-052](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-052)  
[U01048](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01048) Beginning with him, the teacher's retinue—  
[U01049](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01049) twenty thousand noble hearers—  
[U01050](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01050) through the discipline teaching for one age,  
[U01051](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01051) will be established in the result of an arhat.  
[U01052](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01052) Then there will be six ages of darkness.  
[U01053](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01053) Then two hundred thousand arhats  
[U01054](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01054) will gather in his retinue.  
[U01055](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01055) Through the sūtra teaching for eight ages,  
[U01056](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01056) he will establish [them] at the culmination of provisional and definitive activity. [N-052](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-052)  
[U01057](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01057) Having conferred empowerment endowed with miraculous power,  
[U01058](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01058) he will definitely impel [them onward], and the teaching will be complete.  
[U01059](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01059) Again he will show the manner of passing beyond sorrow.  
[U01060](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01060) Then there will be two ages of darkness.  
[U01061](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01061) Then, when the teacher is born,  
[U01062](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01062) a retinue of great bodhisattvas—  
[U01063](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01063) a hundred thousand—will gather.  
[U01064](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01064) Foremost among those gathered, Glorious Great Terror  
[U01065](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01065) will thoroughly collect the teacher's teaching. [N-052](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-052)  
[U01066](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01066) Through the higher teaching for eight ages,  
[U01067](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01067) all will attain the result in one lifetime. [Source variant: ‘A hundred also occurs.’] [N-053](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-053)  
[U01068](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01068) At that time samsara will be emptied from its depths:  
[U01069](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01069) sentient beings will attain buddhahood;  
[U01070](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01070) there can be no appearance called the six destinies.  
[U01071](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01071) For twenty thousand ages,  
[U01072](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01072) the flow of sentient beings' samsara having been cut off, [N-T17](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t17)  
[U01073](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01073) embodied beings will not appear at all.  
[U01074](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01074) Then, through particular karmic differences,  
[U01075](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01075) although sentient beings' afflictions are not manifest,  
[U01076](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01076) through the force of dormant tendencies rising up,  
[U01077](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01077) the forms taken on will be extremely subtle.  
[U01078](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01078) From these exceedingly subtle, numerous [forms],  
[U01079](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01079) the bodies of sentient beings endowed with form will develop. [N-052](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-052)  
[U01080](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01080) Thus, the inconceivable final extent of the teaching,  
[U01081](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01081) which cannot be encompassed by expression,  
[U01082](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01082) is for now indicated through words.  

[U01083](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01083) [Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]  
[U01084](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01084) As for the body and branches of the continuum:  
[U01085](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01085) the arrays in its body number two hundred  
[U01086](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01086) and exactly fifty. [N-054](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-054)  
[U01087](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01087) First, through clarity of ascertainment,  
[U01088](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01088) in the branches of the manifold array,  
[U01089](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01089) there are exactly seventy questions;  
[U01090](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01090) the arrays of the branches number one hundred and one. [Source variant: ‘Eleven also occurs.’] [N-054](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-054)  
[U01091](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01091) The body itself is thereby completed.  
[U01092](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01092) Its branches number two hundred and ten.  
[U01093](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01093) The foregoing body and branches are complete.  
[U01094](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01094) The body is complete; the branches disclose the secret. [N-054](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-054)  
[U01095](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01095) The second body is closely taught:  
[U01096](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01096) in the array gathering the crucial points,  
[U01097](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01097) from twenty-eight questions,  
[U01098](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01098) there are thirty-one arrays of ‘crucial-point times’ (gnad dus). [N-054](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-054)  
[U01099](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01099) Through these the body itself is complete.  
[U01100](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01100) The branches number two hundred and eight.  
[U01101](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01101) Through these, the second body  
[U01102](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01102) and its branches are complete.  
[U01103](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01103) The third body is closely taught:  
[U01104](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01104) for twenty-two questions,  
[U01105](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01105) there are fifty-eight arrays of primordial knowing;  
[U01106](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01106) the branches themselves number two hundred.  
[U01107](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01107) The branches of the third body are complete.  
[U01108](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01108) As for the fourth body and its branches:  
[U01109](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01109) there are twenty-one questions,  
[U01110](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01110) thirty-two arrays of the nature of phenomena,  
[U01111](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01111) and fifty-eight branches.  
[U01112](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01112) As for the fifth body and its branches:  
[U01113](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01113) there are eleven questions,  
[U01114](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01114) thirty-two arrays of viewing and cultivation,  
[U01115](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01115) and sixty-four branches.  
[U01116](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01116) The fifth body and its branches are complete.  
[U01117](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01117) As for the sixth body and its branches:  
[U01118](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01118) from the eight questions asked,  
[U01119](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01119) there are sixteen arrays of embodiments;  
[U01120](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01120) their branches number thirty-two.  
[U01121](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01121) The sixth body and its branches are complete.  

[U01122](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01122) [Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]  
[U01123](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01123) The account of the elements is as follows.  
[U01124](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01124) Through the particulars of shared karma,  
[U01125](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01125) sentient beings, with karma as their support,  
[U01126](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01126) depend on the outer elements.  
[U01127](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01127) Actions, clothing, and faculties, [N-055](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-055)  
[U01128](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01128) retinues and births, are held to become complete.  
[U01129](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01129) The sequence of activity of the inner elements is this:  
[U01130](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01130) earth makes the body's foundation; [N-055](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-055)  
[U01131](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01131) through its generation, maturation into flesh is held to occur.  
[U01132](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01132) Water gathers the whole body together;  
[U01133](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01133) through its generation, maturation into blood is held to occur.  
[U01134](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01134) Fire brings the whole body to maturity;  
[U01135](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01135) through its generation, maturation into warmth is held to occur.  
[U01136](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01136) Wind raises the whole body;  
[U01137](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01137) through its generation, it brings about maturation into breath.  
[U01138](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01138) Through the particulars of their word and meaning,  
[U01139](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01139) the general aspects are definitely taught. [N-055](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-055)  
[U01140](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01140) Through training with form, the body will be exhausted. [N-056](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-056)  
[U01141](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01141) Through sound, languages will be known. [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U01142](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01142) Through smell, taking nourishment from vital essences is accomplished. [N-T16](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t16)  
[U01143](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01143) Through taste, substances are transformed into nectar.  
[U01144](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01144) [Source note: Words have been lost.] [N-056](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-056)  
[U01145](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01145) The nectar completes the benefit of other sentient beings  
[U01146](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01146) and also one's own benefit. [N-056](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-056)  

[U01147](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01147) [Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]  
[U01148](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01148) The times of sentient beings have eight aspects:  
[U01149](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01149) illness, wind, and heat;  
[U01150](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01150) knowing, afflictions, awakening ordinary mind, [N-T25](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t25)  
[U01151](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01151) primordial knowing itself, and the elements. [N-057](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-057)  
[U01152](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01152) In the four times—summer, winter, autumn, and spring—  
[U01153](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01153) wind, bile, phlegm, and their conjunction, the four,  
[U01154](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01154) shift from their respective places.  
[U01155](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01155) This is the time of sentient beings' wind-mind shifting.  
[U01156](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01156) At this time wind is layered,  
[U01157](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01157) or slow and interrupted,  
[U01158](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01158) or subtle and hot. [N-057](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-057)  
[U01159](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01159) This shortens lifespans;  
[U01160](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01160) it steals knowing and brings stupefaction.  
[U01161](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01161) At intervals [knowing] becomes distracted and shifts.  
[U01162](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01162) Through the gathering of the five afflictions,  
[U01163](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01163) [beings] are projected into places of lower destiny.  
[U01164](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01164) Awakening ordinary mind dissipates, [N-T25](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t25)  
[U01165](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01165) flutters greatly, and becomes subtle. [N-057](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-057)  
[U01166](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01166) Primordial knowing is not clear.  
[U01167](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01167) The elements proliferate from their dispersal and penetration. [N-057](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-057)  
[U01168](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01168) This is the time of an individual sentient being.  
[U01169](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01169) As for the times common to sentient beings:  
[U01170](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01170) from the first, immeasurable [lifespan],  
[U01171](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01171) to the final lifespan of ten years,  
[U01172](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01172) sixty successive periods occur.  
[U01173](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01173) Because afflictions change under the power of karma,  
[U01174](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01174) through the karma of concordant, common purposes, [N-057](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-057)  
[U01175](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01175) from a lifespan of a thousand years,  
[U01176](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01176) also among the people of Jambudvīpa,  
[U01177](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01177) down to a hundred, the successive periods  
[U01178](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01178) number thirty.  
[U01179](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01179) Beginning from there, down to ten,  
[U01180](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01180) thirty successive periods occur.  
[U01181](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01181) Through eight stages in the times when habituation shifts,  
[U01182](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01182) activity subsequently becomes worse.  
[U01183](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01183) Periods of afflicting spirits and famine,  
[U01184](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01184) weapons and illness, arise.  
[U01185](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01185) Their particulars are many: these are the successive periods. [N-057](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-057)  

[U01186](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01186) [Source heading: Twentieth reply.]  
[U01187](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01187) Among the outer elements, wind  
[U01188](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01188) is joined with the element of space.  
[U01189](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01189) Upon it is water, and above that, earth.  
[U01190](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01190) The earth's extent and thickness are equal. [N-058](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-058)  
[U01191](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01191) ‘Four innumerables and one million’;  
[U01192](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01192) ‘one is a thousand of its quarter.’ [Numerical construction unresolved.] [N-058](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-058)  
[U01193](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01193) There are the arrays of continents and Mount Meru,  
[U01194](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01194) together with wish-granting trees and jewels,  
[U01195](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01195) the sun and moon, and the respective gods,  
[U01196](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01196) surrounded by the depths of the great ocean.  
[U01197](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01197) The Meru mountains and tributary streams  
[U01198](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01198) are definitely arrayed with seven precious substances.  
[U01199](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01199) Inwardly, first there is the formless [realm];  
[U01200](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01200) then the form realm, clear light;  
[U01201](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01201) gradually, also in the desire realm,  
[U01202](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01202) from the stainless moon  
[U01203](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01203) and the sun that makes things shine brightly,  
[U01204](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01204) the world system proliferates. [N-058](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-058)  
[U01205](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01205) Beginning with Mastery over Others' Emanations, in descending order,  
[U01206](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01206) then the abodes of the demigods,  
[U01207](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01207) then the humans of the four continents, successively.  
[U01208](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01208) Through afflictions together with selfhood,  
[U01209](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01209) the places of lower destiny gradually form.  
[U01210](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01210) The respective beings of the six classes  
[U01211](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01211) form within; their bodily size  
[U01212](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01212) and lifespan, through shared karma,  
[U01213](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01213) and their respective abodes and merit,  
[U01214](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01214) abide in accordance with their continent. [N-058](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-058)  
[U01215](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01215) The meaning is destruction and formation,  
[U01216](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01216) abiding itself and becoming empty;  
[U01217](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01217) birth, aging, illness, and death—  
[U01218](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01218) the mechanism of existence itself.  

[U01219](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01219) [Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]  
[U01220](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01220) The shifting of times is as follows.  
[U01221](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01221) From the initial immeasurable [lifespan]  
[U01222](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01222) down to the final ten [years],  
[U01223](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01223) there are increases upward and decreases downward.  
[U01224](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01224) Illness, famine, and weapons—  
[U01225](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01225) the three minor ages—join the intervals.  
[U01226](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01226) When sixty have thus passed,  
[U01227](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01227) seven [destructions] are by fire, and seven by water.  
[U01228](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01228) When these fourteen have been completed,  
[U01229](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01229) the remaining [destruction] is by wind. [N-059](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-059)  
[U01230](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01230) Thus there are sixty [periods] of abiding,  
[U01231](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01231) and the duration of destruction is as long;  
[U01232](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01232) for sixty [periods] it abides empty.  
[U01233](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01233) Thus, from one hundred and eighty [periods]—[Source gloss: ‘Formation, destruction, emptiness.’] [N-059](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-059)  
[U01234](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01234) the duration is reckoned as the first age.  
[U01235](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01235) Likewise, with one hundred and eighty,  
[U01236](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01236) there is the second, intermediate age.  
[U01237](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01237) Through that many such repetitions—[Source gloss: ‘Formation, destruction, emptiness.’] [N-059](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-059)  
[U01238](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01238) there is one great age.  

[U01239](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01239) [Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]  
[U01240](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01240) Three tens of days  
[U01241](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01241) constitute one month.  
[U01242](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01242) When twelve such [months] are complete,  
[U01243](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01243) they are reckoned as one year.  
[U01244](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01244) When seven hundred  
[U01245](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01245) and twenty mornings and evenings are complete,  
[U01246](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01246) they are reckoned as three hundred and sixty days.  
[U01247](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01247) Their completion constitutes one year.  
[U01248](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01248) Days are distinguished as male and female according to activity. [N-060](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-060)  
[U01249](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01249) The months of autumn, spring, summer, and winter—  
[U01250](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01250) three for each—  
[U01251](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01251) enter into operation and enact the five aspects of the elements.  
[U01252](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01252) Rat, ox, and tiger;  
[U01253](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01253) long-eared hare, snake, and dragon;  
[U01254](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01254) horse, sheep, and monkey;  
[U01255](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01255) bird, dog, and pig—through these twelve,  
[U01256](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01256) the elemental calculations are extended. [N-061](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-061)  

[U01257](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01257) [Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]  
[U01258](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01258) The rites for increasing and restoring life,  
[U01259](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01259) accomplishing it and making it flourish—  
[U01260](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01260) when a yogin knows how to apply them,  
[U01261](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01261) [the lifespan] becomes equal to the sun and moon. [N-062](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-062)  
[U01262](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01262) For others, from equalizing and dismantling,  
[U01263](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01263) there are summoning, binding, and bringing down a slayer,  
[U01264](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01264) expelling, and the activities of killing.  
[U01265](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01265) When a yogin knows how to summon, [Source variant: ‘Stir also.’] [N-062](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-062)  
[U01266](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01266) beings are gathered under his power.  
[U01267](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01267) Increasing and restoring ascendancy,  
[U01268](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01268) making it abide and become established,  
[U01269](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01269) and, further, the application of equalizing—  
[U01270](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01270) these a yogin should undertake. [N-062](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-062)  

[U01271](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01271) [Source heading: Twenty-fourth reply.]  
[U01272](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01272) Dispersal, penetration, and equalizing comprise twelve:  
[U01273](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01273) for earth, water, fire, and wind, the four,  
[U01274](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01274) three each make twelve. [N-063](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-063)  
[U01275](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01275) Earth's penetration produces particles;  
[U01276](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01276) its dispersal produces branches;  
[U01277](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01277) through its equalizing, the body's elements are complete.  
[U01278](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01278) Fire's penetration produces illness;  
[U01279](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01279) through its dispersal, the body will abide;  
[U01280](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01280) its equalizing makes the elements abide.  
[U01281](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01281) Wind's penetration protects the elements;  
[U01282](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01282) its dispersal destroys other activities;  
[U01283](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01283) its equalizing gathers wind-mind into one.  
[U01284](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01284) These are the limits of what the three do.  
[U01285](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01285) Through water's penetration, earth gathers together.  
[U01286](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01286) [Source note: The two—water's dispersal and equalizing—are missing.] [N-063](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-063)  
[U01287](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01287) When penetration meets penetration,  
[U01288](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01288) nothing is accomplished, and destruction follows. [N-063](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-063)  
[U01289](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01289) Through dispersal itself, illness occurs;  
[U01290](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01290) through equalizing, elixir preparations and medicines are accomplished.  
[U01291](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01291) Fire and wind together destroy the aggregates;  
[U01292](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01292) through dispersal, all activities are accomplished;  
[U01293](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01293) through equalizing, activities of conflict reach their culmination.  
[U01294](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01294) Through water and fire, purposes are thoroughly accomplished;  
[U01295](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01295) through dispersal, one enters another;  
[U01296](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01296) through equalizing, long life and ascendancy are accomplished.  
[U01297](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01297) Through earth and wind, pacification is accomplished;  
[U01298](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01298) through dispersal, increase and subjugation are enacted;  
[U01299](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01299) through equalizing, aggressive ritual activities are accomplished. [N-063](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-063)  
[U01300](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01300) As for the activity of wind and water:  
[U01301](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01301) through dispersal, all virtuous activities are accomplished;  
[U01302](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01302) through equalizing, there is victory in warfare.  
[U01303](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01303) Through earth and fire, increase is accomplished;  
[U01304](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01304) through dispersal, transferring the elements is accomplished;  
[U01305](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01305) through equalizing, the slayer's rite is accomplished.  
[U01306](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01306) At the culmination of the activities of penetration and dispersal,  
[U01307](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01307) [these results] will definitely occur, Deity of the Faculties. [N-063](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-063)  

[U01308](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01308) [Source heading: Twenty-fifth reply.]  
[U01309](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01309) To cut the karmic continuum of samsara:  
[U01310](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01310) complete in freedom from doing, it is complete.  
[U01311](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01311) Free from extremes, it does not abide in a center.  
[U01312](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01312) Entirely complete, it is free from entities.  
[U01313](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01313) Without remainder, it is not a portion.  
[U01314](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01314) Not being one, the two extremes are exhausted.  
[U01315](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01315) Not appearing as many, it is beyond one.  
[U01316](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01316) Inexpressible, [it is] the Ground of words. [N-064](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-064)  
[U01317](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01317) Free from entities and empty: the core meaning.  
[U01318](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01318) Not falling to any side: the meaning of the view.  
[U01319](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01319) Knowing this blocks the path of samsara.  
[U01320](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01320) Direct perception arising from the crucial point itself—  
[U01321](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01321) through the power of familiarity, delusion is destroyed.  
[U01322](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01322) With intrinsic nature pure, there is no distraction.  
[U01323](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01323) Abiding continuously, there is no separation.  
[U01324](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01324) Being self-appearance, differentiating conceptualization is exhausted.  
[U01325](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01325) Wind exhausted, one engages in primordial knowing itself.  
[U01326](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01326) Through never being separate from this,  
[U01327](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01327) one cuts the flow of the paths of the six destinies.  
[U01328](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01328) Without making or contriving,  
[U01329](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01329) through activity by its own momentum, delusion is purified. [N-064](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-064)  
[U01330](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01330) Arising in letting be, there is non-arising.  
[U01331](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01331) Without grasping or attachment, one plays as one pleases.  
[U01332](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01332) Settled into its own condition, turbidity clears.  
[U01333](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01333) Being intrinsic nature, it is beyond methods.  
[U01334](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01334) This, too, blocks the gate of samsara.  

[U01335](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01335) [Source heading: Twenty-sixth reply.]  
[U01336](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01336) The qualities of the teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,  
[U01337](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01337) cannot be encompassed by expression.  
[U01338](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01338) Through sixty excellences,  
[U01339](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01339) he upholds the foundation of the sacred teaching. [N-065](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-065)  
[U01340](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01340) For a long time in the world system,  
[U01341](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01341) the buddha's teaching abides.  
[U01342](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01342) Its qualities cannot be encompassed by expression.  
[U01343](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01343) From what do these arise?  
[U01344](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01344) From embodiment, speech, and awakened mind, [N-T04](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t04)  
[U01345](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01345) and three sets of twelve deeds:  
[U01346](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01346) the teacher having come into the world,  
[U01347](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01347) these are the qualities for those to be trained. [N-065](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-065)  
[U01348](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01348) Through the outer, inner, and secret four activities,  
[U01349](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01349) there are immeasurable qualities that purify.  
[U01350](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01350) These follow the sequence taught before.  
[U01351](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01351) The teaching's growth and completion, too,  
[U01352](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01352) are qualities of the Blessed One.  

[U01353](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01353) [Source heading: Twenty-seventh reply.]  
[U01354](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01354) In the sequence of training in the three embodiments,  
[U01355](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01355) making the desired purposes of the elements primary,  
[U01356](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01356) above all, by training in the sounds  
[U01357](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01357) of earth, water, fire, and wind, one definitely accomplishes [them]. [N-066](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-066) [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U01358](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01358) Water's sound, gushing,  
[U01359](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01359) holds the melody of the ḍākinīs.  
[U01360](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01360) Through constant familiarity with this,  
[U01361](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01361) the emanation embodiment, too, is definitely accomplished.  
[U01362](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01362) Earth's sound is cool and heavy,  
[U01363](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01363) endowed with the voice of Great Brahmā.  
[U01364](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01364) Through constant, stable engagement in this,  
[U01365](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01365) the complete enjoyment embodiment is definitely accomplished.  
[U01366](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01366) For fire's sound, train in lengtheners; [N-066](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-066)  
[U01367](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01367) it reveals the melodic speech of the Great All-Pervader.  
[U01368](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01368) Whoever engages in listening to this  
[U01369](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01369) will definitely attain the qualities of the dharma embodiment.  
[U01370](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01370) Wind's sound is pressing and fierce;  
[U01371](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01371) it expresses the application of the king of sky-soarers. [N-066](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-066)  
[U01372](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01372) Knowing how to repeat this constantly,  
[U01373](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01373) one should undertake the common training in the three embodiments.  
[U01374](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01374) Thus the word and meaning of the four elements  
[U01375](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01375) are definitely joined to the outer times:  
[U01376](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01376) winter, spring, summer, and autumn;  
[U01377](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01377) water, earth, fire, and wind.  
[U01378](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01378) Definitely relating their sequence to the times,  
[U01379](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01379) and bringing it into accord with the yogin's body,  
[U01380](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01380) there is no doubt that accomplishment occurs. [N-066](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-066)  

[U01381](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01381) [Source heading: Twenty-eighth reply.]  
[U01382](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01382) As for the culmination of common worldly activities:  
[U01383](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01383) one wishing to fly relies on a pill. [N-067](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-067)  
[U01384](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01384) One wishing to become invisible rests the ordinary mind;  
[U01385](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01385) into a net of empty spheres,  
[U01386](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01386) placing both body and ordinary mind, one accomplishes [invisibility].  
[U01387](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01387) One wishing to attain unimpeded penetration  
[U01388](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01388) holds wind and places it in a sphere.  
[U01389](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01389) At intervals, knowing, like mist,  
[U01390](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01390) is to be brought out from Brahmā's abode. [N-067](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-067)  
[U01391](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01391) One wishing to overcome weapons and poison  
[U01392](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01392) directs the ordinary mind toward the four elements.  
[U01393](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01393) When one combines [them] into powder and performs the activity,  
[U01394](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01394) these are definitely accomplished. [N-067](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-067)  
[U01395](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01395) To overcome dogs, bears, and wolves,  
[U01396](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01396) one voices the four elements.  
[U01397](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01397) When the four dependent connections are made,  
[U01398](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01398) one will overcome them.  
[U01399](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01399) One wishing to be freed from the eight fears  
[U01400](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01400) cultivates a wheel endowed with a design  
[U01401](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01401) at the crown; cultivating wind,  
[U01402](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01402) through this, too, one overcomes those [fears].  
[U01403](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01403) One wishing to accomplish eye medicine—  
[U01404](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01404) the preparation involving crow's wings—  
[U01405](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01405) combines [it] properly and constantly repeats  
[U01406](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01406) the letters of the clarifiers. [N-067](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-067)  
[U01407](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01407) Then even the three realms appear clearly.  
[U01408](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01408) In warfare, trade, and arranging a marriage,  
[U01409](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01409) whoever wishes to prevail  
[U01410](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01410) takes account of the time and direction of the elements.  
[U01411](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01411) When these are made free from confusion,  
[U01412](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01412) there is no doubt of definite accomplishment. [N-067](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-067)  
[U01413](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01413) Whoever wishes to become a sword-holder,  
[U01414](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01414) with a preparation of yellow sa-pa-la, [Source variant: ‘se yi also occurs.’] [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01415](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01415) intensely repeats the elemental letters.  
[U01416](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01416) When [these] are properly applied at the body's five places,  
[U01417](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01417) and the time of the elements is thoroughly absorbed, [Source variant: ‘Combined also occurs.’] [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01418](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01418) through teaching the thorough application,  
[U01419](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01419) one becomes a great sword-holder.  
[U01420](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01420) A person gathering ḍākinīs under his power,  
[U01421](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01421) having gone to a place where mamos assemble,  
[U01422](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01422) a preparation of precious substances—[Source variant: ‘rin chen bse'i also occurs.’] [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01423](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01423) at the time when the heads of the elements meet, [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01424](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01424) properly combines [them] into the pill itself. [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01425](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01425) Whoever holds it inside the mouth  
[U01426](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01426) will bring the ḍākinīs under his power.  
[U01427](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01427) One wishing to accomplish swift feet  
[U01428](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01428) adorns sixteen vajra wings  
[U01429](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01429) with A and YAṂ.  
[U01430](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01430) At the time when the bird preparation arises,  
[U01431](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01431) without error concerning the time,  
[U01432](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01432) one smears paste on the soles of the feet  
[U01433](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01433) in accordance with the number. [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01434](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01434) In an instant, there is no doubt  
[U01435](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01435) that one will travel throughout Jambudvīpa.  
[U01436](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01436) Also, to travel through charnel-ground places,  
[U01437](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01437) one properly combines the eye-preparation pill  
[U01438](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01438) at its own times of application,  
[U01439](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01439) and surrounds it with A. [Source variant: ‘With āli also occurs.’] [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01440](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01440) Taking the vitality of the elements of one's own year,  
[U01441](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01441) by going to the place, one definitely accomplishes [this]. [N-068](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-068)  
[U01442](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01442) One wishing to take nourishment from vital essences: [N-T16](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t16)  
[U01443](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01443) the method of combining the five nectars,  
[U01444](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01444) and, further, balancing the elements—  
[U01445](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01445) a skilled person properly combines them in equal parts  
[U01446](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01446) and pours [them] into a precious vessel.  
[U01447](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01447) Then honey and ud-tsid-ta  
[U01448](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01448) are properly added as supplements. [N-069](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-069)  
[U01449](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01449) Taking a drop of mercury at a time, [Editorial safety note: Do not carry out this prescription.] [N-069](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-069)  
[U01450](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01450) likewise, [a portion] the size of a bean—  
[U01451](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01451) if those skilled consume [it],  
[U01452](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01452) there is no doubt that it becomes  
[U01453](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01453) a great [practice of] taking nourishment from vital essences. [N-069](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-069)  

[U01454](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01454) [Source heading: Twenty-ninth reply.]  
[U01455](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01455) When accomplishing the particular embodiments,  
[U01456](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01456) one trains in the smells of the four elements.  
[U01457](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01457) Water's smell gives bliss.  
[U01458](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01458) Adding [that] whose consuming power greatly increases in this,  
[U01459](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01459) and joining [it] to wind, [N-070](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-070)  
[U01460](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01460) one accomplishes the embodiment of the vajra-holder.  
[U01461](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01461) Earth's smell gathers realization.  
[U01462](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01462) Through the manner of preparing palāśa  
[U01463](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01463) and continually teaching dependent connections,  
[U01464](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01464) the embodiment of essence itself, too, is accomplished. [N-070](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-070)  
[U01465](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01465) Fire's smell generates experience.  
[U01466](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01466) Through familiarity with the experience  
[U01467](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01467) of one's own body being clear, Heruka, too, is accomplished.  
[U01468](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01468) Wind's smell is an object of training.  
[U01469](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01469) Combining [it] through the activity of abiding and entering,  
[U01470](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01470) if one clearly cultivates the sequence of the definite body,  
[U01471](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01471) the embodiments of the five families are well accomplished. [N-070](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-070)  
[U01472](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01472) Furthermore, through training in taste and touch themselves,  
[U01473](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01473) according to their respective sequences,  
[U01474](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01474) there is no doubt that whichever embodiments  
[U01475](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01475) one desires are accomplished.  

[U01476](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01476) [Source heading: Thirtieth reply.]  
[U01477](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01477) Whoever cultivates the yoga of sleep  
[U01478](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01478) thereby makes dull confusion into the path. [N-071](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-071)  
[U01479](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01479) For the outer times, from the Ground, [N-071](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-071)  
[U01480](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01480) one trains the expressiveness of the yogic body.  
[U01481](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01481) The crucial point is to hold the channels at their sources.  
[U01482](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01482) Through the four inner times themselves,  
[U01483](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01483) one trains the expressiveness of yoga.  
[U01484](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01484) The crucial point is to cut the flows of expression.  
[U01485](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01485) Through the four secret times, too,  
[U01486](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01486) one trains the expressiveness of the yogic ordinary mind.  
[U01487](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01487) The crucial point is familiarity with sleep.  
[U01488](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01488) Through the four times of suchness, [N-071](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-071)  
[U01489](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01489) definitely gathering body, speech, and ordinary mind,  
[U01490](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01490) one mixes great clear light with sleep.  
[U01491](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01491) Having become familiar in this way, the yogin  
[U01492](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01492) definitely joins [this] to the three embodiments.  
[U01493](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01493) Taking the crucial points of dreams onto the path  
[U01494](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01494) consists of preparation and applying the crucial points.  
[U01495](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01495) First training body, speech, and ordinary mind,  
[U01496](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01496) and relying on the signs of proficiency,  
[U01497](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01497) examination and overpowering, and  
[U01498](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01498) habitual tendencies—the three—are definitely to be retained. [N-072](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-072)  
[U01499](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01499) Then, at the time of applying the crucial points:  
[U01500](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01500) training, transformation, cutting off, and  
[U01501](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01501) stirring, concentrating the vitality, and releasing at the crucial point;  
[U01502](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01502) by applying the crucial points of gathering and reversing, [N-072](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-072)  
[U01503](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01503) karmic dreams are exhausted to their limit,  
[U01504](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01504) and the delusion of habitual tendencies is uprooted.  
[U01505](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01505) At this time, through one's own diligence,  
[U01506](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01506) for the superior they cease; for the middling they are known;  
[U01507](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01507) for the inferior they definitely change.  
[U01508](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01508) As for the beginning of these dreams, too:  
[U01509](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01509) for the superior they are forgotten, and for the inferior they cease;  
[U01510](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01510) for the middling they are exceedingly clear,  
[U01511](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01511) and the inferior knows that [they are dreams]. [N-072](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-072)  
[U01512](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01512) For the inferior they are unclear, then change.  
[U01513](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01513) Through these, the measure [of proficiency] is reached.  

[U01514](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01514) [Source heading: Thirty-first reply.]  
[U01515](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01515) A person's own ordinary mind, when untamed,  
[U01516](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01516) is definitely tamed by cultivating non-conceptuality.  
[U01517](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01517) Having settled into its own condition, it is one's own. [N-073](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-073)  
[U01518](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01518) The sequence of prior training  
[U01519](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01519) and the main practice depend on one's own continuum.  
[U01520](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01520) The sequence of prior training is this:  
[U01521](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01521) in the preliminary practice of the body,  
[U01522](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01522) the causes of the respective activities are stopped.  
[U01523](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01523) Assuming the vajra cross-legged posture,  
[U01524](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01524) one trains the body, benefiting the ordinary mind.  
[U01525](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01525) For speech, abiding in the syllable HŪṂ,  
[U01526](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01526) one applies the seal and trains expressiveness;  
[U01527](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01527) by entering the path of seeking the formidable, [N-073](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-073)  
[U01528](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01528) one thus trains speech, benefiting the ordinary mind.  
[U01529](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01529) For the ordinary mind: where it initially arises,  
[U01530](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01530) where it abides in between, and where it finally goes—  
[U01531](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01531) by examining and investigating these three,  
[U01532](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01532) one trains the ordinary mind and knows its natural state.  
[U01533](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01533) The main practice, relying on one's own continuum,  
[U01534](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01534) depends on the crucial points of body and speech  
[U01535](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01535) and the crucial point of ordinary mind.  
[U01536](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01536) Wind is to be made slow by every means.  
[U01537](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01537) Arising from thorough projection, [N-073](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-073)  
[U01538](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01538) the connection between body and ordinary mind is cut;  
[U01539](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01539) there is no turning back into the three realms.  

[U01540](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01540) [Source heading: Thirty-second reply.]  
[U01541](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01541) Wind has two kinds of purpose.  
[U01542](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01542) In the world, it generates the complete body,  
[U01543](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01543) and [supports] the lifespan through which it is sustained and abides;  
[U01544](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01544) it increases power, lifespan, and so forth.  
[U01545](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01545) It performs the activities of merit and karma;  
[U01546](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01546) it generates beings' luster and splendor;  
[U01547](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01547) it performs every activity of spreading and gathering.  
[U01548](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01548) For the transcendent: embodiments, primordial knowing,  
[U01549](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01549) and familiarity with spheres themselves;  
[U01550](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01550) pervasion that is light, empty, and clear; [N-074](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-074)  
[U01551](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01551) the exhaustion of the body's elements;  
[U01552](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01552) the stopping of the ordinary mind's conceptual thought;  
[U01553](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01553) the arising of non-conceptual primordial knowing;  
[U01554](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01554) the exhaustion of outer appearances;  
[U01555](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01555) and the mixing of wind and ordinary mind, the two. [N-074](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-074)  
[U01556](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01556) Thus wind performs all activities.  

[U01557](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01557) [Source heading: Thirty-third reply.]  
[U01558](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01558) The crucial point of the body is the channels:  
[U01559](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01559) their places, arrangement, and movement;  
[U01560](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01560) their support of the wheels and the body's life-force;  
[U01561](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01561) names, particulars, causes, and conditions;  
[U01562](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01562) the activities they perform and their characteristics;  
[U01563](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01563) afflictive activities and activities of primordial knowing;  
[U01564](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01564) manifest appearance through reliance on the path;  
[U01565](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01565) illnesses and the particulars of the elements—  
[U01566](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01566) they are distinguished by the extent of their abiding in confusion. [N-075](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-075)  
[U01567](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01567) Constricting stops; wringing binds. [Source variant: ‘Packing also occurs.’] [N-075](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-075)  
[U01568](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01568) Pressing makes [them] abide; squeezing makes [them] clear.  
[U01569](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01569) Twisting makes [them] cross; drawing makes [them] known.  
[U01570](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01570) Pushing makes [them] stay; forcefulness makes [them] held. [N-075](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-075)  
[U01571](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01571) Thus, through the crucial points of the channels, too,  
[U01572](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01572) the abode of buddhahood is indicated.  
[U01573](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01573) The winds that depend on the channels  
[U01574](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01574) move twenty thousand, one thousand,  
[U01575](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01575) and six hundred [times].  
[U01576](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01576) These are reckoned as the winds of great movement.  
[U01577](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01577) Their activity has two aspects:  
[U01578](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01578) moving upward and clearing downward.  
[U01579](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01579) Or, when a skilled person seeks the crucial point,  
[U01580](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01580) those definitely moving from the channels—  
[U01581](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01581) one hundred thousand, twenty thousand,  
[U01582](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01582) six thousand, and six hundred—  
[U01583](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01583) definitely arise from the channels.  
[U01584](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01584) They are reckoned as the winds of small movement. [N-075](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-075)  
[U01585](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01585) Times of illness, afflicting spirits, and lifespan  
[U01586](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01586) are to be known by the skilled person.  
[U01587](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01587) Through their crucial point, one strives in methods of application.  
[U01588](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01588) For the great [movements], one calculates the time  
[U01589](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01589) and examines supreme and common accomplishments.  
[U01590](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01590) Through the particulars of their respective activities,  
[U01591](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01591) between karma and primordial knowing, the two,  
[U01592](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01592) and among afflictions and freedom from conceptualization,  
[U01593](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01593) one takes the respective numerical measures. [N-075](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-075)  
[U01594](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01594) The cultivator trains thoroughly;  
[U01595](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01595) through this the measures of the respective winds are grasped.  

[U01596](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01596) [Source heading: Thirty-fourth reply.]  
[U01597](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01597) One wishing to stabilize the nature of phenomena of the sphere: [N-076](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-076)  
[U01598](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01598) there are the ultimate and the relative.  
[U01599](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01599) For now, relying on the relative sphere,  
[U01600](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01600) one wishing for buddhahood—  
[U01601](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01601) a woman of the family, with complete characteristics,  
[U01602](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01602) from gods, demigods, or Brahmā's [class],  
[U01603](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01603) or even a low-caste non-Buddhist—[N-076](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-076)  
[U01604](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01604) if one sees [her] complete characteristics,  
[U01605](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01605) one definitely undertakes the application of summoning.  
[U01606](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01606) Through what is to be relied upon, the channels,  
[U01607](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01607) and the objects of focus, one completes the body. [N-076](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-076)  
[U01608](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01608) Then the relative sphere itself  
[U01609](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01609) is lowered, retained, and reversed;  
[U01610](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01610) it is placed in the channels and mixed with wind.  
[U01611](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01611) Emptiness is to be stabilized; the traces of conceptual mind are cut.  
[U01612](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01612) Body and ordinary mind, the two, are to exchange their positions. [N-076](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-076)  
[U01613](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01613) Through stabilizing the ultimate sphere,  
[U01614](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01614) one finds the empty domains of the dharma embodiment.  
[U01615](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01615) Packing the lamp of the empty sphere itself,  
[U01616](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01616) one trains the expressiveness of awareness. [N-076](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-076)  
[U01617](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01617) Engaging in familiarity without [distinguishing] day and night,  
[U01618](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01618) this itself appears directly, free from effort.  
[U01619](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01619) This is the measure of proficiency.  

[U01620](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01620) [Source heading: Thirty-fifth reply.]  
[U01621](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01621) The extent of the activities of ḍākinīs,  
[U01622](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01622) if differentiated, cannot be encompassed by expression;  
[U01623](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01623) nevertheless, it will be explained in brief.  
[U01624](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01624) Through profound dependent connections,  
[U01625](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01625) one is free of food, clothing, and so forth,  
[U01626](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01626) attains uncontaminated great bliss,  
[U01627](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01627) and gains mastery over the four elements. [N-077](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-077)  
[U01628](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01628) Through the culmination of activities examined in actuality,  
[U01629](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01629) and through examining the domains of form, sound, smell, taste,  
[U01630](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01630) and touch, [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U01631](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01631) the culmination of common worldly activities  
[U01632](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01632) and higher knowing are perfected,  
[U01633](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01633) and one enjoys the fields.  

[U01634](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01634) [Source heading: Thirty-sixth reply.]  
[U01635](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01635) There are three particular kinds of transference, [N-T32](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t32)  
[U01636](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01636) according to distinctions in faculties:  
[U01637](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01637) clear light and illusory body;  
[U01638](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01638) for the last, body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-077](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-077)  
[U01639](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01639) Transference of body and speech has two aspects:  
[U01640](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01640) actual transference and nonactual transference.  
[U01641](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01641) The actual is a method of training through wind.  
[U01642](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01642) Through training in word, craft, illusory expressiveness,  
[U01643](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01643) and dependent connections,  
[U01644](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01644) when the respective elements accord inwardly,  
[U01645](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01645) it is accomplished by directing body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-077](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-077)  
[U01646](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01646) For the nonactual, stabilize the power of the ordinary mind's familiarity.  
[U01647](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01647) Train the illusory body in dreams.  
[U01648](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01648) Through proficiency in this, the body itself  
[U01649](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01649) becomes like the form of a shadow.  
[U01650](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01650) Through this, the body of the intermediate state  
[U01651](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01651) comes to be held within this condition. [N-077](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-077)  
[U01652](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01652) Clear-light transference is for the superior:  
[U01653](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01653) having trained in the meditative stabilization of sleep,  
[U01654](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01654) through proficiency, without [distinguishing] day and night,  
[U01655](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01655) when outer appearances are clear,  
[U01656](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01656) through this great clear light is accomplished.  
[U01657](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01657) Its body, too, is threefold. [Source variant: ‘Manner also.’] [N-077](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-077)  
[U01658](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01658) Through the stages of diligence,  
[U01659](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01659) into the emanation embodiment, complete enjoyment embodiment, and dharma embodiment,  
[U01660](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01660) one can definitely transfer in actuality.  

[U01661](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01661) [Source heading: Thirty-seventh reply.]  
[U01662](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01662) The application of dependent connections has two aspects:  
[U01663](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01663) accomplishing manifold miracles  
[U01664](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01664) and the rites of the crucial points of the elements.  
[U01665](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01665) Manifold miracles have manifold methods.  
[U01666](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01666) Grasping the various paths, one applies one's own knowing. [N-078](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-078)  
[U01667](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01667) The moving and unmoving are encircled with wind.  
[U01668](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01668) [Source variant: ‘Filled also occurs.’] [N-078](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-078)  
[U01669](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01669) They are differentiated through accomplishing body and faculties.  
[U01670](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01670) The crucial points of the elements definitely produce  
[U01671](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01671) beneficial and harmful results for oneself and others.  
[U01672](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01672) Join [them] with each person's body and elements:  
[U01673](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01673) water, earth, fire, and wind.  
[U01674](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01674) Through the dependent connections of their respective activities,  
[U01675](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01675) one restores, increases, or diminishes [them].  
[U01676](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01676) Earth and water destroy;  
[U01677](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01677) earth and fire increase;  
[U01678](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01678) earth and wind bring down a slayer;  
[U01679](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01679) water and fire cause decline;  
[U01680](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01680) water and wind restore;  
[U01681](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01681) fire and wind equalize;  
[U01682](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01682) the four gathered together produce confusion. [N-078](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-078)  
[U01683](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01683) Whatever dependent connections are expressed,  
[U01684](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01684) relate them to each yogin's experience.  
[U01685](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01685) For the mental faculty's dependent connections, inquire of wind. [N-078](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-078)  
[U01686](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01686) As for the activity of the three gathered together,  
[U01687](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01687) when properly combined with each corresponding arrangement,  
[U01688](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01688) through this, too, benefit and harm are accomplished.  

[U01689](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01689) [Source heading: Thirty-eighth reply.]  
[U01690](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01690) One wishing to be liberated from illness and suffering  
[U01691](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01691) applies [the methods] distinctly to oneself and others.  
[U01692](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01692) For others, act through dependent connections;  
[U01693](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01693) for oneself, perform transferring and stroking. [N-079](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-079)  
[U01694](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01694) There are three actions:  
[U01695](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01695) through the crucial point of body, speech,  
[U01696](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01696) and likewise the abiding of ordinary mind, one is liberated.  
[U01697](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01697) For the body, in the various bodily mechanisms:  
[U01698](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01698) the lion and elephant,  
[U01699](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01699) vulture and sheep shake [the body];  
[U01700](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01700) the vixen and the person,  
[U01701](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01701) worms and so forth—with bodily [forms]  
[U01702](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01702) and manifold bodily mechanisms,  
[U01703](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01703) outer and inner, upper and lower, limbs and body,  
[U01704](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01704) wind, bile, phlegm, and their conjunction,  
[U01705](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01705) are combined with the four groups of elemental illnesses. [N-079](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-079)  
[U01706](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01706) Distinguishing their extremes as hot and cold,  
[U01707](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01707) one joins each with its counterpart and is liberated from illness.  
[U01708](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01708) For speech, there are manifold sounds and voices:  
[U01709](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01709) with āli and kāli exceedingly clear,  
[U01710](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01710) for the letters abiding by intrinsic nature in the channels,  
[U01711](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01711) through the six ways of reversing [them], [Source variant: ‘Pronouncing also.’] [N-079](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-079)  
[U01712](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01712) one joins [them] to their respective places.  
[U01713](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01713) The voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,  
[U01714](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01714) rākṣasas and sky-soarers;  
[U01715](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01715) Brahmā, the Hundred-Giver, and the All-Pervader;  
[U01716](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01716) spirits and wind gods; [Source variant: ‘Pure voice also occurs.’] [N-079](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-079)  
[U01717](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01717) kumbhāṇḍas, belly-crawlers, and gandharvas,  
[U01718](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01718) and the voices of further entities—[N-079](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-079)  
[U01719](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01719) by joining the respective voices, one is liberated.  
[U01720](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01720) For ordinary mind, manifold forms of mindfulness,  
[U01721](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01721) and the extent of whatever movement occurs,  
[U01722](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01722) are distinguished through the yogin's awareness.  
[U01723](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01723) Furthermore, transform through body, speech, and mental faculty.  
[U01724](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01724) Or, through the activity of ordinary mind's doing,  
[U01725](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01725) transform through manifold deep absorptions,  
[U01726](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01726) and distinguish the extent of suffering as well. [N-079](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-079)  

[U01727](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01727) [Source heading: Thirty-ninth reply.]  
[U01728](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01728) The shifting of the elements is like this:  
[U01729](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01729) Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.  
[U01730](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01730) Through the distinction of outer and inner,  
[U01731](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01731) earth [joins] earth; the remaining [elements], too,  
[U01732](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01732) are to be understood by extension from the preceding [account].  
[U01733](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01733) Day and night, morning and evening, days,  
[U01734](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01734) months and years, through the manner of error—[Source variant: ‘Manner of confusion also.’] [N-080](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-080)  
[U01735](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01735) sets of twelve, and the coarse [divisions], too;  
[U01736](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01736) penetration, dispersal, and equalizing,  
[U01737](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01737) and abiding indeterminately in confusion—the four—[N-080](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-080)  
[U01738](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01738) are definitely distinguished by time: sixteen.  
[U01739](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01739) Their respective definite times, the intervals of shifting,  
[U01740](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01740) and the places of the elements in dependent connections and so forth:
