[U01741](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01741) at the time of shifting, there is shifting;  
[U01742](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01742) likewise, at the time of entering, entering;  
[U01743](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01743) at the time of abiding, abiding;  
[U01744](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01744) and in confusion itself, confusion.  
[U01745](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01745) These concern day and night, days and months.  
[U01746](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01746) The places of shifting are examined by the skilled person.  
[U01747](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01747) Through water, earth, fire, and wind,  
[U01748](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01748) concordance and discordance,  
[U01749](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01749) and the respective combinations of activities and times,  
[U01750](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01750) the culminations of activities are to be transformed. [N-080](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-080)  

[U01751](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01751) [Source heading: Fortieth reply.]  
[U01752](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01752) Distinguishing the elements of the crucial points:  
[U01753](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01753) from four hundred and four,  
[U01754](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01754) the specified entries are condensed into eighty;  
[U01755](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01755) from these, twenty-four;  
[U01756](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01756) the supreme abidings themselves are sixteen;  
[U01757](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01757) through male–female pairing, they become eight.  
[U01758](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01758) Through four pure in their own identity,  
[U01759](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01759) the crucial points are gathered and definitely taught. [N-081](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-081)  
[U01760](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01760) When the activities of dispersal, penetration, and equalizing  
[U01761](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01761) are joined to dates, months, and years  
[U01762](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01762) without error,  
[U01763](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01763) the crucial points, too, become definite.  

[U01764](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01764) [Source heading: Forty-first reply.]  
[U01765](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01765) Thus, relying on the crucial points,  
[U01766](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01766) the branches of the application of calculation are explained.  
[U01767](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01767) Ema! How greatly wondrous!  
[U01768](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01768) Not spoken by other buddhas,  
[U01769](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01769) the sequence of elemental calculation—  
[U01770](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01770) relying on which one measures samsara and nirvana—  
[U01771](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01771) listen to this utmost secret! [N-082](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-082)  
[U01772](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01772) From water, earth, fire, and wind,  
[U01773](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01773) there is a sequence of differentiation.  
[U01774](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01774) The fifth one is neuter and empty.  
[U01775](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01775) This is the sequence of knowing and calculating.  
[U01776](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01776) [Source variant: ‘Is not, also.’] [N-082](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-082)  
[U01777](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01777) From the twelve activities  
[U01778](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01778) of penetration, dispersal, and equalizing, the mother-years, too,  
[U01779](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01779) number twelve; in groups of three,  
[U01780](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01780) the respective objects of focus and dependent connections, too,  
[U01781](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01781) are examined by the skilled person in their application to times.  
[U01782](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01782) Za-ma, days, months, and years—[N-082](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-082)  
[U01783](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01783) through the times of dispersal, penetration, and equalizing,  
[U01784](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01784) one grasps the times of birth, aging, illness,  
[U01785](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01785) and likewise death.  
[U01786](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01786) Planets, stars, and afflicting spirits,  
[U01787](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01787) and the sequence of the sun's times:  
[U01788](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01788) calculate their particular favorable and unfavorable aspects.  
[U01789](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01789) The skilled person grasps what should and should not be done.  
[U01790](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01790) Through form, sound, smell, and taste, [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U01791](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01791) examine the karmic connections of earlier and later lives.  
[U01792](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01792) Through what moves the respective elements,  
[U01793](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01793) grasp the growth of virtuous application and the times of afflicting spirits.  
[U01794](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01794) Through sixteen inwardly concordant distinctions,  
[U01795](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01795) examine karma, afflictions, and primordial knowing.  
[U01796](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01796) The elements generate, abide, and destroy;  
[U01797](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01797) empty, wander, and spread without limit;  
[U01798](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01798) coil, revolve, and turn back;  
[U01799](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01799) become sharp, sluggish, fierce, and weak;  
[U01800](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01800) and [act as] enemy, slayer, and friend. [N-082](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-082)  
[U01801](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01801) Through these, calculate the categories of each respective activity.  
[U01802](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01802) Through the forms of the outer elements,  
[U01803](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01803) taking four million, bring each into accord. [Source variant: ‘gzhi also occurs.’] [N-083](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-083)  
[U01804](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01804) Through the respective combinations of their elements,  
[U01805](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01805) grasp the times of lifespan and ascendancy.  
[U01806](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01806) [Source variant: ‘Folding and bringing into contact also occur.’] [N-083](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-083)  
[U01807](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01807) Birth, illness, and death  
[U01808](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01808) are definitely related to oneself and others.  
[U01809](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01809) For the months of winter, summer, spring, and autumn,  
[U01810](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01810) counting three [for each] makes twelve.  
[U01811](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01811) The days of a thousand years, too,  
[U01812](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01812) number three hundred thousand. [Source variant spanning U01811–U01812: ‘A year; the days, too, [are] three tens.’] [N-083](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-083)  
[U01813](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01813) Through the stars' not being two,  
[U01814](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01814) years and months are piled one upon another. [N-083](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-083)  
[U01815](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01815) Through what transforms day and night,  
[U01816](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01816) there are two times when day and night are equal.  
[U01817](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01817) Among the categories known as dates,  
[U01818](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01818) in the ascending half, nights are long.  
[U01819](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01819) Then, by one hundred and eighty,  
[U01820](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01820) in the descending half, days are long. [N-083](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-083)  
[U01821](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01821) For making day and night long or short,  
[U01822](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01822) through distinguishing the means of male and female elements,  
[U01823](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01823) from the twelvefold sequence of day and night,  
[U01824](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01824) the skilled person grasps the times of reversal.  
[U01825](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01825) Through the length and shortness of day and night,  
[U01826](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01826) virtue and nonvirtue are distinguished.  
[U01827](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01827) For days, too, by extension from day and night,  
[U01828](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01828) months and years: dispersal, penetration, and equalizing.  
[U01829](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01829) [Source variant: ‘Three—the times are joined as twelve.’] [N-083](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-083)  
[U01830](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01830) Join the twelve times of dependent connections.  
[U01831](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01831) Knowing all twelve in sequence,  
[U01832](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01832) one completes Mañjuśrī's knowing.  
[U01833](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01833) Through the magical displays of the outer elements  
[U01834](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01834) and the categories of long and short days and nights,  
[U01835](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01835) join the elemental years to the measures of time.  
[U01836](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01836) For years, apply the respective extensions;  
[U01837](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01837) at the outset, join their own times equally.  
[U01838](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01838) Wind, light, and great sound;  
[U01839](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01839) earthquakes, evenness, and reflections;  
[U01840](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01840) and the manner in which [they] vanish into space—  
[U01841](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01841) definitely relating these to their respective directions,  
[U01842](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01842) one knows frost, heavy rain, and absence of rain;  
[U01843](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01843) famine, illness, and classes of afflicting spirits;  
[U01844](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01844) conflict and movement of ‘mountain-throne’ (ri khri), [N-084](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-084)  
[U01845](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01845) through their respective times and classes.  
[U01846](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01846) Kings, common people, and spiritual practitioners;  
[U01847](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01847) queens, ministers, and paṇḍitas;  
[U01848](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01848) the old, the young, and livestock;  
[U01849](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01849) further, the particulars of the various animals;  
[U01850](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01850) the forms of men and women;  
[U01851](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01851) fruit harvests, field crops, and medicinal harvests—  
[U01852](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01852) their success and failure, favorable and unfavorable aspects,  
[U01853](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01853) are to be known by the yogin in sequence. [N-084](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-084)  

[U01854](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01854) [Source heading: Forty-second reply.]  
[U01855](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01855) Ema! Wondrous and marvelous,  
[U01856](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01856) this bliss of the elements is like this:  
[U01857](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01857) not known in other teachings,  
[U01858](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01858) utterly secret and unsurpassed.  
[U01859](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01859) Because you, fortunate one, command great regard,  
[U01860](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01860) unable to refuse [you], I explain it to you. [N-085](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-085)  
[U01861](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01861) The supremely fortunate, diligent person,  
[U01862](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01862) free from the mental faculty [turned toward] samsara,  
[U01863](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01863) should offer to and praise the guru.  
[U01864](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01864) Abandoning all worldly activities,  
[U01865](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01865) accord with the respective elements:  
[U01866](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01866) water, earth, fire, and wind.  
[U01867](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01867) Definitely relate each to its respective body.  
[U01868](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01868) Through the order of the respective elements,  
[U01869](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01869) definitely examine the forms of entities.  
[U01870](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01870) Regarding form, sound, smell, and taste,  
[U01871](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01871) and the qualities of their respective desires,  
[U01872](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01872) one should train, beginning with sound. [N-085](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-085) [N-T03](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t03)  
[U01873](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01873) Furthermore, the fortunate yogin  
[U01874](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01874) properly examines and investigates the colors  
[U01875](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01875) and shapes of the respective entities,  
[U01876](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01876) and applies [them] to common and supreme accomplishment.  
[U01877](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01877) Then apply [this] to their respective sounds.  
[U01878](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01878) Through definite proficiency in sound,  
[U01879](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01879) undertaking the remainder brings accomplishment.  
[U01880](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01880) Thus, concerning the outer elements,  
[U01881](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01881) through superior, middling, and inferior diligence,  
[U01882](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01882) in days, months, and years,  
[U01883](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01883) one joins the dharma embodiment and the embodiment of essence itself,  
[U01884](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01884) supremely, as the result of embodiment. [N-085](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-085)  
[U01885](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01885) For one is neuter and empty.  
[U01886](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01886) The distinctions of sound and so forth  
[U01887](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01887) depend on the respective particulars  
[U01888](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01888) of the times of abiding, entering, arising, and reversing.  
[U01889](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01889) For the application of common activities,  
[U01890](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01890) relying on the voices of ḍākinīs, the six classes,  
[U01891](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01891) and the respective elements,  
[U01892](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01892) one accomplishes higher knowing.  
[U01893](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01893) Free of food and clothing, and, regarding the elements,  
[U01894](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01894) free from sound, one confers empowerment. [N-085](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-085)  
[U01895](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01895) The body's elements are exhausted,  
[U01896](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01896) and [the body] becomes like the shadow of a lamp.  
[U01897](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01897) Thoroughly free from the sufferings of aging and death,  
[U01898](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01898) one becomes equal to the sun and moon.  
[U01899](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01899) Having cut the flow of outer and inner breath, [Editorial note: Do not attempt breath cessation from this draft.] [N-085](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-085)  
[U01900](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01900) all differentiating conceptualization is stopped.  
[U01901](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01901) Relying on non-conceptual meditative stabilization,  
[U01902](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01902) one closely joins the basic space of phenomena.  
[U01903](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01903) Having assumed a common worldly body,  
[U01904](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01904) when this itself is exhausted,  
[U01905](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01905) there is no return to the three realms.  

[U01906](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01906) [Source heading: Forty-third reply.]  
[U01907](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01907) The activity of transferring has two aspects:  
[U01908](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01908) entities themselves and emptiness.  
[U01909](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01909) Entities are included within form.  
[U01910](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01910) When one is thus proficient in the elements,  
[U01911](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01911) having stabilized familiarity with wind,  
[U01912](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01912) one can make any entity possessing form move  
[U01913](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01913) by placing knowing within it.  
[U01914](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01914) This is merely transferring into an entity. [N-086](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-086)  
[U01915](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01915) For emptiness, familiarity of ordinary mind is primary.  
[U01916](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01916) By joining the basic space of phenomena and the dharma embodiment  
[U01917](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01917) nondually, one knows [them] as empty.  
[U01918](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01918) In emptiness, whose intrinsic nature is clarity,  
[U01919](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01919) ordinary mind, too, is in that condition.  
[U01920](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01920) Through completion of the rite of dependent connections,  
[U01921](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01921) For transferring at the crucial point, the method of releasing  
[U01922](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01922) and the utterance of voices—through these, one should know. [N-086](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-086)  
[U01923](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01923) Through the meeting and gathering of the respective elements,  
[U01924](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01924) one is to transfer through seeing alone.  
[U01925](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01925) On seeing the form of an animal,  
[U01926](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01926) the skilled person joins [this] to wind.  
[U01927](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01927) But when accomplishment is reached,  
[U01928](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01928) through an invisible form all bodies are accomplished. [N-086](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-086)  
[U01929](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01929) On seeing material substances to which one is attached,  
[U01930](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01930) take [them] by transferring, to offer to the guru.  
[U01931](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01931) It is not to be done otherwise.  
[U01932](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01932) Likewise, youthful forms and so forth: [N-086](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-086)  
[U01933](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01933) when the guru, the hero, commands,  
[U01934](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01934) through familiarity with wind, take these too.  
[U01935](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01935) Possessing an ordinary mind that enters,  
[U01936](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01936) the yogin should transfer. [N-086](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-086)  

[U01937](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01937) [Source heading: Forty-fourth reply.]  
[U01938](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01938) A greatly fortunate yogin wishing to transfer,  
[U01939](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01939) within what is gathered by ordinary mind, together with its family,  
[U01940](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01940) to living beings appearing in form,  
[U01941](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01941) through inwardly concordant transformations of activity,  
[U01942](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01942) transfers his own illness and so forth to others.  
[U01943](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01943) By transferring in this way, one is released from the actual [illness]. [N-087](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-087)  
[U01944](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01944) Further, the application of dependent connections is taught.  
[U01945](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01945) From the four elements of insentient matter,  
[U01946](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01946) through dependent connections that transform entities,  
[U01947](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01947) with numbers, signs, and voices,  
[U01948](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01948) casting the object of focus, one transfers illness and so forth.  
[U01949](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01949) Sticks, pebbles, and grit;  
[U01950](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01950) grass, fragments of firebrands, potsherds, and so forth;  
[U01951](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01951) likewise, water, earth, and space—  
[U01952](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01952) definitely taking these up, one transforms the activity of transferring  
[U01953](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01953) through the power of the yogin's ordinary mind.  
[U01954](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01954) The skilled person examines its signs and measures:  
[U01955](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01955) what has been placed is broken and turned,  
[U01956](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01956) raised, laid down, and straightened;  
[U01957](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01957) shifted, lifted, and discarded;  
[U01958](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01958) held, wiped, and struck.  
[U01959](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01959) Through these, the practitioner should know thoroughly. [N-087](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-087)  
[U01960](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01960) Possessing the gesture of summoning,  
[U01961](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01961) food and wealth bound by miserliness  
[U01962](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01962) are taken by transferring, to offer to the guru.  
[U01963](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01963) When this itself is clear as an object of focus in ordinary mind,  
[U01964](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01964) like iron gathering to a lodestone,  
[U01965](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01965) one definitely takes [them].  
[U01966](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01966) But when undertaken for one's own worldly desires,  
[U01967](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01967) it is not accomplished. [N-087](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-087)  

[U01968](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01968) [Source heading: Forty-fifth reply.]  
[U01969](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01969) Not explained in any other continuum:  
[U01970](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01970) the elements' crucial points—drawing forth and reversing.  
[U01971](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01971) I will definitely explain their profundity; listen.  
[U01972](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01972) Wind, bile, and phlegm;  
[U01973](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01973) the conjunction of two and the conjunction of three;  
[U01974](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01974) their halves and twelve parts—[N-088](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-088)  
[U01975](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01975) definitely relating their sequence to the times,  
[U01976](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01976) for two causes and eight conditions,  
[U01977](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01977) identify hot and cold.  
[U01978](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01978) Join the outer and inner domains as pairs.  
[U01979](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01979) The skilled person distinguishes body and limbs,  
[U01980](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01980) relating the upper, lower, and middle places.  
[U01981](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01981) Possessing the three places, one draws out illnesses. [N-088](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-088)  
[U01982](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01982) Through the body's manifold bodily mechanisms  
[U01983](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01983) and the culmination of activities of restraining through vital essences,  
[U01984](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01984) draw all elemental illnesses outward.  
[U01985](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01985) Definitely relating body and limbs, outer and inner,  
[U01986](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01986) one should turn ordinary mind back. [N-088](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-088)  
[U01987](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01987) For speech, act through manifold sounds and voices,  
[U01988](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01988) manifold combinations of letter-signs,  
[U01989](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01989) abiding, and the meaning beyond expression.  
[U01990](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01990) For ordinary mind, relate earth, water, fire, and wind,  
[U01991](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01991) and manifold objects of focus, to the illness.  
[U01992](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01992) Reversal is through the crucial points of the faculties.  
[U01993](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01993) Through the respective numbers and measures,  
[U01994](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01994) definite signs, measures, and the interval of liberation—  
[U01995](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01995) by definitely knowing their measures of time,  
[U01996](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01996) one's own body is liberated from illness,  
[U01997](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01997) and one knows the application of dependent connections for others' bodies.  
[U01998](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01998) By acting in this way, the purpose is accomplished. [N-088](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-088)  

[U01999](alignment/chapter-01.md#u01999) [Source heading: Forty-sixth reply.]  
[U02000](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02000) The enlightened intent of tenet systems has two aspects: [N-089](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-089)  
[U02001](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02001) the enlightened intent for entering the worldly path,  
[U02002](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02002) and the enlightened intent of the ultimate meaning.  
[U02003](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02003) Through their respective Grounds and faculties,  
[U02004](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02004) the worldly and the transcendent  
[U02005](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02005) are held to be two vehicles.  
[U02006](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02006) For the cause there are three; for the result, two.  
[U02007](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02007) Dividing the latter two gives six.  
[U02008](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02008) This is according to the sequence of faculties.  
[U02009](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02009) Thus, relying on nine,  
[U02010](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02010) the three realms, too, are made to enter  
[U02011](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02011) the authentic path. [N-090](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-090)  
[U02012](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02012) The ultimate meaning is free from conceptual mind.  
[U02013](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02013) Through self-liberation for faculties of the simultaneous type,  
[U02014](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02014) and through the crucial point of seeing in direct perception,  
[U02015](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02015) the limits of one's own tenet system dissolve.  
[U02016](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02016) Without attachment or holding to anything,  
[U02017](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02017) having tasted the authentic nature of phenomena,  
[U02018](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02018) without a support for the samsara of the three realms,  
[U02019](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02019) like space dissolving into space itself,  
[U02020](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02020) the supreme yogin, too, is like that. [N-090](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-090)  

[U02021](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02021) [Source heading: Forty-seventh reply.]  
[U02022](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02022) The enlightened intent of the ultimate is this.  
[U02023](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02023) The culmination of generation is the body.  
[U02024](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02024) Through its union with the deity,  
[U02025](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02025) it culminates in the exhaustion of entities.  
[U02026](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02026) The culmination of the completion stage itself:  
[U02027](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02027) because there is movement and holding,  
[U02028](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02028) wind and knowing are united.  
[U02029](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02029) It culminates in the exhaustion of appearances.  
[U02030](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02030) For the subject expressed by a tenet system,  
[U02031](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02031) through uniting one's own and others' assertions,  
[U02032](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02032) it culminates in knowing one's own identity.  
[U02033](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02033) The culmination of Mahamudra itself:  
[U02034](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02034) transformation is bliss.  
[U02035](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02035) Through the union of bliss and the empty,  
[U02036](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02036) it culminates in the absence of mental engagement. [N-091](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-091)  
[U02037](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02037) The culmination of the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature:  
[U02038](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02038) phenomena are left in their own places.  
[U02039](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02039) Through uniting appearance and knowing with the nature of phenomena,  
[U02040](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02040) it culminates in leaving primordial liberation just as it is. [N-091](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-091)  
[U02041](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02041) Enlightened intent transcends conceptual mind;  
[U02042](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02042) what has characteristics is pure in its own place;  
[U02043](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02043) there is liberation from the extremes of the empty and entities;  
[U02044](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02044) movement is exhausted, and conceptual thought is absent;  
[U02045](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02045) conceptual mind is exhausted, and the mental faculty is transcended. [N-091](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-091)  

[U02046](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02046) [Source heading: Forty-eighth reply.]  
[U02047](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02047) Ema! In the ultimate Secret Mantra,  
[U02048](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02048) although the stages of accomplishment are numerous,  
[U02049](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02049) their principal distinction is into  
[U02050](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02050) methods and discerning knowing, supremely profound.  
[U02051](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02051) Condensing [the account] from those,  
[U02052](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02052) for now, discerning knowing will be explained.  
[U02053](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02053) Yoginī, who reveals blessings:  
[U02054](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02054) from this there are seven practices of accomplishment.  
[U02055](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02055) Approach, accomplishment, and the culmination of activities—  
[U02056](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02056) their respective supreme mantras are supremely profound.  
[U02057](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02057) Through the particular combinations and connections of letters,  
[U02058](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02058) take the respective root mantras,  
[U02059](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02059) and the practitioner begins recitation. [N-092](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-092)  
[U02060](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02060) From the clarifiers that generate,  
[U02061](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02061) through their respective colors, from outside,  
[U02062](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02062) complete all the branches of the rite,  
[U02063](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02063) and arise in the form of the Blessed Lady.  
[U02064](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02064) Adorned with empowerment of primordial knowing,  
[U02065](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02065) one desiring accomplishment continually cultivates.  
[U02066](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02066) Focus on the garlands of āli and kāli,  
[U02067](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02067) and let the yogin's conceptual mind abide.  
[U02068](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02068) Apply the seal; rely on completion in ordinary mind.  
[U02069](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02069) Restore [this]; through the body of primordial knowing,  
[U02070](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02070) repeatedly engaging in familiarity,  
[U02071](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02071) with six, seven, nine, one,  
[U02072](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02072) two, three, and five,  
[U02073](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02073) there is no doubt of accomplishment. [N-092](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-092)  
[U02074](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02074) The Ray-Bearing One, who reveals accomplishments:  
[U02075](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02075) for her, there are three practices of accomplishment.  
[U02076](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02076) Through the inner combinations of garlands of clarifiers,  
[U02077](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02077) one knows these respective mantras, too.  
[U02078](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02078) Their approach and accomplishment are as before.  
[U02079](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02079) From the rite whose branches are gathered,  
[U02080](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02080) the goddess's color, faces, and hands—  
[U02081](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02081) the yogin lets the body abide clearly [as these].  
[U02082](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02082) From the garland of light of awakened mind,  
[U02083](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02083) make supplication for whatever object of focus is desired.  
[U02084](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02084) For ordinary mind, the letters are exceedingly clear;  
[U02085](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02085) leave [it] in the Great Perfection of union.  
[U02086](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02086) Continually cultivating in this way,  
[U02087](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02087) or relying continuously on six sessions,  
[U02088](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02088) with nine, seven, and five,  
[U02089](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02089) there is definitely no doubt of accomplishment. [N-092](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-092)  
[U02090](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02090) Tsantika, who reveals activities:  
[U02091](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02091) for her, too, there are three practices of accomplishment.  
[U02092](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02092) Through wondrous combinations of the letters of basic space,  
[U02093](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02093) one should know these mantras, too.  
[U02094](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02094) The yogin endowed with the rite  
[U02095](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02095) imagines one's own ordinary mind abiding pure.  
[U02096](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02096) In the three colors of the goddess's form,  
[U02097](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02097) through the application of placing letters,  
[U02098](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02098) one clears away discordant characteristics.  
[U02099](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02099) For the activities, cultivate the respective wrathful female [forms].  
[U02100](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02100) When the activities are complete, the yoga  
[U02101](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02101) of empty ordinary mind is perfected.  
[U02102](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02102) Through continually cultivating in this way,  
[U02103](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02103) with one, two, and three,  
[U02104](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02104) all desired purposes are accomplished. [N-092](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-092)  
[U02105](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02105) The profound practices of accomplishment of methods  
[U02106](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02106) will be explained in brief.  
[U02107](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02107) The culmination of activities is the Slayer of Death.  
[U02108](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02108) For him, there are two practices of accomplishment.  
[U02109](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02109) Through joining āli and kāli equally,  
[U02110](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02110) one should also know his mantras.  
[U02111](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02111) Having completed the activities of the wheel,  
[U02112](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02112) and with the enlightened intent of the rite complete,  
[U02113](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02113) Whichever letters accord with one's aspiration—  
[U02114](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02114) definitely imagine their colors, [N-093](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-093)  
[U02115](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02115) and undertake the yoga of the Slayer of Death.  
[U02116](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02116) In one, transform the culmination of activities;  
[U02117](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02117) rely on aspiration in accordance with whatever is desired.  
[U02118](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02118) In the other, with the letters complete,  
[U02119](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02119) the yogin's ordinary mind is to abide.  
[U02120](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02120) The culmination of power is the Unmoving Protector.  
[U02121](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02121) For him, there are three practices of accomplishment.  
[U02122](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02122) Through the respective connections of āli and kāli,  
[U02123](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02123) one should know these mantras.  
[U02124](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02124) For the five pledges to be guarded,  
[U02125](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02125) complete the rites.  
[U02126](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02126) The embodiment of the wrathful king, the Unmoving Protector—  
[U02127](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02127) with faith, definitely imagine one's own body [as this].  
[U02128](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02128) Through rays of light in the form of hooks,  
[U02129](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02129) proliferate the activities of summoning, killing, and binding, [Source variant: ‘Expelling also.’] [N-093](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-093)  
[U02130](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02130) in whatever way one desires.  
[U02131](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02131) Dedicate [them] toward complete awakening.  
[U02132](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02132) For these profound practices of accomplishment:  
[U02133](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02133) the Slayer of Death—three hundred thousand;  
[U02134](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02134) with two and five [hundred thousand], yoga is complete.  
[U02135](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02135) The Unmoving One—one hundred thousand;  
[U02136](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02136) likewise, with two and three [hundred thousand], it is accomplished. [N-093](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-093)  
[U02137](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02137) The two practices of accomplishment of the profound meaning  
[U02138](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02138) are definitely condensed in brief.  
[U02139](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02139) Whoever entertains doubt about these  
[U02140](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02140) breaks the command of the buddha himself.  
[U02141](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02141) Therefore, accomplishment is certain. [N-093](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-093)  

[U02142](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02142) [Source heading: Forty-ninth reply.]  
[U02143](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02143) The manner in which the natural state abides is like this.  
[U02144](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02144) Were it explained, hearers and solitary buddhas  
[U02145](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02145) would be greatly terrified and faint.  
[U02146](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02146) Therefore, it should be kept supremely secret.  
[U02147](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02147) Phenomena without examination  
[U02148](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02148) are called the Ground without arising. [N-094](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-094)  
[U02149](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02149) Because there is no abiding in it,  
[U02150](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02150) neither entities nor the empty abide.  
[U02151](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02151) Clarity and darkness have no possible arising,  
[U02152](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02152) because there is no possible limit of going or coming.  
[U02153](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02153) Not bound by endeavor and effort,  
[U02154](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02154) there is openness itself, and penetration occurs. [N-094](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-094)  
[U02155](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02155) Because there is no confinement or falling to a side,  
[U02156](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02156) neither virtue nor misdeeds are established,  
[U02157](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02157) for both virtue and misdeeds are excluded.  
[U02158](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02158) Because samsara and nirvana do not abide,  
[U02159](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02159) and the empty nature of phenomena pervades all,  
[U02160](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02160) primordial knowing abides in its own identity;  
[U02161](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02161) in the manner of three, [they are] indivisible. [N-094](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-094)  

[U02162](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02162) [Source heading: Fiftieth reply.]  
[U02163](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02163) Not explained elsewhere,  
[U02164](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02164) the wondrous application of calculating delusion—  
[U02165](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02165) I will explain it; listen well.  
[U02166](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02166) From the root dependent connections,  
[U02167](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02167) with the elements of the respective bodies  
[U02168](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02168) and the linkages of the wheel of dependent connections,  
[U02169](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02169) definitely grasp the measures of the times of delusion.  
[U02170](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02170) From the times of coarse abiding,  
[U02171](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02171) identify the delusion of the yogin's ordinary mind.  
[U02172](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02172) The time of delusion, time of abiding, and time of reversal:  
[U02173](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02173) from these three distinctions, twelve measures.  
[U02174](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02174) Through the bounded measuring mechanism,  
[U02175](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02175) the skilled person grasps the cells of the grid. [N-095](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-095)  
[U02176](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02176) Through time, number, and appropriation,  
[U02177](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02177) relate [these] to aggregates, elements, and so forth.  
[U02178](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02178) Through the crucial point that reverses, too,  
[U02179](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02179) reverse the delusion of the respective embodied beings.  

[U02180](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02180) [Source heading: Fifty-first reply.]  
[U02181](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02181) Regarding the individual characteristics of objects of knowledge,  
[U02182](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02182) through knowing the transformations of general and particular,  
[U02183](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02183) although the respective assertions differ,  
[U02184](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02184) for viewing the worldly activity of the mental faculty,  
[U02185](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02185) conceptual minds delighting in elaborations  
[U02186](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02186) temporarily engage with words. [N-096](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-096)  
[U02187](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02187) In the tradition, through two particular combinations,  
[U02188](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02188) cut the elaborations that distinguish samsara and nirvana.  
[U02189](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02189) Through the wheel of the crucial points of cultivation,  
[U02190](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02190) cut the elaborations of the connection of wind-mind.  
[U02191](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02191) Through the sequence of the path of entering knowing,  
[U02192](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02192) cut the conceptual minds of doubt and delusion. [N-096](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-096)  

[U02193](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02193) [Source heading: Fifty-second reply.]  
[U02194](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02194) By the guru, the great vajra-holder,  
[U02195](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02195) the sequence will be explained.  
[U02196](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02196) The guru whose characteristics are complete  
[U02197](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02197) is the source of all qualities  
[U02198](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02198) and upholds the Ground of complete awakening. [N-097](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-097)  
[U02199](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02199) There are two gurus: root and branch.  
[U02200](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02200) For those whose respective characteristics are complete,  
[U02201](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02201) with sovereignty and one's own body,  
[U02202](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02202) retinue, and enjoyments, rely on [them].  
[U02203](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02203) Pleasing [them] through bodily and verbal activity,  
[U02204](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02204) rely on the guru in equal union with the buddha. [N-097](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-097)  
[U02205](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02205) Endowed with faith and discerning knowing,  
[U02206](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02206) with aspiration and without delusion,  
[U02207](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02207) free from delusion and error about what is to be expressed,  
[U02208](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02208) with clear faculties, rely on the guru.  
[U02209](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02209) From this reliance, the qualities [attained]  
[U02210](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02210) are like a wish-granting tree, a wish-fulfilling jewel,  
[U02211](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02211) and a cow that yields whatever is desired:  
[U02212](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02212) one attains immeasurable qualities.  
[U02213](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02213) Focusing on that, rely on the guru,  
[U02214](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02214) for it turns one back from the battlefield of samsara. [N-097](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-097)  

[U02215](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02215) [Source heading: Fifty-third reply.]  
[U02216](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02216) The outer elemental signs are solidity,  
[U02217](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02217) heat and burning, gathering and moisture,  
[U02218](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02218) lightness and separation, and pervasion.  
[U02219](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02219) Through portions of what is to be learned about the elements,  
[U02220](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02220) the yogin's body and speech  
[U02221](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02221) hold these as well.  
[U02222](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02222) The definite times of the twelve years,  
[U02223](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02223) properly joined in accord with oneself:  
[U02224](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02224) at the beginning of each respective time,  
[U02225](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02225) sound, instructions, speech, and so forth—[Source variant: ‘Bad news also occurs.’] [N-098](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-098)  
[U02226](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02226) from twelve magical displays of objects of focus,  
[U02227](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02227) the gods—among the worldly gods,  
[U02228](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02228) the eleven who act as protectors—  
[U02229](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02229) are definitely taken as objects of focus as portents,  
[U02230](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02230) and made to appear in Jambudvīpa. [N-098](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-098)  
[U02231](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02231) Through their manner of vanishing into space,  
[U02232](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02232) the magical displays, too, are definitely distinguished.  
[U02233](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02233) Grasping the directions of one's own place in Jambudvīpa,  
[U02234](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02234) to the kings of the east and so forth,  
[U02235](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02235) great people, common people, and others,  
[U02236](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02236) they definitely show virtue and nonvirtue.  
[U02237](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02237) Likewise, the success and failure of kingdoms,  
[U02238](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02238) virtue and nonvirtue, are examined by the skilled person.  
[U02239](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02239) One thereby gains accomplishment concerning entities,  
[U02240](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02240) and worldly higher knowing is definitely accomplished. [N-098](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-098)  

[U02241](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02241) [Source heading: Fifty-fourth reply.]  
[U02242](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02242) The time is when the continuation of karma awakens,  
[U02243](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02243) when the elements transform, and at the crucial point of ordinary mind.  
[U02244](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02244) Through purification of delusion, one's own time is found.  
[U02245](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02245) That is the time of its reversal.  
[U02246](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02246) The teaching's hearth (thab) is the empty,  
[U02247](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02247) emptiness itself, and the terminator. [N-099](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-099)  
[U02248](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02248) The remaining times of individual persons  
[U02249](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02249) are the same as what was taught above.  

[U02250](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02250) [Source heading: Fifty-fifth reply.]  
[U02251](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02251) Ema! Lord of gods, sovereign,  
[U02252](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02252) faithful one of great fortune, listen.  
[U02253](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02253) Hidden in other continua,  
[U02254](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02254) known by no one,  
[U02255](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02255) the rite of conferring empowerment will be explained.  
[U02256](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02256) Outer, inner, secret,  
[U02257](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02257) and the fully complete secret:  
[U02258](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02258) through these four combinations,  
[U02259](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02259) the four—beings' bodies, speech, mental faculty, and ordinary mind—  
[U02260](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02260) in order to purify [them],  
[U02261](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02261) through four empowerments, too,  
[U02262](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02262) bring the fortunate to maturity. [N-100](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-100)  
[U02263](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02263) With elaborations, without elaborations,  
[U02264](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02264) utterly without elaborations,  
[U02265](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02265) and, likewise, completely without elaborations:  
[U02266](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02266) through these four distinctions,  
[U02267](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02267) the faithful mature their own continua.  
[U02268](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02268) For the purpose of [empowerment] with elaborations, too,  
[U02269](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02269) approach, accomplishment, and close approach  
[U02270](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02270) are to be undertaken by a guru whose characteristics are complete.  
[U02271](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02271) Furthermore, with the earth rite,  
[U02272](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02272) preparations, lines, and spreading colors,  
[U02273](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02273) construct the maṇḍala that occurs in the continuum.  
[U02274](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02274) Then, for the rite of conferring empowerment, too,  
[U02275](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02275) beginning with the hearer vehicle,  
[U02276](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02276) complete the eight stages,  
[U02277](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02277) then confer the Great Perfection empowerment of awareness's expressiveness. [N-100](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-100)  
[U02278](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02278) Explain their respective enlightened intents,  
[U02279](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02279) entry, and distinctive capacities.  
[U02280](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02280) To mature the faithful who enter without elaborations,  
[U02281](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02281) for the supreme empowerment without elaborations,  
[U02282](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02282) relying on a maṇḍala and vase,  
[U02283](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02283) give empowerment in verses.  
[U02284](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02284) For [empowerment] utterly without elaborations,  
[U02285](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02285) distinguish samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary, as below. [N-100](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-100)  
[U02286](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02286) Then, through the characteristics of space,  
[U02287](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02287) abandon the obscurations of body, speech, and mental faculty.  
[U02288](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02288) Definitely examine [this] through its characteristics.  
[U02289](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02289) When activity is impure,  
[U02290](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02290) purify [it] through virtue appropriate to each individual.  
[U02291](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02291) For empowerment, relying on signs,  
[U02292](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02292) introduce the own identity of clear self-awareness. [N-T46](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t46)  
[U02293](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02293) The skilled person should give pith instructions that explain the signs.  
[U02294](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02294) For [empowerment] completely without elaborations,  
[U02295](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02295) open the gate of the maṇḍala of ordinary mind,  
[U02296](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02296) and definitely undertake the bodily posture.  
[U02297](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02297) Becoming familiar with it, apply the gaze.  
[U02298](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02298) Directly perceived awareness is the sign of authoritative transmission. [N-100](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-100)  

[U02299](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02299) [Source heading: Fifty-sixth reply.]  
[U02300](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02300) Hero Free from Conceptualization, son of the gods,  
[U02301](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02301) to satisfy the sovereign Delight-Maker,  
[U02302](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02302) the pledges depending on empowerment will be explained.  
[U02303](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02303) Though words do not suffice for their particulars,  
[U02304](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02304) in brief, relate embodiment, speech, and awakened mind  
[U02305](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02305) to the yogin's body, speech, and mental faculty.  
[U02306](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02306) The objects of focus—the guru and vajra siblings—  
[U02307](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02307) are distinguished as the objects to be guarded.  
[U02308](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02308) Vows are included in body and speech. [N-101](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-101)  
[U02309](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02309) Through verbal expressions and the categories of tenet systems,  
[U02310](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02310) the nine stages involve the activity of the mental faculty.  
[U02311](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02311) Separated from the crucial point of direct perception,  
[U02312](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02312) future sentient beings to be trained  
[U02313](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02313) will place their trust in words.  
[U02314](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02314) Sentient beings with coarse conceptual thought,  
[U02315](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02315) because they engage in words, are not mature.  
[U02316](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02316) At the final time of this teaching,  
[U02317](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02317) this is called the ground of aspirational activity. [N-101](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-101)  

[U02318](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02318) [Source heading: Fifty-eighth reply.]  
[U02319](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02319) Beyond words, elaborations are greatly complete. [N-102](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-102)  
[U02320](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02320) This continuum of definite secrecy,  
[U02321](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02321) relying on the path of direct perception,  
[U02322](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02322) gathers one's own knowing into awareness.  
[U02323](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02323) Gathering all buddhas,  
[U02324](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02324) the definite nature of phenomena of self-awareness  
[U02325](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02325) is enacted through evenness without mixing. [N-102](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-102)  
[U02326](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02326) This is the stage beyond words.  

[U02327](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02327) [Source heading: Fifty-ninth reply.]  
[U02328](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02328) The crucial point of direct perception: body, speech, and ordinary mind.  
[U02329](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02329) Definitely bring their respective crucial points into accord.  
[U02330](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02330) Furthermore, the body [abides] in the three embodiments;  
[U02331](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02331) one should never depart from them. [N-103](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-103)  
[U02332](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02332) For speech, training and abiding, [Source variant: ‘Waves also.’] [N-103](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-103)  
[U02333](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02333) and likewise passing beyond, are to be taught.  
[U02334](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02334) Ordinary mind is accomplished by directing it toward basic space.  
[U02335](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02335) Do not separate from the pith instructions on the three crucial points.  
[U02336](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02336) Furthermore, rely on the crucial points of the door, object,  
[U02337](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02337) wind, and awareness.  
[U02338](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02338) For the door, view the three embodiments with the eyes.  
[U02339](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02339) For the object, be free of what obscures.  
[U02340](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02340) Through wind and awareness being slow, primordial knowing is accomplished.  
[U02341](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02341) In this there are four visions.  
[U02342](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02342) Through the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,  
[U02343](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02343) one transcends words held through mental examination. [N-103](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-103)  
[U02344](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02344) Through the vision of increasing experience,  
[U02345](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02345) delusive appearances are made to subside.  
[U02346](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02346) Through the vision of awareness reaching its limit,  
[U02347](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02347) one transcends the path appearances of realizing the three embodiments.  
[U02348](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02348) Through the vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena,  
[U02349](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02349) one cuts the flow of samsara of the three realms.  
[U02350](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02350) Rely on this to the end and have conviction. [N-103](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-103)  
[U02351](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02351) With a Ground in the three nonmovements,  
[U02352](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02352) one arrives at the crucial point of wind-mind.  
[U02353](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02353) Taking the measure through three ways of remaining,  
[U02354](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02354) dreams are reversed; for body, speech, and mental faculty,  
[U02355](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02355) signs and measures are definitely grasped.  
[U02356](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02356) Fixing [this] with the three attainments,  
[U02357](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02357) contaminated aggregates do not appear. [N-103](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-103)  

[U02358](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02358) [Source heading: Sixtieth reply.]  
[U02359](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02359) Ema! How greatly wondrous!  
[U02360](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02360) Unless samsara and nirvana are distinguished through the secret preliminary,  
[U02361](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02361) the connection of the three realms with body, speech,  
[U02362](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02362) and mental faculty will not be cut.  
[U02363](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02363) Therefore, I explain distinguishing samsara and nirvana through the secret preliminary.  
[U02364](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02364) This has three aspects. [N-104](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-104)  
[U02365](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02365) For the body: walking and remaining,  
[U02366](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02366) compressing [the body] and manifold bodily mechanisms,  
[U02367](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02367) extending and gathering the limbs,  
[U02368](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02368) and relying on manifold actions.  
[U02369](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02369) Rousing [these] with a mindful ordinary mind,  
[U02370](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02370) the activities of the forms of the six destinies—  
[U02371](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02371) take [them] up in ordinary mind and perform [them] with the body.  
[U02372](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02372) Whoever can engage in this way—  
[U02373](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02373) whatever is undertaken, its flow is later cut. [N-104](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-104)  
[U02374](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02374) This has two purposes,  
[U02375](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02375) examined as common and supreme.  
[U02376](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02376) When signs and measures are reached in this way,  
[U02377](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02377) the yogin begins verbal activity.  
[U02378](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02378) Because expression has the intrinsic nature of conceptual thought,  
[U02379](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02379) [use] manifold sounds and voices.  
[U02380](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02380) Because whatever is spoken has no reversal, [N-104](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-104)  
[U02381](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02381) [use] the voices of gods, nāgas, and yakṣas,  
[U02382](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02382) gandharvas, kumbhāṇḍas, and the All-Pervader;  
[U02383](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02383) utter their favorable and unfavorable varieties.  
[U02384](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02384) In brief, the voices of the six destinies:  
[U02385](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02385) utter ordinary mind's mindfulness and reflection through speech. [N-104](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-104)  
[U02386](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02386) Here, too, there are two purposes:  
[U02387](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02387) the distinction between supreme and common culminations.  
[U02388](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02388) Having reached the measure in this way, too,  
[U02389](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02389) the yogin should train ordinary mind.  
[U02390](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02390) For past, future, and present,  
[U02391](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02391) rely on the wheel of mindfulness and reflection, proliferating and gathering.  
[U02392](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02392) Ordinary minds of liking and disliking, favorable and unfavorable—  
[U02393](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02393) all manifold [notions] of existence and nonexistence—  
[U02394](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02394) examine outwardly and reflect inwardly.  
[U02395](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02395) The mindfulness of whatever is reflected on—  
[U02396](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02396) afterward, its flow of arising will definitely be cut. [N-104](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-104)  
[U02397](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02397) Its purposes are distinguished by what was said above.  
[U02398](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02398) When activity has been completed in this way,  
[U02399](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02399) continually apply [it] to the crucial point of direct perception.  
[U02400](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02400) When that is complete, one does not enter the three realms.  
[U02401](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02401) It should be taught in utmost secrecy.  

[U02402](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02402) [Source heading: Sixty-first reply.]  
[U02403](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02403) Relying on bodily activity,  
[U02404](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02404) one wishing to retain the inner wind:  
[U02405](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02405) the twenty-one activities of retaining wind— [N-105](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-105)  
[U02406](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02406) when the yogin performs and engages in these,  
[U02407](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02407) one becomes familiar with wind and proficient in the body.  
[U02408](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02408) For one endowed with ornaments,  
[U02409](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02409) make a wheel of dependent connections at the place.  
[U02410](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02410) Direct wind and knowing toward the ornaments.  
[U02411](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02411) While moving, apply the manner of gazing with the eyes.  
[U02412](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02412) While remaining, project ordinary mind. [N-105](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-105)  

[U02413](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02413) [Source heading: Sixty-second reply.]  
[U02414](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02414) Through twenty-one trainings in retaining ordinary mind,  
[U02415](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02415) first, bring it to abide;  
[U02416](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02416) second, separate body and ordinary mind;  
[U02417](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02417) third, see the nature of phenomena.  
[U02418](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02418) At this time, the crucial points of body, speech, and ordinary mind  
[U02419](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02419) are to be allowed to settle by one endowed with yoga.  
[U02420](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02420) Also with the signs of the wind that causes movement,  
[U02421](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02421) mixing, projecting, and gathering—  
[U02422](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02422) when the skilled person relies on these crucial points,  
[U02423](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02423) the flow of ordinary mind's conceptual thought is cut. [N-105](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-105)  

[U02424](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02424) [Source heading: Sixty-third reply.]  
[U02425](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02425) There are twenty-one signs of death, too.  
[U02426](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02426) Having gone to a secluded charnel-ground place,  
[U02427](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02427) offer to the guru and make a torma.  
[U02428](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02428) Furthermore, with food and drink,  
[U02429](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02429) satisfy other beings as well.  
[U02430](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02430) Then examine the outer, inner, and secret  
[U02431](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02431) signs of death, too.  
[U02432](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02432) When they are unfavorable, undertake reversal;  
[U02433](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02433) when favorable, make supplication to the guru. [N-105](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-105)  

[U02434](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02434) [Source heading: Sixty-fourth reply.]  
[U02435](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02435) Lord of the gods, for the sake of sentient beings,  
[U02436](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02436) the answer to that compassionate question you asked—  
[U02437](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02437) I teach it; grasp it without delusion.  
[U02438](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02438) In the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,  
[U02439](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02439) although the conceptual mind of a view with a directed object of focus is exhausted,  
[U02440](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02440) for now, establish certainty through words. [N-106](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-106)  
[U02441](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02441) Through the crucial point of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena, too,  
[U02442](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02442) one realizes that activities have neither favorable nor unfavorable [status].  
[U02443](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02443) Through definitely gathering the heart-sphere,  
[U02444](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02444) faculties are neither sharp nor dull.  
[U02445](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02445) Because the limit of the words of mental examination is exhausted,  
[U02446](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02446) one sees through the faculty of the eye of discerning knowing.  
[U02447](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02447) Because words do not reveal the nature of phenomena,  
[U02448](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02448) the common vehicles do not accomplish the meaning.  
[U02449](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02449) Because the three embodiments are path appearances,  
[U02450](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02450) seeing this, one does not return to the three realms. [N-106](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-106)  
[U02451](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02451) Because all phenomena are equally of one taste,  
[U02452](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02452) there is no dependence on Ground and result.  
[U02453](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02453) Because self-awareness is free from all conceptual thought,  
[U02454](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02454) there are four ways of liberation.  
[U02455](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02455) Through confidence in liberation at the four crucial points,  
[U02456](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02456) samsara and passing beyond sorrow do not abide.  
[U02457](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02457) Phenomena that have not arisen and are not made  
[U02458](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02458) have neither Ground nor root.  
[U02459](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02459) The crucial points of liberation through hearing and seeing  
[U02460](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02460) are realized through two, five, and eight. [N-106](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-106)  
[U02461](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02461) Through four great confidences, one joins basic space.  
[U02462](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02462) Through three instances of pointing out, one should gain conviction.  

[U02463](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02463) [Source heading: Sixty-fifth reply.]  
[U02464](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02464) Next, the life-tree of cultivation is explained.  
[U02465](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02465) Definitely relate [this] through the authoritative transmission of ordinary mind.  
[U02466](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02466) Furthermore, concerning the crucial points of cultivation,  
[U02467](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02467) preliminary practice, main practice,  
[U02468](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02468) and subsequent practice are held to be three.  
[U02469](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02469) Training the body, taming the nature of ordinary mind,  
[U02470](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02470) and completing the activities of speech,  
[U02471](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02471) the nature of ordinary mind enters the path.  
[U02472](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02472) Relying on wind, body and ordinary mind separate.  
[U02473](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02473) In order to cut the opening from which the three realms proliferate,  
[U02474](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02474) reverse attachment to body and speech from its depths.  
[U02475](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02475) Because the precipices of the six destinies are equal,  
[U02476](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02476) press the crucial points of body, speech, and mental faculty. [N-107](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-107)  
[U02477](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02477) In order to mix the nature of ordinary mind with phenomena,  
[U02478](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02478) draw wind and knowing upward.  
[U02479](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02479) In order to exhaust delusive appearances,  
[U02480](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02480) continually become familiar with wind. [N-107](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-107)  

[U02481](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02481) [Source heading: Sixty-sixth reply.]  
[U02482](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02482) For the application of activity, in basic space and awareness,  
[U02483](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02483) endowed with three fixings, without separation,  
[U02484](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02484) continual familiarity makes activity easeful.  
[U02485](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02485) When all phenomena are free from doing,  
[U02486](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02486) and the connection of samsara and nirvana is cut,  
[U02487](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02487) delusion with conceptual thought is stopped.  
[U02488](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02488) By cutting the flow of samsara's precipice,  
[U02489](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02489) of the great yogin's activity—[Source heading: Sixty-seventh reply.] [N-108](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-108)  
[U02490](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02490) although the result cannot be expressed,  
[U02491](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02491) one reaches the exhaustion of phenomena,  
[U02492](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02492) and the limits of one's own tenet system, too, collapse.  
[U02493](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02493) At this time, the guru's pith instructions subside.  
[U02494](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02494) Because the limits of viewing, cultivation, and activity are empty,  
[U02495](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02495) there is no appearance as phenomena.  
[U02496](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02496) Because the flow of embodiments and primordial knowing is cut,  
[U02497](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02497) there are neither buddhas nor sentient beings.  
[U02498](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02498) In brief, nothing whatsoever abides.  
[U02499](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02499) Because there is no going, there is no coming. [N-108](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-108)  

[U02500](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02500) [Source heading: Sixty-eighth reply.]  
[U02501](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02501) One wishing to cut the flow of samsara,  
[U02502](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02502) having reversed attachment to body and speech,  
[U02503](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02503) should press the crucial point of direct perception.  
[U02504](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02504) Through the application of calculating delusion,  
[U02505](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02505) grasp the measures of the times of samsara and nirvana,  
[U02506](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02506) and become skilled in the crucial point of reversal.  
[U02507](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02507) By reaching the measure of the six capabilities,  
[U02508](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02508) one is thoroughly liberated from the six faults.  
[U02509](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02509) This cuts the flow of samsara. [N-109](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-109)  

[U02510](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02510) [Source heading: Sixty-ninth reply.]  
[U02511](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02511) Taking nourishment from vital essences is like this:  
[U02512](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02512) not taught in any other continuum,  
[U02513](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02513) Ema! It is explained as greatly wondrous.  
[U02514](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02514) Lord of gods, hero, listen!  
[U02515](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02515) By relying on precious substances,  
[U02516](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02516) the lifespan becomes equal to the sun and moon.  
[U02517](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02517) Through preparations of various stones,  
[U02518](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02518) weapons do not penetrate the body.  
[U02519](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02519) Through preparations relying on herbs,  
[U02520](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02520) white hair and wrinkles disappear. [N-110](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-110)  
[U02521](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02521) Through taking nourishment from vital essences of medicinal preparations,  
[U02522](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02522) one becomes endowed with splendor and youthful.  
[U02523](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02523) Through preparations relying on meat,  
[U02524](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02524) the bodily constituents are sustained, and strength develops.  
[U02525](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02525) Furthermore, relying on wind,  
[U02526](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02526) greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.  
[U02527](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02527) What is called cutting the flow of the elements  
[U02528](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02528) is known through the upper and lower winds.  
[U02529](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02529) For the skilled person, wind is easily made to abide.  
[U02530](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02530) Next, through calculating wind's movement,  
[U02531](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02531) there is the yoga of food.  
[U02532](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02532) Relying on the elements,  
[U02533](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02533) whatever appears becomes food.  
[U02534](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02534) Through taking nourishment from vital essences in this way,  
[U02535](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02535) the torments of beings are cut off. [N-110](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-110)  

[U02536](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02536) [Source heading: Seventieth reply.]  
[U02537](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02537) The yoga of clothing is like this:  
[U02538](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02538) perform it through wind and profound dependent connections.  
[U02539](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02539) From projecting and gathering wind,  
[U02540](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02540) through the crucial point of going, coming, and holding, too,  
[U02541](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02541) distinguish the respective elemental objects of focus  
[U02542](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02542) in accord with the yogin's body.  
[U02543](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02543) Through the application of dependent connections,  
[U02544](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02544) with two, six, seven, and three,  
[U02545](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02545) properly combine pills and powders,  
[U02546](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02546) and carry out the culmination of ingesting and applying [them]. [N-111](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-111)  

[U02547](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02547) [Source heading: Seventy-first reply.]  
[U02548](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02548) Next, for cutting the flow of the body's elements,  
[U02549](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02549) greatly wondrous taking of nourishment from vital essences is explained.  
[U02550](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02550) By continually circulating and reversing ‘great odor’ (dri chen), [N-112](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-112)  
[U02551](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02551) finally, the body's discharges are exhausted.  
[U02552](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02552) Alternatively, extracted vital essence and liquid butter—  
[U02553](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02553) by relying on [these] in the proper measure,  
[U02554](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02554) the flows of the elements are cut.  
[U02555](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02555) Because the flow of the body's elements is cut,  
[U02556](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02556) living creatures do not abide in the body;  
[U02557](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02557) there are no feces, urine, and so forth;  
[U02558](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02558) outer and inner wind, too, cease to be. [N-112](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-112)  
[U02559](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02559) At this time, the great yogin  
[U02560](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02560) becomes pure with respect to both food and clothing.  
[U02561](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02561) Through this, concerning lifespan and enjoyments,  
[U02562](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02562) whatever is desired is accomplished. [N-112](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-112)  

[U02563](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02563) [Source heading: Seventy-second reply.]  
[U02564](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02564) For the four elements,  
[U02565](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02565) a yogin who makes their tastes equal  
[U02566](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02566) brings the crucial points of earth, water, fire, and wind into accord.  
[U02567](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02567) Mixing [them] with the body, raise [them] through wind.  
[U02568](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02568) Transform one's own body through the respective elements,  
[U02569](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02569) and, in engaging in this itself,  
[U02570](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02570) cultivate the maṇḍala gathering the crucial points. [N-113](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-113)  
[U02571](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02571) Through reaching the measure of familiarity with this,  
[U02572](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02572) the powers of the four become equal in their own identity.  
[U02573](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02573) One engages easefully and is free from destruction.  
[U02574](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02574) Through this, too, both food and clothing are accomplished,  
[U02575](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02575) and lifespan and enjoyments are complete.  

[U02576](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02576) [Source heading: Seventy-third reply.]  
[U02577](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02577) The qualities of the treasury are like this:  
[U02578](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02578) with common and supreme accomplishments,  
[U02579](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02579) one plays as one pleases, accomplishing [them] easefully.  
[U02580](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02580) The branches of dependent connections are complete.  
[U02581](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02581) Miracles and higher knowing, too,  
[U02582](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02582) are definitely accomplished through engaging with the treasury.  
[U02583](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02583) Illnesses and measures of time become known. [N-113](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-113)  

[U02584](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02584) [Source heading: Seventy-fourth reply.]  
[U02585](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02585) One wishing to accomplish noble miracles,  
[U02586](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02586) with a support that is an entity or with what is not an entity,  
[U02587](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02587) definitely accomplishes [them] through training in wind.  
[U02588](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02588) Word, craft, and dependent connections;  
[U02589](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02589) signs, phrases, and conventions;  
[U02590](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02590) examples, actions, and methods of training—  
[U02591](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02591) relying on these and engaging in karmic wind,  
[U02592](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02592) one accomplishes whatever miracles are desired.  
[U02593](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02593) Through cultivating primordial knowing free from conceptualization,  
[U02594](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02594) reflecting on emptiness, and the crucial point, too,  
[U02595](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02595) without endeavor or effort, miracles  
[U02596](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02596) will definitely be accomplished. [N-114](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-114)  

[U02597](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02597) [Source heading: Seventy-fifth reply.]  
[U02598](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02598) One wishing to enjoy inexhaustible treasure  
[U02599](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02599) relies on the mechanism of the market (khrom).  
[U02600](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02600) Become skilled in calculating its application. [N-114](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-114)  
[U02601](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02601) Endowed with times of three, four, and two,  
[U02602](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02602) having concealed [it] for ten months and thoroughly dried [it],
