[U02603](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02603) begin directing [it] toward whichever direction is desired.  
[U02604](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02604) By joining [it] with rays and wind,  
[U02605](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02605) whatever activities one wishes to undertake are accomplished. [N-114](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-114)  
[U02606](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02606) Alternatively, through profound practice of accomplishment,  
[U02607](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02607) distinguishing approach, accomplishment, and the culmination of activities,  
[U02608](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02608) rely on the lord of wealth.  
[U02609](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02609) Through familiarity with mantra, enjoy [the treasure].  

[U02610](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02610) [Source heading: Seventy-sixth reply.]  
[U02611](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02611) By bringing four and five under one's power,  
[U02612](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02612) the master of ordinary mind holds the body.  
[U02613](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02613) Within the wheels of the three places, too,  
[U02614](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02614) with four and five syllables, and the assignment of forms, [N-115](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-115)  
[U02615](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02615) [Source variant: ‘Of color also occurs.’] [N-115](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-115)  
[U02616](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02616) perform proliferating, gathering, absorbing, and coiling.  
[U02617](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02617) Through completing this, there is flight,  
[U02618](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02618) entry without separation, and no burning;  
[U02619](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02619) one cannot sink and can run. [N-115](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-115)  
[U02620](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02620) [Source annotation: This is their concluding summary.]  
[U02621](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02621) The teacher, the buddha, the Blessed One,  
[U02622](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02622) all his qualities complete in themselves,  
[U02623](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02623) without utterance or spoken discourse,  
[U02624](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02624) from the play of naturally arisen primordial knowing,  
[U02625](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02625) as a miraculous display of words, phrases, and names,  
[U02626](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02626) appears to those of good fortune.  
[U02627](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02627) Thus, the secret phrases of the Great Perfection—  
[U02628](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02628) the lord of the gods, having definitely heard them,  
[U02629](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02629) with respectful body and palms properly joined,  
[U02630](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02630) rejoiced in the teacher himself. [N-115](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-115)  
[U02631](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02631) From the Great Perfection of everything,  
[U02632](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02632) intrinsic nature became free from elaborations,  
[U02633](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02633) inseparable from the basic space of the nature of phenomena. [N-115](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-115)  
[U02634](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02634) Thus, from The Great All-Penetrating Word, the Root of All Phenomena,  
[U02635](alignment/chapter-01.md#u02635) this is the first chapter: ‘Array of Diversity: The Definite Arising of the Root of Naturally Arisen Pith Instructions.’ [N-115](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-115)  

**S0002 — scan-only material, PDF page 102:** [Scan-only annotation at the chapter transition: reading unresolved.] [N-116](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-116)


<a id="part-2"></a>

## Chapter 2

[U02636](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02636) Then the lord of the gods, free from conceptual thought,  
[U02637](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02637) though inseparable from the basic space of phenomena,  
[U02638](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02638) for the sentient beings of future times,  
[U02639](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02639) through realization, definitely from samsara—  
[U02640](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02640) with the conceptual mind [intent on] liberating them,  
[U02641](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02641) first arousing ordinary mind,  
[U02642](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02642) addressed the teacher himself in these words: [N-117](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-117)  
[U02643](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02643) ‘Kyé, kyé! Miraculously born embodiment!  
[U02644](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02644) For sentient beings, from the samsara of the three realms,  
[U02645](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02645) what is the crucial point that brings liberation?  
[U02646](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02646) Blessed One, I make this request.  
[U02647](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02647) What is the beginning of sentient beings' samsara?  
[U02648](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02648) By what is the final extent of passing beyond [sorrow] grasped? [N-117](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-117) [N-122](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-122)  
[U02649](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02649) What is this crucial point of buddhas' not being deluded?  
[U02650](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02650) From what does sentient beings' delusion arise?  
[U02651](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02651) Where does the primordial knowing of self-awareness abide?  
[U02652](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02652) From what does its path arise?  
[U02653](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02653) What is the door through which primordial knowing arises?  
[U02654](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02654) What is the crucial point of the lamps like?  
[U02655](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02655) How do their characteristics appear?  
[U02656](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02656) Where does the crucial point of the object arise?  
[U02657](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02657) Relying on that, by what are the distinctive [qualities] accomplished?  
[U02658](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02658) What is the crucial point of the stages of conceptual mind like?  
[U02659](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02659) How is [this] to be taken up in experience?  
[U02660](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02660) What appearances of signs and measures does it have? [N-117](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-117)  
[U02661](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02661) What are the appearances of the intermediate state?  
[U02662](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02662) By what is the liberation of one's own ordinary mind effected?  
[U02663](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02663) After liberation, how does compassionate responsiveness arise?  
[U02664](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02664) What is the crucial point of that primordial knowing itself?  
[U02665](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02665) What are its manner of abiding and natural state like?  
[U02666](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02666) Is enlightened intent one or different?  
[U02667](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02667) What is the crucial point concerning the all-basis and dharma embodiment, the two?  
[U02668](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02668) What is the crucial point concerning ordinary mind and primordial knowing?  
[U02669](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02669) By what are the clear and turbid aspects of the elements distinguished?  
[U02670](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02670) How are the lamps packed? [N-117](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-117)  
[U02671](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02671) What is the appearance of packing like?  
[U02672](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02672) How many crucial points of the channels are there?  
[U02673](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02673) How many aspects of training ordinary mind are there?  
[U02674](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02674) What causes afflictions to arise?  
[U02675](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02675) Because I do not know these,  
[U02676](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02676) Teacher of great compassionate responsiveness,  
[U02677](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02677) although you have neither verbal expression nor conceptual examination,  
[U02678](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02678) from the space of uncontrived intrinsic nature,  
[U02679](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02679) speak to me through inexpressible speech.’ [N-117](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-117)  
[U02680](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02680) [Source annotation: The first [reply] has seventeen subdivisions.]  
[U02681](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02681) Then the teacher, the vajra-holder,  
[U02682](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02682) from the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature free from conceptual elaborations,  
[U02683](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02683) though not abiding as words, phrases, or names,  
[U02684](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02684) spoke to the lord of the gods in these words.  
[U02685](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02685) From the space in which conceptual thought is exhausted,  
[U02686](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02686) though not proclaimed as words, phrases, or names,  
[U02687](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02687) great naturally arisen primordial knowing itself,  
[U02688](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02688) from intrinsic nature, unmade from the outset,  
[U02689](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02689) arose thus as unarranged phrases: [N-118](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-118)  
[U02690](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02690) ‘The beginning of samsara:  
[U02691](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02691) from the undifferentiated, unmade, self and other,  
[U02692](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02692) an object held as a focus arose.  
[U02693](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02693) From conceptualizing and holding, distinct appearances [arose].  
[U02694](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02694) In the twelve connections of focusing and holding,  
[U02695](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02695) there is the very beginning of samsara. [N-118](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-118)  
[U02696](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02696) This is the manner of delusion through three ignorances.  
[U02697](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02697) The single cause, undifferentiated, is the root:  
[U02698](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02698) not knowing [this] to be one's own identity.  
[U02699](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02699) This, too, is the beginning of samsara.  
[U02700](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02700) From this: one, three, and four hundred—[Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-118](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-118)  
[U02701](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02701) definitely grasped through eight surrounding conditions—  
[U02702](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02702) the application of calculating delusion abiding in the Ground. [N-118](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-118)  
[U02703](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02703) From this, there is the co-emergent [ignorance]. [N-T58](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t58)  
[U02704](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02704) From the ultimate and the relative, apparent and imputed—  
[U02705](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02705) the objects belonging to the two truths—  
[U02706](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02706) engagement in all activities becomes twenty. [N-118](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-118)  
[U02707](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02707) From the particulars of the apparent aspects of those objects,  
[U02708](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02708) [imputing ignorance has] four definite conditions: [N-T58](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t58)  
[U02709](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02709) definitely grasped through cause and dominance,  
[U02710](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02710) the object of focus itself, and simultaneity. [N-118](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-118)  
[U02711](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02711) The portions of delusion, too, are eight and two.  
[U02712](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02712) Because the times of the objects of focus coincide,  
[U02713](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02713) as for the delusion imputed as formations:  
[U02714](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02714) for a hundred gatherings, one agent. [Source variant: ‘Eight also.’] [N-119](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-119)  
[U02715](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02715) Through the completion of all activities, limitless [forms] arise.  
[U02716](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02716) Calculate their times and faculties.  
[U02717](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02717) According to the manner of the delusion of consciousness,  
[U02718](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02718) the objects appearing are eight and six.  
[U02719](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02719) Examination through knowing becomes twenty.  
[U02720](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02720) Through this, calculate the body and limbs. [N-119](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-119)  
[U02721](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02721) Furthermore, for the portion of delusion of name-and-form:  
[U02722](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02722) from naming, four designations, sixty-two—[Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-119](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-119)  
[U02723](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02723) samsara and nirvana, four, together with letters.  
[U02724](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02724) For form, distinguish fine and coarse characteristics;  
[U02725](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02725) establishing and holding: one hundred and five.  
[U02726](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02726) Calculate four grounds that transform all activities. [N-119](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-119)  
[U02727](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02727) For the six wheels of the sense bases, too,  
[U02728](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02728) the crucial points of objects, knowing, and faculties:  
[U02729](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02729) their respective portions of entry are eight, two, and six.  
[U02730](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02730) That is the extent of calculating the objects of activities. [N-120](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-120)  
[U02731](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02731) For the portion of contact, the manner of ordinary mind's delusion:  
[U02732](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02732) causing, appearing, and accompanied by experience—six.  
[U02733](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02733) Activities, agents, and contact-knowing—five.  
[U02734](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02734) Calculate seven transformations of the final conceptual thought. [N-120](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-120)  
[U02735](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02735) The manners of feeling are seven and two.  
[U02736](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02736) From conceptual thought concerning the apparent aspect endowed with mental faculty,  
[U02737](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02737) transformations of mental faculty and ordinary mind become eighty.  
[U02738](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02738) From this, calculate seventy accompanied by conceptual thought.  
[U02739](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02739) From the three and five delusions of craving,  
[U02740](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02740) when enacted, the number is sixty. [Source variant: ‘Accumulation also.’] [N-120](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-120)  
[U02741](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02741) The number of ways of reversing at the limits of the branches  
[U02742](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02742) is two hundred; reverse at the culmination of activities.  
[U02743](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02743) The Grounds of delusion of grasping: thirteen, six. [Numerical grouping unresolved.] [N-120](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-120)  
[U02744](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02744) A hundred can contact forms at the culmination of activities.  
[U02745](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02745) For an object held in delusion, [these] become aspects of appearance.  
[U02746](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02746) Calculate their transformation into agents. [N-120](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-120)  
[U02747](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02747) For the twelve delusions of becoming,  
[U02748](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02748) the undifferentiated portion has sixteen.  
[U02749](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02749) Through this, objects and ordinary mind, with conceptual thought, are bound.  
[U02750](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02750) Examine the four activities individually. [N-120](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-120)  
[U02751](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02751) From the portion of birth: three, two, and five.  
[U02752](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02752) Entering and coming are performed by wind.  
[U02753](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02753) For the times of rising and abiding, there are eight.  
[U02754](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02754) The results of the manner of circling are twelve.  
[U02755](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02755) From aging and death, there are nine conditions of delusion.  
[U02756](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02756) For the conceptual thought of effort and cessation, there are eighteen.  
[U02757](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02757) Entry into an abode: eight, and a portion;  
[U02758](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02758) [there are] dormant tendencies and circling everywhere. [N-121](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-121)  
[U02759](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02759) Thus, from the twelve roots of delusion,  
[U02760](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02760) bodies, faculties, years,  
[U02761](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02761) and the respective elements are distinguished by time.  
[U02762](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02762) Self and other become four.  
[U02763](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02763) These arose at the beginning of samsara.  
[U02764](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02764) For sentient beings' realms, with their many [aspects],  
[U02765](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02765) in order to enter, engagement in all activities to their culmination,  
[U02766](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02766) endowed [with these], is complete and closely taught. [N-121](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-121)  

[U02767](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02767) [Source heading: Second reply.]  
[U02768](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02768) Lord of the gods, listen to this great wonder.  
[U02769](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02769) The final extent of passing beyond sorrow:  
[U02770](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02770) when all conceptual thought of delusion is exhausted,  
[U02771](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02771) and the appearance of the agency of samsara is absent,  
[U02772](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02772) delusion is self-appearance with conceptual thought exhausted.  
[U02773](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02773) To posit transcendence, the realm of sentient beings,  
[U02774](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02774) in its intrinsic nature, too, has this very object:  
[U02775](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02775) not dependent on another, completely established;  
[U02776](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02776) empty of imputed activity and expression;  
[U02777](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02777) not distinguished as possessing an extreme. [N-122](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-122)  
[U02778](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02778) Entering in pairs and pervading everywhere—  
[U02779](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02779) activities, faculties, conceptual minds, and objects—  
[U02780](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02780) the activity of entering is immediate. [N-122](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-122)  

[U02781](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02781) [Source heading: Third reply.]  
[U02782](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02782) Thus, concerning the beginning and end of samsara and nirvana,  
[U02783](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02783) because the buddhas have not become deluded,  
[U02784](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02784) through a noble faculty from the Ground,  
[U02785](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02785) they know self-appearance to be without intrinsic nature.  
[U02786](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02786) Conceptual examination and mental faculty have not strayed outward;  
[U02787](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02787) movement is cut off of itself. [N-123](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-123)  
[U02788](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02788) Furthermore, in the great natural state,  
[U02789](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02789) what is called delusion is pure from the outset;  
[U02790](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02790) it cannot be contrived into nondelusion.  
[U02791](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02791) Phenomena that appear without existing, yet are not seen—  
[U02792](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02792) the conceptual thought that arises through doing ceases of itself.  
[U02793](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02793) There is no appearance as an object of focus through delusion.  
[U02794](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02794) Because the primordial radiance of intrinsic nature has not stirred,  
[U02795](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02795) all phenomena are equally of one taste.  
[U02796](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02796) Free from holding and conceptualizing, there is primordial buddhahood.  
[U02797](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02797) It cannot be contrived or transformed.  
[U02798](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02798) Its crucial point is the object:  
[U02799](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02799) the object, the empty, and awareness's expressiveness are equal.  
[U02800](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02800) This is not known through differentiating conceptual thought. [N-123](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-123)  
[U02801](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02801) Furthermore, the crucial point of not being deluded:  
[U02802](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02802) through knowing one, all are liberated.  
[U02803](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02803) Through knowing that itself, there is reversal.  
[U02804](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02804) With no appearance and no exhaustion,  
[U02805](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02805) conceptual thought of arising, awareness, and abiding is exhausted. [N-123](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-123)  
[U02806](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02806) Not differentiated through doing, it cannot be contrived.  
[U02807](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02807) Because there is no conceptual mind of action and agent,  
[U02808](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02808) with objects and conditions cleared away, there is buddhahood.  
[U02809](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02809) Free from extremes and without cessation,  
[U02810](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02810) one abides in fully complete deep absorption.  
[U02811](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02811) Because it is great, there is nothing above it,  
[U02812](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02812) for intrinsic nature has not stirred. [N-123](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-123)  

[U02813](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02813) [Source heading: Fourth reply.]  
[U02814](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02814) Sentient beings' delusion, by wandering in  
[U02815](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02815) the root dependent connections taught above,  
[U02816](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02816) arises as the foundation of calculating delusion's mechanism.  
[U02817](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02817) From the appearance of the nature of phenomena's own radiance,  
[U02818](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02818) the appearance of conceptualizing and holding captivates the mental faculty.  
[U02819](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02819) The object of delusion, through subtle activity,  
[U02820](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02820) is obscured, and self-appearance becomes impure. [N-124](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-124)  
[U02821](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02821) From this, dormant tendencies themselves,  
[U02822](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02822) subtle, and the portions of particles,  
[U02823](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02823) come to be mere movement and holding.  
[U02824](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02824) From knowing that becomes subtle through subtlety,  
[U02825](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02825) conceptual examination and the apprehended portion become two.  
[U02826](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02826) The appearance of subtle-particle primordial knowing ceases.  
[U02827](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02827) Mere immediate object-knowing arises. [N-124](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-124)  
[U02828](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02828) The twelve phenomena, ignorance and so forth,  
[U02829](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02829) become the twelve elemental classes of beings.  
[U02830](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02830) Their inner knowing, bodies of elements,  
[U02831](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02831) aggregates, elements, and also  
[U02832](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02832) sense bases—the gatherings of activities assemble.  
[U02833](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02833) From immeasurable gatherings of afflictions,  
[U02834](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02834) the assemblages of conceptual thought in beings' bodies are gathered.  
[U02835](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02835) From the many gatherings of dependent connections,  
[U02836](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02836) there are particular discordant forms and so forth. [N-124](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-124)  
[U02837](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02837) The causes generated by the four elements  
[U02838](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02838) are thoroughly grouped by class and number.  
[U02839](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02839) From different, distinct colors,  
[U02840](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02840) they become manifest from the preceding portions.  
[U02841](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02841) Through the gathering of conceptual thoughts from the Ground,  
[U02842](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02842) also through twelve activities of agency,  
[U02843](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02843) the elemental aggregates are supported,  
[U02844](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02844) made to abide, form, and be destroyed.  
[U02845](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02845) From the twelve manners of delusion,  
[U02846](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02846) through maturing into agents,  
[U02847](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02847) beings' karma and merit,  
[U02848](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02848) and pure and impure forms,  
[U02849](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02849) are made to appear in their respective distinctions. [N-124](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-124)  
[U02850](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02850) Furthermore, of the environment and its inhabitants,  
[U02851](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02851) the activities of the elements, too, are performed by wind.  
[U02852](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02852) Wind projects and gathers,  
[U02853](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02853) and makes [them] go, come, and enter.  
[U02854](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02854) Therefore, wind performs all activities.  
[U02855](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02855) Reversal itself also depends on wind;  
[U02856](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02856) distinguish [it] by the culmination of activities of entering and increasing. [N-124](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-124)  

[U02857](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02857) [Source heading: Fifth reply.]  
[U02858](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02858) The primordial knowing of self-awareness abides in the body,  
[U02859](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02859) like oil from a sesame seed.  
[U02860](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02860) The body's luster and splendor  
[U02861](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02861) are pervaded by the moisture of primordial knowing.  
[U02862](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02862) The embodiments that accord with the body,  
[U02863](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02863) and the signs of primordial knowing's light and colors—  
[U02864](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02864) through activities, entry, and embodiments,  
[U02865](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02865) [primordial knowing] pervades together with splendor. [N-125](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-125)  
[U02866](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02866) Through discerning knowing itself and the activities of the lamps,  
[U02867](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02867) the characteristics of ultimate and relative are distinguished.  
[U02868](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02868) The respective crucial points of the spheres that bring maturity,  
[U02869](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02869) the conceptual thought that causes movement and holds coarsely,  
[U02870](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02870) and the four and two portions of primordial knowing's appearance—  
[U02871](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02871) through distinctions of arising and entering activity,  
[U02872](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02872) in the precious palace of the heart (citta), [N-125](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-125)  
[U02873](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02873) a gathering of precious [qualities], with eight corners and doors,  
[U02874](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02874) there are five primordial knowings and five embodiments;  
[U02875](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02875) five winds, and five expressivenesses of knowing.  
[U02876](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02876) All have the intrinsic nature of fivefold groups. [N-125](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-125)  
[U02877](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02877) The crucial point of embodiments is through radiance;  
[U02878](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02878) that of primordial knowing is through its manner of abiding.  
[U02879](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02879) Spheres are accomplished through retaining and stirring.  
[U02880](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02880) Light is spread and gathered. [N-125](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-125)  
[U02881](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02881) The crucial point of wind is binding what is connected.  
[U02882](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02882) The activity of entering is distinguished by its culmination.  
[U02883](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02883) The karmic wind that causes its movement is exhausted,  
[U02884](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02884) and [this] is joined to the arrangement of delusion. [N-125](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-125)  
[U02885](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02885) [Source gloss: From their abode, the heart (citta), through the door of the eyes, primordial knowing actually arises as vajra chains in the object, space.] [N-125](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-125) [N-126](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-126)  
[U02886](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02886) The crucial point of awareness is arising and  
[U02887](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02887) retaining,  
[U02888](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02888) and directing space toward awareness. [N-126](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-126)  
[U02889](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02889) [Source gloss interleaved in U02887–U02889: At this time, drawing the wind is the crucial point.] [N-126](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-126)  
[U02890](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02890) The crucial point of basic space is gathering,  
[U02891](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02891) and joining summoning with the elements' dispersal and spreading.  
[U02892](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02892) The crucial point of appearances is increase and exhaustion;  
[U02893](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02893) the manifold are liberated in their own places. [N-126](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-126)  

[U02894](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02894) [Source heading: Sixth reply.]  
[U02895](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02895) The path of the primordial knowing of awareness:  
[U02896](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02896) from the wheels of one's own channels,  
[U02897](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02897) where the three embodiments abide complete in themselves,  
[U02898](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02898) Roma, Kyangma, and Kundarma—  
[U02899](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02899) through these three ‘mothers’ [ma], the body is held.  
[U02900](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02900) The central [channel] supports the four wheels. [N-127](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-127)  
[U02901](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02901) Because these abide on the right, left, and center,  
[U02902](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02902) through the distinction of relative and ultimate,  
[U02903](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02903) they generate the body and limbs  
[U02904](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02904) and also bring the lamps to maturity.  
[U02905](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02905) Through Roma, tastes enter the wheels:  
[U02906](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02906) wind stirs [them] and projects the spheres.  
[U02907](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02907) Ro [‘taste’], through reliance on the channels,  
[U02908](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02908) expands body and ordinary mind through bliss.  
[U02909](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02909) Ma [‘mother’] gathers the refined parts  
[U02910](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02910) and makes a foundation for the upper and lower abodes. [N-127](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-127)  
[U02911](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02911) Here, retaining and packing are the crucial point.  
[U02912](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02912) On its six branches,  
[U02913](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02913) bloodletting and cauterization obstruct primordial knowing.  
[U02914](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02914) To attain warmth, massage is the crucial point.  
[U02915](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02915) Wind and spheres are to be summoned. [N-127](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-127)  
[U02916](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02916) Through Kyangma, in the generative wheel,  
[U02917](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02917) wind projects and gathers, and spheres enter.  
[U02918](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02918) Rkyang [‘single’] is so called because it has no counterpart;  
[U02919](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02919) it reveals the enlightened intent of the unique.  
[U02920](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02920) Ma [‘mother’] clears away the turbid parts  
[U02921](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02921) and generates the splendor of essence.  
[U02922](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02922) Here, loosening and massage are the crucial point.  
[U02923](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02923) Because its branches do not appear,  
[U02924](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02924) apply spheres to the four junctures of maturation.  
[U02925](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02925) One wishing to gain mastery over food  
[U02926](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02926) packs wind and does not discard saliva.  
[U02927](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02927) Water, too, is to be thoroughly examined through the pledge. [N-127](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-127)  
[U02928](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02928) Through Kundarma, mindfulness  
[U02929](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02929) and the wheel of the summit mature through gathering.  
[U02930](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02930) It reverses karmic wind and retains primordial knowing.  
[U02931](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02931) Kun [‘all’] grants the qualities of the emanation embodiment.  
[U02932](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02932) 'Dar [‘quivering’] [grants] the qualities of the complete enjoyment embodiment.  
[U02933](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02933) Ma [‘mother’] directly reveals the dharma embodiment.  
[U02934](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02934) For its twelve branches,  
[U02935](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02935) loosening and rolling are the crucial point.  
[U02936](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02936) To increase the lifespan, application [of an ointment] is taught.  
[U02937](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02937) Spheres are to be [worked with] through body and ordinary mind. [N-127](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-127)  
[U02938](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02938) From these paths of primordial knowing,  
[U02939](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02939) spheres come to be accompanied by embodiments.  
[U02940](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02940) Wind performs all the activities of going and coming.  
[U02941](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02941) The master of ordinary mind is held by the mental faculty;  
[U02942](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02942) through the path, [it] is held and summoned. [N-127](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-127)  

[U02943](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02943) [Source heading: Seventh reply.]  
[U02944](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02944) The door through which primordial knowing arises:  
[U02945](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02945) the gathering of all refined bodily vital essence  
[U02946](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02946) emerges through the door called cakṣu (‘eye’). [N-128](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-128)  
[U02947](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02947) Holding the two portions, it reveals appearances. [N-128](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-128)  
[U02948](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02948) From the generative channel, arising in connection,  
[U02949](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02949) becoming one at the tip,  
[U02950](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02950) making the faculty arise toward its object—  
[U02951](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02951) one has five branches,  
[U02952](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02952) appearing at the respective sense gates.  
[U02953](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02953) In particular, through its faults and actions,  
[U02954](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02954) from a coil like a buffalo's horn (ba-min), [N-128](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-128)  
[U02955](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02955) what is called a-bras is half white and half black. [N-128](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-128)  
[U02956](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02956) It is aware and holds forms,  
[U02957](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02957) and completely reveals knowing's own expressiveness.  
[U02958](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02958) Here primordial knowing actually arises.  
[U02959](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02959) Through direct perception and its own crucial point,  
[U02960](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02960) in the self-appearing object of the pure nature of phenomena,  
[U02961](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02961) what is accompanied by conceptual thought abides in cessation. [N-128](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-128)  
[U02962](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02962) What brings this about?  
[U02963](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02963) From the arising of the ultimate nature of phenomena,  
[U02964](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02964) there are what are called the four lamps.  
[U02965](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02965) Through maturing, entering, and pervading emptiness,  
[U02966](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02966) they gather the nature of phenomena's appearance as object. [N-129](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-129)  
[U02967](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02967) The water lamp of the far-reaching lasso gathers forms. [N-T64](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t64)  
[U02968](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02968) Wind moves and projects [them].  
[U02969](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02969) Through increase and decrease in coming and going,  
[U02970](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02970) it gathers primordial knowing.  
[U02971](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02971) The lamp of the empty sphere performs all activities. [N-T64](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t64)  
[U02972](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02972) Taking the lead on the path of transcendence,  
[U02973](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02973) through wind it generates luster and splendor;  
[U02974](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02974) transforming [them], it reveals appearances of primordial knowing.  
[U02975](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02975) The lamp of naturally arising discerning knowing  
[U02976](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02976) gathers all phenomena into equal, single taste,  
[U02977](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02977) and holds self-appearance.  
[U02978](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02978) This cuts the connected flow of habitual tendencies.  
[U02979](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02979) Gathering the refined part of basic space,  
[U02980](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02980) and retaining the embodiments of awareness,  
[U02981](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02981) through maturing the three crucial points into vital essence,  
[U02982](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02982) it causes samsara itself to be abandoned. [N-129](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-129)  
[U02983](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02983) Thus, because the lamps arise through the eyes,  
[U02984](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02984) with the crucial points of the buddhas' enlightened intent gathered,  
[U02985](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02985) the objects of self-appearance are held. [N-129](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-129)  

[U02986](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02986) [Source heading: Eighth reply.]  
[U02987](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02987) I will explain the crucial point of the lamps.  
[U02988](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02988) Fortunate son of the gods, listen!  
[U02989](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02989) Through the object, awareness, and wind,  
[U02990](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02990) because the nature of phenomena is taken as the path,  
[U02991](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02991) the nature of phenomena in which primordial knowing is complete—  
[U02992](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02992) this, too, is the crucial point of the lamps.  
[U02993](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02993) The crucial point of the water lamp of the far-reaching lasso is nonmovement. [N-T64](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t64)  
[U02994](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02994) At this time, join [it] with the three gazes.  
[U02995](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02995) The crucial point of the discerning-knowing lamp is training.  
[U02996](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02996) Through three rites, increase [it],  
[U02997](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02997) and through the crucial point of accomplishment, expand its extent.  
[U02998](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02998) The crucial point of the sphere lamp is packing.  
[U02999](alignment/chapter-02.md#u02999) By not separating the channels, thumb, and finger,  
[U03000](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03000) primordial knowing increases. [N-130](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-130)  
[U03001](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03001) The crucial point of basic space is guiding.  
[U03002](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03002) The yogin, in the methods of training with the sun,  
[U03003](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03003) should train thoroughly and guide [this]. [N-131](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-131)  
[U03004](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03004) One should also train with the moon.  
[U03005](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03005) Whoever continually does this  
[U03006](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03006) cuts the flow of delusive appearances.  
[U03007](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03007) The pure light of the five primordial knowings—  
[U03008](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03008) earth, stones, mountains, and crags emptied of their traces—  
[U03009](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03009) in isolated patches and mere portions of a region,  
[U03010](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03010) appears to one skilled in guiding. [N-131](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-131)  
[U03011](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03011) At this time, adjustment through methods is the crucial point.  
[U03012](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03012) The complete nature of phenomena is attained.  
[U03013](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03013) Through familiarity in this way, the power of the nature of phenomena  
[U03014](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03014) [brings] the body's elements to completion in their own places.  
[U03015](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03015) Thus, because the flow of the body's elements is cut,  
[U03016](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03016) without the appearance of contaminated aggregates,  
[U03017](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03017) one attains manifest buddhahood in this. [N-131](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-131)  

[U03018](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03018) [Source heading: Ninth reply.]  
[U03019](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03019) The characteristics of the lamps are like this:  
[U03020](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03020) in general, they reveal appearances,  
[U03021](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03021) join primordial knowing as mother and child,  
[U03022](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03022) and connect the boundary of samsara and nirvana.  
[U03023](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03023) Through their own radiance being clear, within a body of light,  
[U03024](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03024) they gather primordial knowing free from conceptualization.  
[U03025](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03025) Within the appearances of objects accompanied by conceptual thought,  
[U03026](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03026) they hold the foundation of non-conceptual primordial knowing. [N-132](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-132)  
[U03027](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03027) The characteristic of the far-reaching-lasso lamp:  
[U03028](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03028) through pervading it enters; through entering it pervades.  
[U03029](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03029) It abides as the master-agent of the four elements.  
[U03030](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03030) It holds, enters, spreads, and acts. [N-132](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-132)  
[U03031](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03031) The characteristics of discerning knowing:  
[U03032](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03032) entering clear words, it gathers the foundation;  
[U03033](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03033) it burns afflictions, karma, and habitual tendencies;  
[U03034](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03034) it matures and cuts the life-force of samsara and nirvana. [N-132](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-132)  
[U03035](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03035) The characteristics of the empty sphere:  
[U03036](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03036) round, with intrinsic nature entirely clear,  
[U03037](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03037) red and colored, with clear light,  
[U03038](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03038) it spreads with elaboration. [N-132](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-132)  
[U03039](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03039) The characteristics of pure basic space itself:  
[U03040](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03040) blue, complete in itself, with two portions.  
[U03041](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03041) Through methods and discerning knowing being complete,  
[U03042](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03042) from the two paired portions, it holds two embodiments  
[U03043](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03043) and makes appearances spread abundantly.  
[U03044](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03044) These, too, complete one's own knowing.  
[U03045](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03045) They reveal a reflection of the all-pervading lord, the hero.  
[U03046](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03046) They draw vajra chains from the balance (srang). [N-133](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-133)  
[U03047](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03047) Like the form of a complete maṇḍala,  
[U03048](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03048) they reveal the splendor of the primordial knowings,  
[U03049](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03049) and also reveal the deep splendor and luster of light.  
[U03050](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03050) There is aspiration for embodiments and spheres. [N-133](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-133)  
[U03051](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03051) Thus, the embodiment in which primordial knowing is complete—  
[U03052](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03052) whoever sees this attains buddhahood.  
[U03053](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03053) Where the three embodiments are exhausted, intrinsic nature is liberated.  
[U03054](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03054) Crossing the precipice of apprehended object and apprehending subject,  
[U03055](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03055) the tether of samsara and nirvana is cut. [N-133](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-133)  

[U03056](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03056) [Source heading: Tenth reply.]  
[U03057](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03057) Its crucial point of the object is like this:  
[U03058](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03058) in the empty outer object, free from clouds,  
[U03059](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03059) yoga abides in the birds' path through the sky.  
[U03060](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03060) Not separate from this is the nature of phenomena. [N-134](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-134)  
[U03061](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03061) In general, through their individual characteristics,  
[U03062](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03062) objects are distinct; completion in one is granted. [N-134](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-134)  
[U03063](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03063) Through primordial knowing gathering, ordinary mind's flow stops.  
[U03064](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03064) Through the elements gathering, the body is complete.  
[U03065](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03065) From appearances of great distinction,  
[U03066](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03066) [this] abides as the object of maturing, entering, and moving.  
[U03067](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03067) Because the limit of conceptual thought is exhausted, one is liberated from samsara.  
[U03068](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03068) The far-reaching lasso's objects are forms and so forth.  
[U03069](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03069) It gathers, holds, and projects [them].  
[U03070](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03070) Through the crucial point, bind and place [them],  
[U03071](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03071) transform entities and cut the root;  
[U03072](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03072) draw [them] forth, and train with form. [N-134](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-134)  
[U03073](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03073) Discerning knowing's objects are words and meaning.  
[U03074](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03074) Because words and meaning are intended to be connected,  
[U03075](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03075) there is knowing through indication, and through expression.  
[U03076](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03076) Indication: examples and symbolic indications;  
[U03077](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03077) indication through words is expressed last.  
[U03078](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03078) Knowing through expression: conventions,  
[U03079](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03079) pointing out, guiding,  
[U03080](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03080) decisive resolution, and distinguishing. [N-134](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-134)  
[U03081](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03081) The sphere's object is color:  
[U03082](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03082) five, six, three, two, and six.  
[U03083](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03083) Clear, warm, cool,  
[U03084](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03084) light, and moving—it holds these identities.  
[U03085](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03085) These are the elements of the ultimate nature of phenomena. [N-134](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-134)  
[U03086](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03086) Basic space's object is empty,  
[U03087](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03087) clear, free from obscuration, and emitting splendor;  
[U03088](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03088) pervading and vast, gathered into one.  
[U03089](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03089) In space whose fine particles have cleared,  
[U03090](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03090) the pure nature of phenomena is complete in evenness.  
[U03091](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03091) Clear, it is the radiance of the five primordial knowings,  
[U03092](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03092) the very body of pure clear light. [N-134](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-134)  

[U03093](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03093) [Source heading: Eleventh reply.]  
[U03094](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03094) The supports of the lamps of pure primordial knowing:  
[U03095](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03095) in general, through support and what relies upon it,  
[U03096](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03096) the seeds of samsara and nirvana increase.  
[U03097](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03097) The far-reaching lasso's support is appearance.  
[U03098](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03098) Through the distinction of pure and impure,  
[U03099](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03099) it relies on karma and accumulations,  
[U03100](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03100) and on embodiments and primordial knowing. [N-135](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-135)  
[U03101](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03101) Naturally arising discerning knowing relies on  
[U03102](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03102) words, conceptual thought about is and is not,  
[U03103](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03103) tenet systems, viewing and cultivation,  
[U03104](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03104) and the nature of phenomena.  
[U03105](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03105) The empty sphere relies on  
[U03106](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03106) consciousness itself, the intervening space,  
[U03107](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03107) channels, wind, and spheres,  
[U03108](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03108) and primordial knowing.  
[U03109](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03109) Perfectly pure basic space relies on  
[U03110](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03110) the space between the eyebrows, space,  
[U03111](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03111) light and spheres,  
[U03112](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03112) and manifold embodiments. [N-135](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-135)  
[U03113](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03113) Furthermore, the distinctive supports and objects:  
[U03114](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03114) through three, they rely on the distinction of gathered agency. [N-135](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-135)  
[U03115](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03115) By relying on the far-reaching lasso itself,  
[U03116](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03116) free from faults, having abandoned action and agency,  
[U03117](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03117) one engages in the mother nature of phenomena.  
[U03118](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03118) Through purifying karma and habitual tendencies,  
[U03119](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03119) relying on the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena,  
[U03120](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03120) one empties samsara from its depths,  
[U03121](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03121) and can touch the boundary of passing beyond sorrow.  
[U03122](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03122) Through the nature of ordinary mind in which conceptual thought and examination are exhausted,  
[U03123](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03123) samsara is cast far away.  
[U03124](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03124) Having seen the embodiment of pure primordial knowing,  
[U03125](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03125) the point of aim of samsara and nirvana, the two, is destroyed. [N-135](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-135)  
[U03126](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03126) Whoever sees a beautiful flower—  
[U03127](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03127) through this, the nature of phenomena is led into an irrigation channel.  
[U03128](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03128) Having placed awareness on the balance (srang),  
[U03129](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03129) one is brought to where phenomena are exhausted. [N-135](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-135)  
[U03130](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03130) A person who becomes familiar with basic space,  
[U03131](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03131) having emptied delusive appearances,  
[U03132](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03132) through seeing the object of the pure nature of phenomena,  
[U03133](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03133) emerges from the very source of objects of attachment. [N-135](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-135)  

[U03134](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03134) [Source heading: Twelfth reply.]  
[U03135](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03135) The crucial point of the stages of conceptual mind is like this:  
[U03136](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03136) through distinctions in the vessel of the elements,  
[U03137](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03137) through distinctions in the elements' junctures of maturation,  
[U03138](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03138) and through differences in beings' conceptual minds,  
[U03139](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03139) families and faculties differ.  
[U03140](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03140) Furthermore, through the magical displays of the elements:  
[U03141](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03141) water's earth, its activity purified,  
[U03142](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03142) at first can spread;  
[U03143](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03143) finally it does not forget the teaching it has grasped.  
[U03144](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03144) Water's fire grasps readily and forgets.  
[U03145](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03145) Water's wind transforms conceptual mind,  
[U03146](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03146) entering everything, without holding a ground. [N-136](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-136)  
[U03147](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03147) [Source query: Water's wind occurs twice; examine this.] [N-136](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-136)  
[U03148](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03148) Water's wind [brings] spaciousness,  
[U03149](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03149) and brings object and knowing into contact.  
[U03150](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03150) Water's water has a strong inclination,  
[U03151](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03151) with little perception, and grasps. [N-136](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-136)  
[U03152](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03152) From the pure elements of earth:  
[U03153](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03153) earth's water increases words  
[U03154](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03154) and multiplies manifold conceptual thoughts.  
[U03155](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03155) Earth's fire [makes] the faculties supremely pure,  
[U03156](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03156) revealing them as clear and endowed with gentleness.  
[U03157](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03157) Earth's wind [brings] stupefaction  
[U03158](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03158) and interruptions, alternating at intervals.  
[U03159](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03159) Earth's earth extensively holds joints and limbs. [N-136](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-136)  
[U03160](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03160) Fire's earth expands the faculties.  
[U03161](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03161) [Fire's] water reveals entering and pervading.  
[U03162](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03162) [Fire's] wind makes the faculties exceedingly sharp.  
[U03163](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03163) Fire's fire grasps readily and forgets.  
[U03164](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03164) Wind's earth has great engagement with meaning.  
[U03165](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03165) [Wind's] water makes the object of deep absorption clear.  
[U03166](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03166) [Wind's] fire enables enjoyment of words.  
[U03167](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03167) Wind's wind holds conceptual thought and examination. [N-136](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-136)  
[U03168](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03168) When these elements are impure,  
[U03169](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03169) they reveal engagement in all mistaken activities.  
[U03170](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03170) Appearances distract, and knowing changes.  
[U03171](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03171) Conceptual thought is coarse and arouses nonvirtue.  
[U03172](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03172) Even the ordinary mind [oriented] toward the teaching is abandoned. [N-137](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-137)  
[U03173](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03173) Thus, through the vessel of the elements being pure,  
[U03174](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03174) the many paths of liberation are revealed,  
[U03175](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03175) and the three embodiments are authentically established.  
[U03176](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03176) Then, having entered the mother's womb,  
[U03177](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03177) through the calculation of years, months, and days,  
[U03178](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03178) and the respective elements' own times,  
[U03179](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03179) having definitely grasped the measures of time,  
[U03180](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03180) through restoring each respective element,  
[U03181](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03181) the mantra practitioner should diligently rely on dependent connections.  
[U03182](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03182) Through the rite of generating the elements,  
[U03183](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03183) faculties are sharp, and primordial knowing is clear.  
[U03184](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03184) Form and color are complete.  
[U03185](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03185) One is loving toward beings and endowed with enjoyments,  
[U03186](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03186) maturing into one who abandons misdeeds and acts virtuously. [N-137](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-137)  
[U03187](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03187) Thus, the elements protect the vital essence.  
[U03188](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03188) Through differences in the vessel's color,  
[U03189](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03189) just as the color of glass (man-shel), too, changes,  
[U03190](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03190) the stainless nature of ordinary mind, too,  
[U03191](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03191) is like that through the vessel of the elements. [N-137](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-137)  

[U03192](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03192) [Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]  
[U03193](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03193) This is the crucial point of taking [the teaching] up in experience:  
[U03194](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03194) the support of focus and the abiding of reflection.  
[U03195](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03195) The objects of focus are form, sound, smell, taste, and touch.  
[U03196](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03196) Train in the domains of the five desires.  
[U03197](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03197) For form: colors and shapes,  
[U03198](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03198) different crafts and illusory expressiveness,  
[U03199](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03199) and manifold optical illusions. [N-138](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-138)  
[U03200](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03200) For sound: lute and booming pot,  
[U03201](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03201) many-stringed instrument, earthenware drum, and flute,  
[U03202](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03202) and sounds such as ‘phek’ and ‘har.’ [N-138](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-138)  
[U03203](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03203) For smell: combine fragrant substances,  
[U03204](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03204) and act through profound, agreeable dependent connections.  
[U03205](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03205) For taste: jaggery endowed with six [unspecified aspects],  
[U03206](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03206) and various mixtures of grape wine are transformed.  
[U03207](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03207) Join the branches of dependent connections. [N-138](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-138)  
[U03208](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03208) For touch, the skilled person sits on a seat.  
[U03209](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03209) Through crossed bows, a combination of elements,  
[U03210](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03210) and certain leaves of trees,  
[U03211](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03211) through mere touch, in the nature of phenomena,  
[U03212](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03212) ordinary mind abides without separation. [N-138](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-138)  
[U03213](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03213) For ordinary mind: holding and abiding,  
[U03214](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03214) cutting the root and pursuing through entities,  
[U03215](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03215) searching between object and knowing,  
[U03216](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03216) and casting into distraction. [N-138](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-138)  

[U03217](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03217) [Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]  
[U03218](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03218) From taking [this] up in experience in this way,  
[U03219](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03219) the signs and measures are as follows.  
[U03220](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03220) The body appears light, with unimpeded penetration.  
[U03221](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03221) It does not touch the earth and is unobstructed.  
[U03222](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03222) Speech's capacity is complete; its store of words is exhausted.  
[U03223](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03223) Ordinary mind is capable of transference.  
[U03224](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03224) Eye, ear, nose, tongue,  
[U03225](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03225) and all bodily faculties are exhausted.  
[U03226](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03226) Because there is no intrinsic nature, their capacities are exhausted. [N-139](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-139)  

[U03227](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03227) [Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]  
[U03228](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03228) The appearances of the intermediate state:  
[U03229](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03229) the aggregates of abiding in intrinsic nature,  
[U03230](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03230) the elements, and afflictions appear as objects. [N-140](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-140)  
[U03231](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03231) At death there are mistiness and wavering,  
[U03232](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03232) and knowing does not remain in one [place].  
[U03233](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03233) In the intermediate state of the nature of phenomena: embodiments,  
[U03234](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03234) primordial knowing, light, and colors.  
[U03235](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03235) Spheres spread, and elemental appearances are pure.  
[U03236](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03236) Whatever appears is the immeasurable palace of the nature of phenomena. [N-140](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-140)  
[U03237](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03237) The appearances of becoming are fitful,  
[U03238](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03238) fleeting, like dream appearances.  
[U03239](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03239) Appearing true, though false, is the body of mental faculty.  
[U03240](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03240) White, gleg [unresolved], and so forth—the lights of the six classes—  
[U03241](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03241) are appearances of their respective objects of focus. [N-140](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-140)  

[U03242](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03242) [Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]  
[U03243](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03243) What is called the liberation of one's own ordinary mind  
[U03244](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03244) is not a going from one [place] to another.  
[U03245](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03245) Ordinary mind is not released by anyone.  
[U03246](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03246) Complete in the Ground, there is no going or coming.  
[U03247](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03247) It is not found by examination; there is no reason.  
[U03248](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03248) Without Ground or root, it abides empty.  
[U03249](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03249) That ordinary mind, abiding in its own clarity,  
[U03250](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03250) liberated through the crucial point, is not partial to any side. [N-141](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-141)  
[U03251](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03251) Liberated through time, there is no basis of dependence.  
[U03252](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03252) Liberated without effort, there is no effortful accomplishment.  
[U03253](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03253) Liberated through confidence, there is no endeavor or effort.  
[U03254](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03254) This is explained as liberation through confidence.  
[U03255](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03255) Liberated from the outset, there is nothing to repeat.  
[U03256](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03256) Liberated by itself, there is no antidote.  
[U03257](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03257) Liberated nakedly, it vanishes where it is seen.  
[U03258](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03258) Completely liberated, it is effortless. [N-142](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-142)  

[U03259](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03259) [Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]  
[U03260](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03260) In the nature of ordinary mind liberated in this way,  
[U03261](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03261) it is not that compassionate responsiveness is absent.  
[U03262](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03262) Through primordial knowing that abides in the Ground,  
[U03263](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03263) intrinsic nature, by its own momentum, serves as a support for holding. [N-143](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-143)  
[U03264](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03264) Through primordial knowing that holds characteristics,  
[U03265](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03265) purity is brought to maturity through conditions.  
[U03266](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03266) Through primordial knowing of knowing and what is to be known,  
[U03267](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03267) accomplishments are granted to those with aspiration and devotion.  
[U03268](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03268) Furthermore, when the nature of ordinary mind matures,  
[U03269](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03269) the Ground abides in the dharma embodiment.  
[U03270](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03270) The stains of what is pure from the outset are exhausted. [N-143](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-143)  
[U03271](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03271) Essence is undeluded from the outset.  
[U03272](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03272) Making appearances arise without cessation is spontaneous presence.  
[U03273](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03273) Intrinsic nature is not determined as ‘like this.’  
[U03274](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03274) Because it pervades all, it is compassionate responsiveness.  
[U03275](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03275) Samsara and passing beyond are not cut off.  
[U03276](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03276) The primordial knowing of the complete enjoyment embodiment holds characteristics.  
[U03277](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03277) Through objects and entities being pure, there is the mirror-like [primordial knowing],  
[U03278](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03278) the clear reflection of samsara and nirvana.  
[U03279](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03279) There is evenness without partiality.  
[U03280](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03280) Relying on the objects of the faculties, there is discriminating [primordial knowing]. [N-143](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-143) [N-T80](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t80)  
[U03281](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03281) Not through effort, activity is accomplished.  
[U03282](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03282) Because there is no difference, there is the basic space of phenomena. [N-143](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-143) [N-T80](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t80)  
[U03283](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03283) In the emanation embodiment, there is what is to be known.  
[U03284](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03284) Through knowing how [things are], an entity's  
[U03285](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03285) natural state is known for one's own purpose.  
[U03286](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03286) Through knowing the multiplicity, concerning those to be trained,  
[U03287](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03287) one knows how their intentions abide.  
[U03288](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03288) Thus, from the ways compassionate responsiveness arises,  
[U03289](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03289) for the different beings to be trained,  
[U03290](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03290) there are that many embodiments that train them. [N-143](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-143)  

[U03291](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03291) [Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]  
[U03292](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03292) In teaching that very crucial point of primordial knowing:  
[U03293](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03293) first, its manner of pervading the Ground;  
[U03294](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03294) in between, its completion on the path;  
[U03295](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03295) finally, it pervades the bodies of beings.  
[U03296](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03296) At the time of the body, it is like this:  
[U03297](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03297) in the aggregates of gathered elements,  
[U03298](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03298) within the precious palace of the heart (citta),  
[U03299](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03299) the portion of essence, pure from the outset,  
[U03300](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03300) is the radiance of embodiment, empty and clear united.  
[U03301](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03301) Faces and hands complete: the manner of the vase embodiment.  
[U03302](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03302) It abides as an orb of spontaneously present light.  
[U03303](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03303) Compassionate responsiveness is clear in distinct colors.  
[U03304](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03304) Roma, Kyangma, and Kundarma;  
[U03305](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03305) the Kati crystal tube channel—  
[U03306](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03306) the mirror-like, evenness, and discriminating,  
[U03307](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03307) activity-accomplishing and basic-space-of-phenomena primordial knowings,  
[U03308](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03308) with moving and nonmoving spheres,  
[U03309](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03309) abide as supports of completion in their own channels. [N-144](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-144)  
[U03310](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03310) From the two eyes, where the refined parts gather,  
[U03311](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03311) through the distinction of methods and discerning knowing,  
[U03312](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03312) knowing how [things are] matures as the sphere [lamp] and  
[U03313](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03313) the lamp of pure basic space.  
[U03314](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03314) Knowing the multiplicity matures as the far-reaching-lasso [lamp] and  
[U03315](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03315) the discerning-knowing lamp.  
[U03316](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03316) Also concerning the karma of beings,  
[U03317](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03317) whatever the distinctions of their faculties,  
[U03318](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03318) through training in this, they become known. [N-144](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-144)  

[U03319](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03319) [Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]  
[U03320](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03320) The manner of abiding and the natural state have two aspects:  
[U03321](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03321) abiding in the initial Ground,  
[U03322](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03322) and abiding in the object of delusion.  
[U03323](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03323) The initial [abiding] is as essence, intrinsic nature,  
[U03324](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03324) and compassionate responsiveness—the three.  
[U03325](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03325) Furthermore, because essence abides as embodiment,  
[U03326](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03326) into portions of dharma, complete enjoyment, and emanation,  
[U03327](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03327) it is not divided: the manner of no division.  
[U03328](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03328) Even in what is accomplished from its own condition,  
[U03329](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03329) the embodiment's color and so forth are not objects of conceptual mind. [N-145](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-145)  
[U03330](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03330) Intrinsic nature makes appearances arise in the manner of light:  
[U03331](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03331) white, red, yellow, green, and blue.  
[U03332](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03332) Not as characteristics with delineations,  
[U03333](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03333) without delineation, it is accomplished from the condition of what is to be known. [N-145](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-145)  
[U03334](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03334) From compassionate responsiveness arising in manifold ways,  
[U03335](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03335) because it is not determined as one ‘like this,’  
[U03336](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03336) and manifold [appearances] arise, it is called the Ground.  
[U03337](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03337) In the object of delusion, leave [it] of itself,  
[U03338](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03338) and seal leaving just as it is with self-liberation. [N-145](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-145)  

[U03339](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03339) [Source heading: Twentieth reply.]  
[U03340](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03340) Enlightened intent has not moved from one.  
[U03341](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03341) Because appearance is clear right upon appearance,  
[U03342](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03342) as with a face and a mirror,  
[U03343](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03343) liberation—that and that itself are liberated. [N-146](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-146)  
[U03344](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03344) Because moving knowing's object is empty,  
[U03345](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03345) seeing, knowing, and awareness are not different.  
[U03346](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03346) That itself is liberated right where it is.  
[U03347](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03347) Knowing one liberates all.  
[U03348](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03348) The numerical limits of two and three are exhausted.  
[U03349](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03349) No object of conceptualizing and holding appears.  
[U03350](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03350) Movement and holding are empty from the outset.  
[U03351](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03351) Because empty enlightened intent is clear and complete,  
[U03352](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03352) the numerical limits of the many are exhausted. [N-146](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-146)  

[U03353](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03353) [Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]  
[U03354](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03354) The crucial point of the all-basis and the dharma embodiment:  
[U03355](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03355) first, the all-basis will be explained.  
[U03356](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03356) It is the basis of all phenomena and nonphenomena.  
[U03357](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03357) Its essence, word explanation, and subdivisions are explained.  
[U03358](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03358) Its essence is the vessel of habitual tendencies.  
[U03359](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03359) Its subdivisions are object, application,  
[U03360](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03360) the manifold, and the primordial Ground. [N-147](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-147)  
[U03361](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03361) In the word explanation, ‘all’ means gathering;  
[U03362](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03362) ‘basis’ means assembling and accumulating.  
[U03363](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03363) The dharma embodiment is free from conceptual elaborations.  
[U03364](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03364) There are no characteristics of arbitrary holding.  
[U03365](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03365) Its essence is empty and clear, indivisible.  
[U03366](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03366) Its subdivisions: dharma, complete enjoyment, and emanation embodiments.  
[U03367](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03367) Through this threefold division, distinctions apply.  
[U03368](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03368) In the word explanation, ‘dharma’ is the authentic path;  
[U03369](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03369) ‘embodiment’ is what is accomplished from it.  
[U03370](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03370) Thus, through the crucial point of distinguishing,  
[U03371](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03371) time, method, and example,  
[U03372](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03372) and the measure of distinction, there is the result. [N-147](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-147)  

[U03373](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03373) [Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]  
[U03374](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03374) The crucial point of ordinary mind and primordial knowing:  
[U03375](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03375) what is called ordinary mind concerns delusive conceptual thought.  
[U03376](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03376) Its essence enters and pervades;  
[U03377](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03377) it is the Ground of mindfulness and reflection.  
[U03378](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03378) Its subdivisions are pure and impure.  
[U03379](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03379) Its word explanation: thought arises as what is recalled.  
[U03380](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03380) Furthermore, what one thinks about, and that by which one thinks,  
[U03381](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03381) what thinks, and why one thinks. [N-148](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-148)  
[U03382](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03382) Primordial knowing is not conceptual thought.  
[U03383](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03383) Its essence is self-clear: the core meaning.  
[U03384](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03384) Its subdivisions are held to be three.  
[U03385](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03385) Its word explanation means abiding from the outset.  
[U03386](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03386) Through knowing in this way, it is primordial knowing.  
[U03387](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03387) The meaning of distinguishing these is taught:  
[U03388](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03388) time, method, example, and measure;  
[U03389](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03389) the results of their respective enlightened intents;  
[U03390](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03390) and the characteristics of realizing primordial knowing.  
[U03391](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03391) Through body, speech, and ordinary mind, too,  
[U03392](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03392) signs, measures, and entry;  
[U03393](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03393) knowing the changes in characteristics;  
[U03394](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03394) and the distinction of complete evenness. [N-148](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-148)  

[U03395](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03395) [Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]  
[U03396](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03396) One wishing to distinguish the refined and turbid parts of the elements:  
[U03397](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03397) through the distinction of ultimate and relative,  
[U03398](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03398) the elements, too, are included in two.  
[U03399](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03399) Their maturation and activities are likewise twofold.  
[U03400](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03400) Earth's turbid part is coarse.  
[U03401](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03401) Its maturation is exceedingly fine particles.  
[U03402](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03402) Its activity is flesh and its illnesses.  
[U03403](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03403) The refined part completes the bodily constituents,  
[U03404](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03404) increases ultimate awakening ordinary mind, [N-149](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-149) [N-T82](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-t82)  
[U03405](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03405) and matures as the far-reaching-lasso lamp.  
[U03406](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03406) Through familiarity with the crucial point, too, it is accomplished.  
[U03407](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03407) Water's turbid part moistens and gathers.  
[U03408](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03408) Its activity is blood and its illnesses.  
[U03409](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03409) Through maturation it gathers collections of water particles.  
[U03410](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03410) The refined part overcomes aging and death;  
[U03411](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03411) its activity stabilizes the nature of phenomena,  
[U03412](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03412) and it matures as the discerning-knowing lamp.  
[U03413](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03413) Through the crucial point, the yoga of food is accomplished.  
[U03414](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03414) Fire's turbid part burns and spreads.  
[U03415](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03415) Through maturation, there are sheep and ox particles. [N-149](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-149)  
[U03416](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03416) Its activity is warmth and its illnesses.  
[U03417](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03417) The refined part completes speech and body,  
[U03418](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03418) and matures as the sphere lamp.  
[U03419](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03419) Through cultivating the meditative stabilization of sleep,  
[U03420](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03420) the crucial point causes dreams to cease.  
[U03421](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03421) Wind's turbid part separates the refined parts and moves [them].
