[U03422](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03422) Through maturation, sun particles become complete. [N-149](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-149)  
[U03423](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03423) Its activity is breath and its illnesses.  
[U03424](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03424) The refined part gives ordinary mind ease and capacity.  
[U03425](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03425) Its activity makes the nature of phenomena clear  
[U03426](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03426) and distinguishes varieties of differentiating conceptualization.  
[U03427](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03427) Through maturation, from the lamp of basic space,  
[U03428](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03428) the crucial point uproots habitual tendencies.  
[U03429](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03429) Through time, method, crucial point, and word,  
[U03430](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03430) entering its intrinsic nature,  
[U03431](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03431) the pith instructions on the elements' crucial points are complete. [N-149](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-149)  

[U03432](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03432) [Source heading: Twenty-fourth reply.]  
[U03433](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03433) Packing the lamps is like this:  
[U03434](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03434) one with faith, free of a mental faculty [turned toward] samsara,  
[U03435](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03435) should offer to the guru and make a torma.  
[U03436](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03436) Having abandoned all connections with samsara,  
[U03437](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03437) in a secluded place or a charnel ground,  
[U03438](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03438) abandon companions and remain alone.  
[U03439](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03439) Not relying on the three [unspecified supports],  
[U03440](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03440) with one's own thumb and forefinger—[Editorial note: Do not infer or perform eye pressure from this draft.] [N-150](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-150)  
[U03441](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03441) one packs the lamp of the empty sphere.  
[U03442](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03442) Whatever arises from that packing  
[U03443](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03443) is the vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena.  
[U03444](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03444) From this, the experience of increased primordial knowing,  
[U03445](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03445) through colors and shapes,  
[U03446](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03446) clearly pervades both outer and inner.  
[U03447](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03447) It definitely increases further and further.  
[U03448](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03448) At this time, the signs of bliss in body and speech  
[U03449](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03449) are joined to the measures and stages of appearances.  
[U03450](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03450) From the appearance in which primordial knowing is complete,  
[U03451](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03451) and the appearance of the pure nature of phenomena,  
[U03452](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03452) when the three appearances are complete, there is exhaustion.  
[U03453](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03453) This is called the result. [N-150](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-150)  

[U03454](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03454) [Source heading: Twenty-fifth reply.]  
[U03455](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03455) The appearances of packing in this way:  
[U03456](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03456) first, pure red,  
[U03457](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03457) complete in power, round, and fully self-clear.  
[U03458](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03458) From two fingerbreadths, it becomes three. [N-151](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-151)  
[U03459](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03459) Then, through packing again and again,  
[U03460](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03460) the colors of the five primordial knowings—  
[U03461](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03461) white, red, green, yellow, and blue—  
[U03462](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03462) appear as the object; this involves effort.  
[U03463](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03463) Then, also on reaching freedom from effort,  
[U03464](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03464) through the portion of wind, they go and come.  
[U03465](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03465) From merely the form of a mirror,  
[U03466](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03466) they appear in portions of two and three. [N-151](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-151)  
[U03467](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03467) Through becoming continually familiar with this,  
[U03468](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03468) also through the five-colored light,  
[U03469](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03469) isolated patches appear, the size of a blanket;  
[U03470](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03470) the extent of seeing is the size of a region. [N-151](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-151)  
[U03471](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03471) Having found the measure of familiarity with this,  
[U03472](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03472) within the spheres, [appearances] include embodiments.  
[U03473](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03473) For a time they abide as half-embodiments.  
[U03474](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03474) Then, five and five are joined in pairs,  
[U03475](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03475) connected by exceedingly clear spheres.  
[U03476](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03476) There are appearances of father and mother embodiments.  
[U03477](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03477) Delusive appearances exhausted, there are fields.  
[U03478](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03478) At this time, the measures of body, speech, and mental faculty—  
[U03479](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03479) at the time of seeing the respective appearances,  
[U03480](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03480) through maturation in oneself, the signs are complete.  
[U03481](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03481) The measures of exhaustion appear manifestly.  
[U03482](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03482) Through greater or lesser diligence in this,  
[U03483](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03483) in days, months, and years,  
[U03484](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03484) accomplishment definitely occurs at each respective time.  
[U03485](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03485) Through times that do not change,  
[U03486](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03486) one reaches the respective measures. [N-151](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-151)  

[U03487](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03487) [Source heading: Twenty-sixth reply.]  
[U03488](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03488) The crucial points of the channels number two hundred.  
[U03489](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03489) Thoroughly condensing their crucial points:  
[U03490](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03490) one hundred and fifteen perform all activities.  
[U03491](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03491) The crucial point of the channels is thorough transformation.  
[U03492](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03492) Sixty-eight join with the nature of phenomena.  
[U03493](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03493) The channels of supremely great bliss,  
[U03494](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03494) through the Ground, reveal the appearances of beings. [Source variant: ‘Four also occurs.’] [N-152](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-152)  
[U03495](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03495) The place of the Ground and the path of going;  
[U03496](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03496) the door of maturation and the activities of agents;  
[U03497](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03497) time and its measures of appearance.  
[U03498](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03498) The pith instructions on their crucial points:  
[U03499](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03499) rely on abiding, coming, and the path of going.  
[U03500](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03500) Join the ground of maturation and the sphere. [N-152](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-152)  
[U03501](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03501) Whoever acts in this way—  
[U03502](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03502) differentiating conceptualization stops, and primordial knowing arises.  
[U03503](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03503) Grasp time through the measure of wind.  
[U03504](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03504) Through the number of movements, primordial knowing abides.  
[U03505](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03505) The skilled person should know and retain [this].  
[U03506](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03506) Through the numerical measure of wind's activities,  
[U03507](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03507) in freedom from error, [these] reveal the nature of phenomena  
[U03508](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03508) and the distinctions of impure activities.  
[U03509](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03509) The measures of appearance, too, are body, speech, and mental faculty,  
[U03510](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03510) because their respective times have arrived. [N-153](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-153)  

[U03511](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03511) [Source heading: Twenty-seventh reply.]  
[U03512](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03512) The training of ordinary mind is like this:  
[U03513](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03513) outer objects and the inner body;  
[U03514](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03514) between them, restraining body and ordinary mind;  
[U03515](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03515) acting through the activities of speech;  
[U03516](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03516) supremely, training ordinary mind with ordinary mind.  
[U03517](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03517) For objects, reflect on existence and nonexistence,  
[U03518](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03518) and the crucial point of going and coming.  
[U03519](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03519) For the body, through names and exceedingly fine particles,  
[U03520](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03520) analyze [it] into many distinctions.  
[U03521](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03521) Examine the interval in which body and ordinary mind are joined.  
[U03522](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03522) Through the particulars of verbal expression,  
[U03523](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03523) the respective winds, going and coming,  
[U03524](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03524) are accomplished through mixing the nonduality of times. [N-154](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-154)  
[U03525](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03525) Supremely, ordinary mind's going, coming, and abiding—  
[U03526](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03526) train through the absence of examination of their reasons. [N-154](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-154)  
[U03527](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03527) Also grasp the measure of proficiency in this way.  
[U03528](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03528) In this way, its characteristics are complete.  

[U03529](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03529) [Source heading: Twenty-eighth reply.]  
[U03530](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03530) Afflictions arise from their own conditions:  
[U03531](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03531) as when one fears one's own shadow,  
[U03532](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03532) one comes to see one's own intrinsic nature.  
[U03533](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03533) Also, from the numerical measures of wind,  
[U03534](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03534) one knows through the number one hundred and twenty.  
[U03535](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03535) Some rely on objects,  
[U03536](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03536) from which distinctions of true and false arise.  
[U03537](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03537) Some, from having no object of focus,  
[U03538](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03538) are to be known through mere neutrality.  
[U03539](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03539) Some desire samsara and nirvana as well;  
[U03540](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03540) from the two truths, two ordinary minds become deluded. [N-155](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-155)  
[U03541](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03541) From this, appearing conditions, afflictions, and so forth.  
[U03542](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03542) Furthermore, what is to be closely explained:  
[U03543](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03543) the intrinsic nature of delusive appearance  
[U03544](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03544) was not previously seen to be primordial knowing.  
[U03545](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03545) Because there is no Ground of delusion accompanied by conceptual thought,  
[U03546](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03546) the all-basis is realized as the great dharma embodiment.  
[U03547](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03547) The flow of delusion's own conceptual thought is cut.  
[U03548](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03548) Thus, undeluded appearance  
[U03549](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03549) arises from the crucial point of liberation from the outset. [N-155](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-155)  
[U03550](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03550) The aggregates of beings are the buddhas' embodiments:  
[U03551](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03551) who has not known them to be so from the outset?  
[U03552](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03552) As for the aggregates having the identity of the five buddhas,  
[U03553](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03553) [this] arises from the accomplishment of letters abiding in the channels.  
[U03554](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03554) From the bubble-like eyes, primordial knowing—  
[U03555](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03555) who has not seen it to be self-appearance?  
[U03556](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03556) From this, delusive appearance  
[U03557](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03557) arises from the crucial point of the lamps. [N-156](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-156)  
[U03558](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03558) Furthermore, because bodily conduct itself  
[U03559](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03559) is liberated as the three embodiments,  
[U03560](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03560) all actions and agents are the nature of phenomena.  
[U03561](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03561) Whatever is spoken through speech  
[U03562](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03562) is the secret recitation of Secret Mantra,  
[U03563](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03563) arising from the crucial point of the letters' magical displays.  
[U03564](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03564) All the conceptual thoughts of beings' ordinary minds—  
[U03565](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03565) why are they not known to be meditative stabilization?  
[U03566](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03566) Because one is liberated through realizing one's own ordinary mind,  
[U03567](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03567) this arises from the crucial point of the elements.  
[U03568](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03568) Thus, in the nature of phenomena of everything,  
[U03569](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03569) there is nothing whatsoever to be trained in,  
[U03570](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03570) and not the slightest thing to be placed.  
[U03571](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03571) It is the nature of phenomena with nothing above it.  
[U03572](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03572) From actions and agents, expression and reflection,  
[U03573](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03573) examine the crucial point of liberating samsara and nirvana.  
[U03574](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03574) There is not even one who is not a buddha.  
[U03575](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03575) The samsara of the three realms is emptied from its depths. [N-156](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-156)  
[U03576](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03576) Furthermore, all sentient beings of the three realms,  
[U03577](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03577) because they do not go beyond body, speech, and mental faculty,  
[U03578](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03578) need not seek the three embodiments elsewhere.  
[U03579](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03579) Even if sought, there is no place where they are found.  
[U03580](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03580) From what has not arisen and has no arising,  
[U03581](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03581) seeing through looking is greatly wondrous.  
[U03582](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03582) In the play of great wonder,  
[U03583](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03583) there is no division into buddhas and sentient beings.  
[U03584](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03584) Like clouds in space,  
[U03585](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03585) naturally arising, complete in themselves, they subside.  
[U03586](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03586) Through the distinction of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena,  
[U03587](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03587) because faculties are neither sharp nor dull,  
[U03588](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03588) all sentient beings do not abide  
[U03589](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03589) elsewhere than as buddhas.  
[U03590](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03590) Because those with bodies are pervaded by ordinary mind,  
[U03591](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03591) not a buddha—not a sentient being. [N-157](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-157)  
[U03592](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03592) Like a result from a seed,  
[U03593](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03593) the crucial point of the condition of one's own intrinsic nature,  
[U03594](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03594) not through words of expression,  
[U03595](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03595) but through clear appearance in the objects of the faculties,  
[U03596](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03596) is the identity of liberation through naked seeing. [N-157](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-157)  
[U03597](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03597) Furthermore, through conditions, among sentient beings,  
[U03598](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03598) there is not even one who is not a buddha.  
[U03599](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03599) Because self-appearance and primordial knowing accord as phenomena,  
[U03600](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03600) samsara has not existed from the outset.  
[U03601](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03601) Therefore, each is a buddha.  
[U03602](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03602) Birth itself is from conceptual thought.  
[U03603](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03603) Abiding in the womb is the basic space of phenomena.  
[U03604](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03604) Through body and ordinary mind gathering, basic space and awareness are connected.  
[U03605](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03605) Through abiding in a body, there are the three embodiments.  
[U03606](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03606) Through aging, phenomena are exhausted and delusive appearances stop.  
[U03607](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03607) Through illness, there is experiential acquaintance with phenomena.  
[U03608](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03608) Through death, there is emptiness without identification.  
[U03609](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03609) Therefore, sentient beings are buddhas. [N-157](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-157)  
[U03610](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03610) Furthermore, the crucial point of directly perceiving the nature of phenomena:  
[U03611](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03611) through seeing alone, there is knowing;  
[U03612](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03612) through knowing, there is realization;  
[U03613](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03613) through realization alone, one is liberated.  
[U03614](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03614) What is called liberated is not something unbound by an agent.  
[U03615](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03615) Just as space itself, into space,  
[U03616](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03616) dissolves, with intrinsic nature without an object of focus,  
[U03617](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03617) there is no partiality or portion.  
[U03618](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03618) For the fortunate who see directly,  
[U03619](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03619) because even the name ‘samsara of the three realms’ is absent,  
[U03620](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03620) the three existences are decisively resolved. [N-158](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-158)  
[U03621](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03621) Thus, from The Great All-Penetrating Word, the Root of All Phenomena,  
[U03622](alignment/chapter-02.md#u03622) this is the second chapter: ‘Array of Gathered Crucial Points: The Definite Arising of the Root of Awareness.’ [N-158](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-158)  

<a id="part-3"></a>

## Chapter 3

[U03623](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03623) Then the lord of the gods, Delight-Maker,  
[U03624](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03624) concerning what is called ‘Array of Primordial Knowing,’  
[U03625](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03625) questioned the buddha, the Blessed One: [N-159](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-159)  
[U03626](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03626) ‘How does the nature of phenomena abide?  
[U03627](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03627) How does delusion arise from it?  
[U03628](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03628) What are the causes and conditions of delusion?  
[U03629](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03629) Why are characteristics realized?  
[U03630](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03630) What causes sentient beings to differ?  
[U03631](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03631) What is the state in which intrinsic nature is complete?  
[U03632](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03632) What makes the characteristics of names?  
[U03633](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03633) How do karma and faculties accord?  
[U03634](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03634) For what purpose do words enter?  
[U03635](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03635) From what do conceptual mind and knowing arise?  
[U03636](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03636) Why are there an apprehended object and an apprehending subject?  
[U03637](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03637) Essence as primordial purity and primordial knowing,  
[U03638](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03638) and intrinsic nature as spontaneous presence—why are these so?  
[U03639](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03639) Through what does compassionate responsiveness pervade all?  
[U03640](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03640) Why is [primordial knowing] like a mirror?  
[U03641](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03641) What is intended by evenness?  
[U03642](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03642) What is the extent of discriminating [primordial knowing]?  
[U03643](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03643) How is activity accomplished?  
[U03644](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03644) How does one act within the basic space of phenomena?  
[U03645](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03645) What primordial knowing is gathered through knowing?  
[U03646](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03646) What pervades the multiplicity?  
[U03647](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03647) What is the enlightened intent of knowing how things are?  
[U03648](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03648) What single principle gathers the primordial knowings?  
[U03649](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03649) I did not previously know these.  
[U03650](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03650) Teacher of great compassionate responsiveness, speak.’ [N-159](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-159)  
[U03651](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03651) From the intervening space, without dividing space,  
[U03652](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03652) phrases made by no one,  
[U03653](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03653) intrinsically without any conceptual thought,  
[U03654](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03654) from words beyond expression, with reflection pure—[N-159](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-159)  
[U03655](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03655) these kings of phrases  
[U03656](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03656) arose as if spoken by the teacher.  
[U03657](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03657) [Source annotation: The first reply is taught in ten subdivisions.]  
[U03658](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03658) What is called the abiding of the nature of phenomena:  
[U03659](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03659) abiding in time, in intrinsic nature,  
[U03660](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03660) and through the definite measure of shifting;  
[U03661](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03661) abiding from the outset, through effort,  
[U03662](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03662) conditions, causes and conditions, and intervals. [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03663](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03663) The nature of phenomena's abiding in time:  
[U03664](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03664) from the four parts—summer, winter, autumn, and spring—  
[U03665](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03665) at the beginning of autumn, aggregates,  
[U03666](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03666) elements, and primordial knowing increase.  
[U03667](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03667) At this time, profound dependent connections are taught.  
[U03668](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03668) It abides on the ninth and eighth of the waning moon,  
[U03669](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03669) and the fourteenth and [fifteenth] of the waxing moon. [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03670](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03670) For days, half of twelve parts; [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03671](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03671) also, among twelve times of the sun,  
[U03672](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03672) it abides in four aspects of a part. [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03673](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03673) At this time, train in the nature of phenomena. [Source variant: ‘Apply also occurs.’] [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03674](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03674) As for abiding in intrinsic nature:  
[U03675](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03675) in outer objects and inner ordinary mind,  
[U03676](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03676) and in the breath between them, it abides.  
[U03677](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03677) This, too, the yogin should know.  
[U03678](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03678) Abiding in the definite measure of shifting:  
[U03679](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03679) through the winds of great movement,  
[U03680](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03680) in the measure of movement at each respective stage,  
[U03681](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03681) at each five hundred,  
[U03682](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03682) there is a shift in the abiding of the nature of phenomena.  
[U03683](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03683) Conceptual thought and holding are at the thousand-junctures. [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03684](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03684) For the respective places where the six classes fall,  
[U03685](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03685) grasp the conjunctions between wind movements.  
[U03686](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03686) The method of making the nature of ordinary mind abide there:  
[U03687](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03687) reckoning from the time of five hundred,  
[U03688](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03688) strive at the point of applying the respective virtues.  
[U03689](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03689) This, too, changes into afflictions through conditions.  
[U03690](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03690) Grasping [this] from the respective intervals of wind,  
[U03691](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03691) the particulars of lifespan and karma, too,  
[U03692](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03692) are to be known through changes in wind. [N-160](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-160)  
[U03693](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03693) The nature of phenomena that abides from the outset:  
[U03694](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03694) at the initial time when there is no appearance,  
[U03695](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03695) intrinsic nature abides without contrivance.  
[U03696](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03696) Stainless ordinary mind, too, is like that.  
[U03697](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03697) Through effort, pressing the crucial point of direct perception,  
[U03698](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03698) one abides in the uncontrived Ground.  
[U03699](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03699) For abiding through conditions, through the elements'  
[U03700](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03700) bodily distinctions of purity,  
[U03701](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03701) and the distinction of impurity,  
[U03702](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03702) through the activity of primordial knowing's abiding,  
[U03703](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03703) the skilled person grasps the respective times.  
[U03704](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03704) Ordinary mind that abides through a cause,  
[U03705](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03705) because it pervades all, is in the condition of the nature of phenomena;  
[U03706](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03706) if uncontrived, it abides.  
[U03707](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03707) For abiding in an interval, draw through wind.  
[U03708](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03708) Know [this] within the interval of subtle movement.  
[U03709](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03709) This gathers the crucial points of primordial knowing. [N-161](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-161)  
[U03710](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03710) [Source annotation: The second reply is taught in eight points.]  
[U03711](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03711) From this, delusion: cause and conditions,  
[U03712](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03712) portions and branches, activity itself,  
[U03713](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03713) the immature, differentiating conceptualization, and samsara.  
[U03714](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03714) Ignorance has three aspects.  
[U03715](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03715) Through their single identity, it makes the root of delusion.  
[U03716](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03716) Through gathering and holding, it is called delusion.  
[U03717](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03717) As for portions, through formations and so forth,  
[U03718](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03718) subtract twelve from the portion of delusion's objects. [N-162](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-162)  
[U03719](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03719) Through maturation, there are years and activities.  
[U03720](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03720) The branches, from differing elaborations,  
[U03721](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03721) all, as distinctions of names,  
[U03722](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03722) become two aspects in the object of delusion.  
[U03723](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03723) Through activity, there are the desire and form realms,  
[U03724](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03724) and the formless realm: three are made.  
[U03725](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03725) The immature are the forms of the six classes;  
[U03726](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03726) names and particulars become distinct.  
[U03727](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03727) Differentiating conceptualization: dormant tendencies,  
[U03728](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03728) rising up everywhere, and the manner of pervasion.  
[U03729](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03729) What is called samsara is similarity,  
[U03730](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03730) and the activity of gathering—condensing and spreading,  
[U03731](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03731) completion and manifold distinctions.  
[U03732](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03732) Thus, apart from merely the name ‘delusion,’  
[U03733](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03733) through the signs of conceptualizing and holding as agents,  
[U03734](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03734) and the agency of assembling and condensing,  
[U03735](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03735) it is imputed through distinctions of names. [N-162](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-162)  
[U03736](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03736) [Source annotation: The third reply is taught in nine points.]  
[U03737](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03737) The cause of delusion is ignorance:  
[U03738](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03738) the Ground, knowing, contamination,  
[U03739](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03739) and the ways apprehended object and faculties circle. [N-163](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-163)  
[U03740](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03740) Conditions are from the object and the apprehended portion:  
[U03741](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03741) limits, the agents of limits,  
[U03742](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03742) not knowing one, and the support of focus.  
[U03743](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03743) The Ground does not differentiate anything,  
[U03744](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03744) and is merely without determination.  
[U03745](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03745) Knowing engages and vanishes,  
[U03746](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03746) and holds [what] spreads.  
[U03747](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03747) Contamination is holding as a stain:  
[U03748](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03748) one's own continuum is bound by what is to be known.  
[U03749](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03749) Even where there is no apprehended object,  
[U03750](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03750) [holding it as] true binds [one] tightly. [N-163](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-163)  
[U03751](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03751) The faculties, through portions of object and knowing,  
[U03752](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03752) appear in that object held as permanent.  
[U03753](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03753) Through knowing the manner of circling as one,  
[U03754](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03754) differentiating conceptualizations unite in pairs.  
[U03755](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03755) The agents of limits are activity and cause;  
[U03756](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03756) the elements become the faculties' activities.  
[U03757](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03757) Not knowing one: from primordial purity,  
[U03758](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03758) because the nature of phenomena's own identity is not known,  
[U03759](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03759) there is appropriation corresponding to the cause.  
[U03760](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03760) The support of focus, from colors,  
[U03761](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03761) through the moving support of two subtle portions,  
[U03762](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03762) becomes the object of focus of samsaric activity. [N-164](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-164)  
[U03763](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03763) [Source annotation: The fourth reply is taught in eight points.]  
[U03764](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03764) The particulars of realizing characteristics:  
[U03765](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03765) existence, nonexistence, appearance,  
[U03766](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03766) clarity, emptiness, the coarse, the apprehending subject,  
[U03767](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03767) consciousness, and what possesses form. [N-165](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-165)  
[U03768](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03768) What exists has the agency of an entity;  
[U03769](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03769) through entering appearance and characteristics, it pervades,  
[U03770](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03770) and closely reveals activities and particulars.  
[U03771](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03771) Through nonexistence, form and so forth are abandoned.  
[U03772](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03772) There is entry of mental faculty and knowing;  
[U03773](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03773) characteristics are never found.  
[U03774](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03774) Appearance is form and so forth.  
[U03775](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03775) It holds, spreads, and gathers,  
[U03776](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03776) with the intrinsic nature of paired entry and pervasion.  
[U03777](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03777) Clarity reveals its own identity  
[U03778](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03778) and is the reverse of nonappearance.  
[U03779](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03779) Emptiness is not birth;  
[U03780](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03780) it reveals radiance and absence of limits or center.  
[U03781](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03781) Because it has not arisen, it does not cease.  
[U03782](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03782) The coarse, from fine and coarse particles,  
[U03783](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03783) is complete as manifold forms;  
[U03784](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03784) it is tangible and appears as destructible.  
[U03785](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03785) The apprehending subject, toward the objects of the faculties,  
[U03786](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03786) binds through their respective apprehension,  
[U03787](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03787) and performs all activities of selfhood and afflictions.  
[U03788](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03788) Consciousness is a portion of conceptual thought:  
[U03789](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03789) it enters objects and engages with them,  
[U03790](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03790) and differentiating conceptualization concerns thought and activity.  
[U03791](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03791) What possesses form is aware [or] insentient,  
[U03792](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03792) gathering and arranging ordinary mind. [N-165](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-165)  
[U03793](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03793) [Source annotation: The fifth reply is taught in eight points.]  
[U03794](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03794) The causes of all sentient beings' differences:  
[U03795](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03795) elemental causes and activities of agency,  
[U03796](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03796) the faculties themselves, shapes and measures,  
[U03797](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03797) sounds and respective expressions,  
[U03798](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03798) and particulars of the vessels in which they abide.  
[U03799](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03799) The elemental causes, from four,  
[U03800](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03800) when examined, are sixty;  
[U03801](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03801) when differentiated, four hundred and four.  
[U03802](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03802) The activities of agency, from four and two,  
[U03803](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03803) when examined, become twelve and sixty.  
[U03804](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03804) From these, the six classes differ. [N-166](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-166)  
[U03805](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03805) For faculties such as the eyes,  
[U03806](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03806) through differences in shapes and colors,  
[U03807](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03807) also through four hundred and four,  
[U03808](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03808) the bodies of beings differ.  
[U03809](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03809) Through bodily shape, body and limbs,  
[U03810](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03810) and their respective continents and faculties,  
[U03811](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03811) the forms of beings are differentiated.  
[U03812](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03812) Measures concern each of the six classes:  
[U03813](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03813) beginning, middle, and end,  
[U03814](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03814) distinguished by their respective merit and karma.  
[U03815](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03815) Sounds are long and short,  
[U03816](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03816) strong and weak, pressing and releasing,  
[U03817](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03817) subtle, seng [unresolved], and pointed. [N-166](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-166)  
[U03818](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03818) Entities, through earth, water, fire, and wind,  
[U03819](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03819) are examined through knowing itself, merit,  
[U03820](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03820) ascendancy, and bodily portions.  
[U03821](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03821) Expression: place, elements,  
[U03822](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03822) activity, and conceptual thought—their own crucial points.  
[U03823](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03823) The vessels of abiding are pure  
[U03824](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03824) or impure, distinguished by their inhabitants.  
[U03825](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03825) Therefore, through the elements' own crucial points,  
[U03826](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03826) the bodies of beings differ. [N-166](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-166)  
[U03827](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03827) [Source annotation: The sixth reply is taught in eight points.]  
[U03828](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03828) The state in which intrinsic nature is complete:  
[U03829](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03829) the nature of phenomena, basic space, and own identity;  
[U03830](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03830) movement empty and conceptual thought exhausted;  
[U03831](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03831) completion in the Ground, path, and result.  
[U03832](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03832) The nature of phenomena cannot be contrived by anyone.  
[U03833](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03833) Intrinsic nature is pure, free from conceptual elaborations,  
[U03834](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03834) beyond the object of distinct differentiating conceptualizations.  
[U03835](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03835) The flow of delusion arising through words is cut. [N-167](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-167)  
[U03836](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03836) Basic space has outer and inner aspects.  
[U03837](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03837) Outer: hold [the focus] in cloud-free space.  
[U03838](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03838) When fine dust has been cleared by rain,  
[U03839](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03839) the skilled person presses the crucial point of direct perception,  
[U03840](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03840) and takes the inner lamps as the path.  
[U03841](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03841) Own identity: awareness as vajra chains,  
[U03842](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03842) separating going and coming, in space. [N-167](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-167)  
[U03843](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03843) Movement: cut the activity of the two winds.  
[U03844](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03844) Distinguish through the ninth wind;  
[U03845](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03845) distinguish entry into abiding through methods.  
[U03846](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03846) The example: through the winds being empty,  
[U03847](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03847) all appearances become exhausted.  
[U03848](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03848) Likewise, the exhaustion of differentiating conceptualization  
[U03849](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03849) and unborn primordial knowing are simultaneous.  
[U03850](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03850) At this time, samsara and nirvana mix.  
[U03851](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03851) This, too, is the crucial point of completion in the Ground. [N-167](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-167)  
[U03852](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03852) Uncontrived intrinsic nature appears manifestly.  
[U03853](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03853) The path: through connecting with the lamps,  
[U03854](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03854) it appears in direct perception itself.  
[U03855](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03855) The result cannot be turned back anywhere.  
[U03856](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03856) It transcends objects of desire, hope, and fear.  
[U03857](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03857) Self-appearance is complete as pure from the outset;  
[U03858](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03858) knowing, holding, and conceptual thought are all exhausted. [N-167](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-167)  
[U03859](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03859) [Source annotation: The seventh reply is taught in eight points.]  
[U03860](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03860) What makes the characteristics of names:  
[U03861](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03861) cause, conditions, imputation,  
[U03862](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03862) limits, the first, transformation of the last,  
[U03863](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03863) not finding through examination, and the basis of convention. [N-168](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-168)  
[U03864](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03864) The cause of a name is a verbal symbol.  
[U03865](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03865) Because characteristics are not established anywhere,  
[U03866](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03866) what merely appears cannot be designated. [N-168](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-168)  
[U03867](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03867) The conditions are the objects directly before the faculties.  
[U03868](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03868) In their respective appearances accompanied by clinging,  
[U03869](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03869) the gathering of the all-basis arises as aspects of appearance.  
[U03870](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03870) Through imputation, from symbols and agents of transformation,  
[U03871](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03871) characteristics are closely shown.  
[U03872](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03872) In particular, the crucial point of what is transformed by a name:  
[U03873](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03873) joining [it] with each respective occasion,  
[U03874](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03874) even the Blessed One will be destroyed. [N-168](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-168)  
[U03875](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03875) Limits are distinguished through the elements' crucial points.  
[U03876](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03876) Killing, restoring, and destroying activities  
[U03877](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03877) are performed through names and dependent connections.  
[U03878](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03878) First, the letters of the imputed name:  
[U03879](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03879) through relying on their first [letters],  
[U03880](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03880) killing, restoring, and abiding itself—  
[U03881](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03881) know these through their respective measures.  
[U03882](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03882) For the end of the name transformed last,  
[U03883](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03883) reverse and place [it], joining elements and body.  
[U03884](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03884) In the authentic nature of phenomena, free from conceptual thought,  
[U03885](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03885) having thoroughly distinguished the characteristics of names,  
[U03886](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03886) through thorough examination of outer and inner,  
[U03887](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03887) because characteristics are not found through names,  
[U03888](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03888) one wishes to complete one's own rite. [N-168](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-168)  
[U03889](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03889) Conventions are the limits of words.  
[U03890](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03890) Distinguish them everywhere through the extremes of existence and nonexistence.  
[U03891](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03891) Through examining and analyzing body and ordinary mind, the two,  
[U03892](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03892) the basis of convention, too, is empty.  
[U03893](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03893) The Ground is neither one nor two.  
[U03894](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03894) Imputed emptiness: the extremes of existence and nonexistence stop.  
[U03895](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03895) No one finds what is made or what has arisen.  
[U03896](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03896) Likewise, there is no abiding, going, or coming.  
[U03897](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03897) Therefore, the nature of phenomena is empty. [N-168](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-168)  
[U03898](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03898) [Source annotation: The eighth reply is taught in seven points.]  
[U03899](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03899) Sentient beings' karma and faculties:  
[U03900](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03900) wind, knowing, the outer vessel,  
[U03901](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03901) the elements in general, and the manner of pervading;  
[U03902](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03902) three, nine, and twenty-one. [N-169](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-169)  
[U03903](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03903) Sentient beings' karma, too, is impelled  
[U03904](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03904) into the elemental projection of wind.  
[U03905](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03905) From this, through karmic particulars,  
[U03906](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03906) one enters and thoroughly distinguishes through methods.  
[U03907](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03907) The knowing that enacts this,  
[U03908](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03908) like the intrinsic nature of clouds in space  
[U03909](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03909) being scattered by wind,  
[U03910](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03910) reveals karmic appearances piece by piece.  
[U03911](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03911) From this, through maturing into body, speech, and mental faculty,  
[U03912](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03912) the forms of karma are revealed.  
[U03913](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03913) Through the elements—earth, water, fire, and wind—  
[U03914](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03914) completion as a body is wind's activity.  
[U03915](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03915) Pervading: all sentient beings,  
[U03916](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03916) because they do not go beyond a body of four elements,  
[U03917](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03917) are in their respective combinations of karma.  
[U03918](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03918) Manner: being established as form,  
[U03919](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03919) from the earlier and later aspects of its karma,  
[U03920](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03920) each takes up and experiences [them]. [N-169](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-169)  
[U03921](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03921) The three faculties are superior, middling, and inferior.  
[U03922](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03922) Through these, the three vehicles are free from perception. [N-169](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-169)  
[U03923](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03923) Thoroughly dividing each into three,  
[U03924](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03924) there are nine: the faculties' crucial point of the nine vehicles.  
[U03925](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03925) From these, thoroughly dividing into two,  
[U03926](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03926) there are two sets of nine: aspects of appearance.  
[U03927](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03927) Placing three in the Ground, through twenty-one,  
[U03928](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03928) the twenty-one appearances are complete. [N-169](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-169)  
[U03929](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03929) [Source annotation: The ninth reply is taught in six inner points.]  
[U03930](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03930) The entry of words is like this:  
[U03931](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03931) Brahmā, the All-Pervader, and the Hundred-Giver;  
[U03932](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03932) the Sky-Soarer, the Powerful Lord, and the Fierce One;  
[U03933](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03933) their respective sounds and the voices of gods.  
[U03934](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03934) By reckoning other places, one enters words. [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03935](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03935) For Brahmā, the first verse,  
[U03936](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03936) half complete, becomes eight other places.  
[U03937](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03937) For the Powerful Lord, nine verses;  
[U03938](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03938) two halves of their places result.  
[U03939](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03939) For the All-Pervader, in two thousand,  
[U03940](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03940) through mastery of the places, one is complete. [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03941](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03941) For the Hundred-Giver, five verses;  
[U03942](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03942) through reversing sound, three and two are complete.  
[U03943](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03943) For the Sky-Soarer, through six verses,  
[U03944](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03944) distinguishing signs: seven and two.  
[U03945](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03945) For the Fierce One, entry is in three and two. [Source variant: ‘Thirty also occurs.’] [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03946](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03946) The activity of arranging signs: seven and four. [Source variant: ‘Four hundred also.’] [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03947](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03947) Their respective sounds, from abiding,  
[U03948](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03948) entering, arising, and reversing,  
[U03949](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03949) relying on time, are complete with five.  
[U03950](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03950) The voices of gods number six thousand.  
[U03951](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03951) Adorning them with branches doubles that.  
[U03952](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03952) From the root foundation of other places—[Source variant: ‘brtse ba bzhi also’; wording unresolved.] [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03953](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03953) examining words: one hundred and two places. [Source variant: ‘Eight also.’] [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03954](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03954) Extending from that: four hundred and four. [Source variant: ‘Extending: one hundred and eighty-four also.’] [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03955](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03955) The calculation of definite particulars becomes ten. [Source variant: ‘Definitely, that becomes ten also.’] [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03956](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03956) By knowing how to combine these thoroughly,  
[U03957](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03957) one gains mastery through verses and becomes a king of speech.  
[U03958](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03958) Through this, the supreme combination is definitely accomplished. [N-170](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-170)  
[U03959](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03959) [Source annotation: The tenth reply is taught in nine inner points.]  
[U03960](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03960) What are called conceptual mind and knowing:  
[U03961](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03961) ordinary mind, mental faculty, movement,  
[U03962](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03962) proliferation, agitation, rigidity,  
[U03963](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03963) object, apprehending subject, and clinging.  
[U03964](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03964) Ordinary mind: the pure and impure  
[U03965](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03965) three realms, too, enter buddhahood. [N-171](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-171)  
[U03966](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03966) Mental faculty is known through essence, word explanation,  
[U03967](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03967) and subdivisions.  
[U03968](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03968) Movement: portions of mindfulness and appearance,  
[U03969](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03969) through examination, become conceptual mind.  
[U03970](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03970) Proliferation, from the culmination of activities toward objects,  
[U03971](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03971) appears as the expressiveness of conceptual mind and knowing.  
[U03972](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03972) Agitation is the activity enacted by wind;  
[U03973](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03973) formations are a symbol of conceptual mind and knowing.  
[U03974](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03974) Rigidity, from three afflictions,  
[U03975](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03975) changes into what is called conceptual mind and is cut by knowing.  
[U03976](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03976) From the two gathering, three [kinds of] knowing arise. [Source variant: ‘Three realms also.’] [N-171](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-171)  
[U03977](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03977) Objects: from the apparent aspects of the five doors,  
[U03978](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03978) the entry portions of conceptual mind and knowing arise.  
[U03979](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03979) The apprehending subject: through eyes and so forth,  
[U03980](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03980) conceptual mind and knowing are actual in each respective [object].  
[U03981](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03981) Clinging: conceptual mind has four measures of realization;  
[U03982](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03982) through the measures of knowing, these become eight.  
[U03983](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03983) Arising from this, there are holding and attachment. [N-171](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-171)  

[U03984](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03984) [Source heading: Eleventh reply.]  
[U03985](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03985) Thus, from the agency of apparent aspects,  
[U03986](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03986) apprehended object and apprehending subject arise as two.  
[U03987](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03987) The apprehended object is the object; the apprehending subject is ordinary mind.  
[U03988](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03988) Object and ordinary mind gathered together are the three poisons.  
[U03989](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03989) The object's crucial point is in the faculties; the faculties' crucial point is ordinary mind.  
[U03990](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03990) Ordinary mind's crucial point is in the eyes; the eyes' crucial point is the channels.  
[U03991](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03991) Therefore, through bodily posture,  
[U03992](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03992) the connection of habitual tendencies with the body is cut,  
[U03993](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03993) and the buddhas' enlightened intent is complete.  
[U03994](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03994) The channels, through the wheel of dependent connections,  
[U03995](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03995) are the place of seeking, holding, and increasing—  
[U03996](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03996) the yogin's branches. [N-172](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-172)  

[U03997](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03997) [Source heading: Twelfth reply.]  
[U03998](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03998) From essence as primordial purity and primordial knowing,  
[U03999](alignment/chapter-03.md#u03999) there is no possible name ‘ignorance.’  
[U04000](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04000) There is no count of one and two. [N-173](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-173)  
[U04001](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04001) On examination, neither existence nor nonexistence is established.  
[U04002](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04002) In the nature of phenomena, undivided anywhere,  
[U04003](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04003) not even a mere primordial knowing is established.  
[U04004](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04004) Without phrases, nothing is established through expression. [N-174](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-174)  
[U04005](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04005) Pure self-awareness does not abide at an extreme;  
[U04006](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04006) the limits of the names ‘apprehended object and apprehending subject’ are exhausted.  
[U04007](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04007) There is no cause, nor any number of conditions;  
[U04008](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04008) there is no dual appearance, object, or possessor of an object.  
[U04009](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04009) No characteristic is differentiated anywhere;  
[U04010](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04010) coarse objects and conditions are self-exhausted.  
[U04011](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04011) Absent from the beginning, pure through purity,  
[U04012](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04012) with deluded conceptual thought ceased, it does not enact anything.  
[U04013](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04013) Since it has not arisen, cessation is empty. [N-174](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-174)  

[U04014](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04014) [Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]  
[U04015](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04015) What is called primordial knowing of intrinsic nature's spontaneous presence  
[U04016](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04016) does not arise, does not cease, and directs enlightened intent toward nothing,  
[U04017](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04017) because no object is fixed. [N-175](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-175)  
[U04018](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04018) Since expressiveness and qualities do not cease,  
[U04019](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04019) it appears merely as play:  
[U04020](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04020) the foundation in which diversity is complete. [N-175](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-175)  
[U04021](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04021) Because it does not exist, it appears; because it appears, it is empty.  
[U04022](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04022) It has the subsidiary aspects of appearance and emptiness entering together;  
[U04023](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04023) it is the pure domain of buddhas and sentient beings.  
[U04024](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04024) Thus it manifestly abides as what is called ‘the Ground.’  
[U04025](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04025) Abiding merely as intrinsic nature,  
[U04026](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04026) it is empty when examined from the aspect of essence.  
[U04027](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04027) Because its subsidiary aspects unfold, knowing is complete.  
[U04028](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04028) Since it abides of itself in spontaneous presence,  
[U04029](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04029) rejecting and accepting, the two, are absent here. [N-175](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-175)  

[U04030](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04030) [Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]  
[U04031](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04031) From primordial knowing of all-pervading compassionate responsiveness,  
[U04032](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04032) the inexhaustible gates of diverse arising  
[U04033](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04033) are complete in essence, though appearing to be exhausted. [N-176](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-176)  
[U04034](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04034) From the empty intrinsic nature of the dharma embodiment,  
[U04035](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04035) the aspect of primordial knowing whose knowing is complete  
[U04036](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04036) arises for sentient beings through awakened mind.  
[U04037](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04037) Without that, the central connection of samsara and nirvana would be severed;  
[U04038](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04038) through knowing, there is awareness and clarity. [N-176](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-176)  
[U04039](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04039) From the very identity of clear self-awareness,  
[U04040](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04040) through the force of intrinsic nature, compassionate responsiveness itself  
[U04041](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04041) has not ceased and does not cease.  
[U04042](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04042) From the pure aspect of the elements,  
[U04043](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04043) the one that is not an activity is complete. [N-176](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-176)  
[U04044](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04044) Like light from the sun,  
[U04045](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04045) it is inherent and unobscured.  

[U04046](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04046) [Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]  
[U04047](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04047) Then I shall explain the characteristic-holding  
[U04048](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04048) primordial knowing of the complete enjoyment embodiment.  
[U04049](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04049) Mirror-like primordial knowing makes forms clear;  
[U04050](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04050) the appearance aspects of shape and color are complete. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  
[U04051](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04051) It is white because stains are purified.  
[U04052](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04052) Appearing inherently, it joins samsara and nirvana.  
[U04053](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04053) Because it sees the forms of all phenomena,  
[U04054](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04054) it is called ‘primordial knowing through itself.’ [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  
[U04055](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04055) Clear and empty, it is liberated into emptiness;  
[U04056](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04056) conceptual thought at the limits of liberation and grasping is exhausted.  
[U04057](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04057) With no other, the Ground of liberation is complete.  

[U04058](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04058) [Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]  
[U04059](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04059) What is called ‘evenness’  
[U04060](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04060) is through two causes of evenness, three conditions of evenness,  
[U04061](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04061) and the time and measure of evenness.  
[U04062](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04062) Here there is no duality, and it is free from awareness. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  
[U04063](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04063) Neither divided nor dividing, it abides of itself.  
[U04064](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04064) What is called ‘even’ is, in the nature of phenomena,  
[U04065](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04065) freedom from the elaborations of pain.  
[U04066](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04066) ‘-ness’ means there is no contrivance or accomplishment through effort.  
[U04067](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04067) Intrinsic nature abides; essence is empty.  
[U04068](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04068) In the nature of ordinary mind, all conceptual thought is exhausted.  
[U04069](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04069) What is called ‘primordial knowing’ abides;  
[U04070](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04070) through realizing its characteristics,  
[U04071](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04071) one does not abide in either samsara or nirvana. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  

[U04072](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04072) [Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]  
[U04073](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04073) Discriminating [primordial knowing]: the types of faculty,  
[U04074](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04074) whatever appears to them, and the phenomena of that appearance  
[U04075](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04075) are each clear in sequence. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  
[U04076](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04076) When the self-appearance of examination is purified,  
[U04077](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04077) awareness itself becomes the object.  
[U04078](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04078) What is called ‘individually’ arises from distinctions  
[U04079](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04079) in the manner of an antidote.  
[U04080](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04080) What is called ‘realization’ is seeing the characteristics,  
[U04081](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04081) together with the increase of self-appearance.  
[U04082](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04082) Primordial knowing arises at the beginning;  
[U04083](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04083) through knowing, the faults of afflictions are purified. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  

[U04084](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04084) [Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]  
[U04085](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04085) What is called ‘doing accomplished’:  
[U04086](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04086) when striving and effort have self-ceased,  
[U04087](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04087) all phenomena rest of themselves and are self-liberated.  
[U04088](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04088) From ground-appearance in which self-liberation is complete,  
[U04089](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04089) the subtle aspect attains freedom from particles and stains.  
[U04090](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04090) Beyond the object of conceptual thought and examination,  
[U04091](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04091) it is the nature of phenomena, divided by no one.  
[U04092](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04092) Intrinsic nature being complete, the limits of appearance are exhausted.  
[U04093](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04093) Simultaneous realization is ‘doing.’  
[U04094](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04094) Through being ‘accomplished,’ desire is absent;  
[U04095](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04095) there is neither repetition nor reversal.  
[U04096](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04096) ‘Primordial’ has the meaning of abiding;  
[U04097](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04097) through ‘knowing,’ it becomes manifest,  
[U04098](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04098) and one reaches the place where phenomena are exhausted. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  

[U04099](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04099) [Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]  
[U04100](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04100) The basic space of phenomena is vast;  
[U04101](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04101) no edge or center is an object of focus.  
[U04102](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04102) In the pure nature of phenomena, the Ground of liberation,  
[U04103](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04103) ‘phenomena’ means engaging in activity:  
[U04104](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04104) doing the doings of the transcendent and the world.  
[U04105](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04105) Intrinsic nature is clear and limpid.  
[U04106](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04106) ‘Basic space’ opens room;  
[U04107](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04107) it is great naturally arisen abiding.  
[U04108](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04108) ‘Primordial’ is the very identity of abiding,  
[U04109](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04109) uncontrived from the beginning.  
[U04110](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04110) Through ‘knowing,’ one is liberated from both samsara and nirvana.  
[U04111](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04111) This is enlightened intent in which self-appearance is complete. [N-177](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-177)  

[U04112](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04112) [Source heading: Twentieth reply.]  
[U04113](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04113) Primordial knowing encompassed by knowing  
[U04114](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04114) pervades all buddhas and sentient beings  
[U04115](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04115) indivisibly, without intrinsic nature.  
[U04116](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04116) In buddhas it is completely pure;  
[U04117](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04117) it abides unstained and free from particles.  
[U04118](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04118) In the appearance aspect of sentient beings of the six classes,  
[U04119](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04119) it abides in each one's continuum:  
[U04120](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04120) in gods, it is complete in its own clarity;  
[U04121](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04121) in asuras, subtle and flashing;  
[U04122](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04122) in humans, round and clear of itself;  
[U04123](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04123) in animals, clear within;  
[U04124](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04124) in hungry ghosts, subtle;  
[U04125](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04125) in hell, complete as self-appearance. [N-178](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-178)  
[U04126](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04126) Through ‘knowing,’ there is awareness, clear in its own place.  
[U04127](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04127) Through ‘encompassed,’ essence is self-awareness.  
[U04128](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04128) ‘Primordial’ is the clear aspect of knowing and awareness;  
[U04129](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04129) ‘knowing’ is self-liberation, a single mandala. [N-178](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-178)  

[U04130](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04130) [Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]  
[U04131](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04131) Primordial knowing encompassed by objects of knowledge  
[U04132](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04132) is to be known in two aspects.  
[U04133](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04133) Of these, I shall explain objects of knowledge in their multiplicity.  
[U04134](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04134) Through this, the intentions of those to be trained are known.  
[U04135](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04135) Through understanding others' benefit,  
[U04136](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04136) one knows the intrinsic nature of phenomena.  
[U04137](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04137) ‘Whatever’ refers to the natural state;  
[U04138](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04138) ‘multiplicity’ completes everything without remainder.  
[U04139](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04139) ‘Primordial’ concerns the benefit of all sentient beings;  
[U04140](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04140) through ‘knowing,’ the three realms are drawn out from their depths. [N-178](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-178)  
[U04141](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04141) The knowing aspect of the emanation embodiment  
[U04142](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04142) is complete in the identity of self-appearance.  

[U04143](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04143) [Source heading: Twenty-second reply.]  
[U04144](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04144) I shall explain the primordial knowing that knows how [things are].  
[U04145](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04145) The appearance aspect is free from a basis for expression;  
[U04146](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04146) the reflection of pure self-appearance is clear.  
[U04147](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04147) Through knowing the natural state of things,  
[U04148](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04148) and realizing one's own benefit, the flow of delusion is exhausted.  
[U04149](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04149) ‘How’ refers to the natural state;  
[U04150](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04150) ‘view’ engages without distraction. [N-178](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-178)  
[U04151](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04151) ‘Knowing’ is complete because it does not cease.  
[U04152](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04152) ‘Primordial’ has become the path;  
[U04153](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04153) ‘knowing’ has reached its measure.  
[U04154](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04154) There is no movement from the Ground  
[U04155](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04155) of great uncontrived self-liberation. [N-178](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-178)  

[U04156](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04156) [Source heading: Twenty-third reply.]  
[U04157](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04157) The gathering of primordial knowings  
[U04158](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04158) has pervasive and clear characteristics;  
[U04159](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04159) intrinsic nature is clarity-emptiness, indivisible,  
[U04160](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04160) and essence is not differentiated anywhere.  
[U04161](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04161) Gathered into four divisions,  
[U04162](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04162) it shows distinctions in the methods by which conceptual thought enters.  
[U04163](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04163) With Ground, core, and flowers,  
[U04164](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04164) it is beautiful and truly complete. [N-179](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-179)  
[U04165](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04165) Through the manner of pervasion and that which pervades,  
[U04166](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04166) it shows self-appearance in the Ground  
[U04167](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04167) and exhausts differentiating conceptualization.  
[U04168](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04168) Distinguishing the gathered special features,  
[U04169](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04169) from the self-radiance of the nature of phenomena, free from conceptual thought,  
[U04170](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04170) there arises an appearance of primordial knowing without an object of focus.  
[U04171](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04171) There the yogin's ordinary mind is complete.  
[U04172](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04172) As a subsidiary aspect of the true gathering,  
[U04173](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04173) the crucial point of primordial knowing arises from the eyes.  
[U04174](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04174) Through knowing the eyes' gaze,  
[U04175](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04175) one engages together with the basic space of phenomena.  
[U04176](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04176) In order to exhaust the three realms,  
[U04177](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04177) the pulsating channel behind the eyes  
[U04178](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04178) is turned upward, bringing down primordial knowing. [N-179](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-179)  
[U04179](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04179) Whatever primordial knowing arises from the crucial point,  
[U04180](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04180) just as the nature of phenomena endowed with awareness,  
[U04181](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04181) is completely liberated from all conceptual thought:  
[U04182](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04182) it is primordial knowing arising from basic space.  
[U04183](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04183) The crucial points of the eyes are above and below;  
[U04184](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04184) whichever avenue of effort the adept employs,  
[U04185](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04185) there is the appearance of all-gathering primordial knowing. [N-179](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-179)  
[U04186](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04186) Alternatively, in the primordial knowing of the eyes,  
[U04187](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04187) through clearly distinguishing the five colors,  
[U04188](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04188) there is a likeness of the form of a complete mandala.  
[U04189](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04189) The experiential acquaintance arising from this  
[U04190](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04190) cannot be expressed by anyone—  
[U04191](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04191) Ema! Such great wonder!—  
[U04192](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04192) an appearance complete in qualities.  
[U04193](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04193) From pervasive primordial knowing, subtle in aspect,  
[U04194](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04194) it is through direct perception and on the path. [N-179](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-179)  
[U04195](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04195) Furthermore, this is to be explained:  
[U04196](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04196) from mindfulness and reflection in which differentiating conceptualization gathers,  
[U04197](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04197) even the dharma embodiment becomes form. [N-180](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-180)  
[U04198](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04198) One who wishes to go beyond the three realms,  
[U04199](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04199) through the great crucial point of sacred primordial knowing,  
[U04200](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04200) examines the respective winds as breaths.  
[U04201](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04201) From the self-purification of stains in all phenomena,  
[U04202](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04202) the three embodiments are complete in the center of the body.  
[U04203](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04203) Why is there conceptual thought concerning this?  
[U04204](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04204) From the intrinsic nature of gathered mindfulness and reflection,  
[U04205](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04205) even the dharma embodiment becomes samsara. [N-180](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-180)  
[U04206](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04206) When the limits of samsara are exhausted,  
[U04207](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04207) one is liberated in one's own place without anything being done.  
[U04208](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04208) In this liberation there is nothing at all.  
[U04209](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04209) Because there is nothing, the nature of phenomena is not a thing.  
[U04210](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04210) Primordial knowing being complete, the flow of delusion is cut;  
[U04211](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04211) being cut, going and coming are exhausted.  
[U04212](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04212) Because they are exhausted, it is empty primordial knowing.  
[U04213](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04213) Since the primordial knowing of the three embodiments has not ceased,  
[U04214](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04214) the existence of the three realms is empty.  
[U04215](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04215) By finding going and arising,  
[U04216](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04216) things being pure, form is exhausted. [N-180](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-180)  
[U04217](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04217) Furthermore, I shall explain the primordial knowing of appearance.  
[U04218](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04218) From earth, water, fire, wind, and space,  
[U04219](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04219) through the wonder of primordial knowing's appearance,  
[U04220](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04220) the blessed protector is without awareness. [N-181](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-181)  
[U04221](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04221) Primordial knowing, not found on examination,  
[U04222](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04222) decisively resolves the three realms  
[U04223](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04223) and cuts the flow of clinging at the five gates.  
[U04224](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04224) The moon, a jewel, ‘mar gar,’ and light—  
[U04225](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04225) one who wishes to hold these  
[U04226](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04226) holds the appearance of embodiment arising from holding. [N-181](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-181)  
[U04227](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04227) Then, through knowing the crucial point  
[U04228](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04228) of the Great Perfection, primordial knowing  
[U04229](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04229) is free from doing, just like space.  
[U04230](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04230) Through complete realization, it is free from blue. [N-181](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-181)  
[U04231](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04231) Wondrous, marvelous primordial knowing  
[U04232](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04232) was absent before, is absent afterward, and absent from the beginning.  
[U04233](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04233) Now it is beyond the object of differentiating conceptualization:  
[U04234](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04234) an empty intrinsic nature free from extremes.  
[U04235](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04235) Beyond phrases, free from the conceptual mind, empty in essence,  
[U04236](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04236) its intrinsic nature not differentiated anywhere,  
[U04237](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04237) the deeds of compassionate responsiveness do not appear. [N-181](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-181)  
[U04238](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04238) From the third appearance of primordial knowing,  
[U04239](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04239) discerning knowing arising from the mental faculty is held as an aspect of delusion. [N-181](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-181)  
[U04240](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04240) Here there is no limit of differentiated focus.  
[U04241](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04241) From the marvelous play of absence,  
[U04242](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04242) the result of the buddhas is found.  
[U04243](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04243) Outside and inside are all the basic space of phenomena;  
[U04244](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04244) here nothing whatsoever appears.  
[U04245](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04245) Furthermore, I shall explain the primordial knowing of objects.  
[U04246](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04246) Through distinctions of form and sound,  
[U04247](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04247) the experiences to be trained in become five.  
[U04248](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04248) There is the appearance of the exhaustion of knowing's experience;  
[U04249](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04249) increase is shown like the waxing moon.  
[U04250](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04250) With each appearance, an experience comes. [N-182](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-182)  
[U04251](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04251) Through warmth, measure, and signs,  
[U04252](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04252) the two truths enter in union, and the limits of conceptual thought are exhausted.  
[U04253](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04253) With no doing, characteristics are empty.  
[U04254](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04254) The basis of expression having ceased, one does not abide at an extreme.  
[U04255](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04255) Since outflows are exhausted, there is no material weight.  
[U04256](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04256) The interior of mindfulness ceases; essence is clear. [N-183](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-183)  
[U04257](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04257) Clear awareness does not conceptualize the limits of objects.  
[U04258](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04258) Free from words, truly without phrases,
