[U04259](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04259) all bases of dependence are exhausted.  
[U04260](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04260) Furthermore, one directly engages the desirable qualities.  
[U04261](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04261) The two trainings through form  
[U04262](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04262) are a support for focus and freedom from focus.  
[U04263](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04263) Through the special activity agreeable to the mental faculty,  
[U04264](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04264) first make offerings to the lama.  
[U04265](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04265) Become accustomed to shapes and then to colors;  
[U04266](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04266) through this familiarity, delusory appearance ceases.  
[U04267](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04267) For sound, with a pure support for focus:  
[U04268](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04268) piwaṃ, earthenware drum, resonant pot,  
[U04269](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04269) draba, the many-stringed instrument, the three-junctioned instrument,  
[U04270](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04270) har, flute, cymbals,  
[U04271](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04271) pheg, rdob, small hand drum, bell, and so forth—  
[U04272](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04272) join wind to each respective sound. [N-184](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-184)  
[U04273](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04273) For smell, through a condition for focus agreeable to the mental faculty,  
[U04274](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04274) faith and great faith,  
[U04275](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04275) the supreme and the greatly distinctive,  
[U04276](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04276) join the yogin to great bliss. [N-184](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-184)  
[U04277](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04277) For taste, an adept combines cane sugar, that endowed with six [tastes],  
[U04278](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04278) grape wine, and rice beer.  
[U04279](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04279) Knowing the five stages endowed with restraint,  
[U04280](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04280) merely tasting makes primordial knowing blaze. [N-184](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-184)  
[U04281](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04281) For touch, with a support for focus agreeable to the mental faculty,  
[U04282](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04282) join each awareness to experiential acquaintance.  
[U04283](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04283) When a tree leaf curls rightward and contracts,  
[U04284](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04284) raise it: through touch, buddhahood is realized. [N-184](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-184)  
[U04285](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04285) Concerning phenomena, accord with the lama's authoritative transmission.  
[U04286](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04286) When excellent cultivation is realized,  
[U04287](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04287) practicing thus brings it onto the path,  
[U04288](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04288) and the flow of the machinery of delusion in the three realms is cut.  
[U04289](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04289) Furthermore, the appearance of primordial knowing:  
[U04290](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04290) from the white of stainless purity,  
[U04291](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04291) because qualities are complete, the form of appearance  
[U04292](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04292) becomes the indivisible, complete form embodiment.  
[U04293](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04293) From the yellow that generates qualities,  
[U04294](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04294) in an activity in which the Ground and differentiation are simultaneous,  
[U04295](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04295) the nature of phenomena is shown. [N-185](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-185)  
[U04296](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04296) From the red of complete self-expressiveness,  
[U04297](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04297) the center and surrounding of pure intrinsic nature,  
[U04298](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04298) the encircling bands and enclosure, are shown in completeness.  
[U04299](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04299) From the green free from doing-expressiveness,  
[U04300](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04300) emanations of pure enlightened activity arise.  
[U04301](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04301) From the blue of unchanging completeness,  
[U04302](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04302) not abiding at edge or center, it is pervasively empty.  
[U04303](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04303) Through pure intrinsic nature, the embodiments are ‘three, four.’ [N-185](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-185)  
[U04304](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04304) Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:  
[U04305](alignment/chapter-03.md#u04305) the third chapter, the Array of Primordial Knowing, in which the root of appearance definitely emerges.  

<a id="part-4"></a>

## Chapter 4

[U04306](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04306) Then the Lord of Gods, Maker of Joy,  
[U04307](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04307) asked the teacher, the great all-pervading lord,  
[U04308](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04308) about the intrinsic nature of the Array of the Nature of Phenomena.  
[U04309](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04309) ‘Kye kye! Truly complete buddha,  
[U04310](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04310) through the Array of Primordial Knowing,  
[U04311](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04311) I have gained respite from the activities of samsara,  
[U04312](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04312) and been uplifted on the path of passing beyond sorrow. [Source variant: gzengs.] [N-186](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-186)  
[U04313](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04313) I have also seen the object of intrinsic nature's appearance.  
[U04314](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04314) Although I have gained confidence in the buddhas' enlightened intent,  
[U04315](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04315) for sentient beings who will enter in the future,  
[U04316](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04316) so that primordial knowing free from conceptual thought may be complete,  
[U04317](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04317) I seek the Array of the Nature of Phenomena in evenness. [N-186](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-186)  
[U04318](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04318) Where is the nature of phenomena of abiding to be applied?  
[U04319](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04319) What is the crucial point of the intervals of movement?  
[U04320](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04320) How is the nature of phenomena of time distinguished?  
[U04321](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04321) What is the nature of phenomena of buddhas like?  
[U04322](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04322) How does it abide at the time of sentient beings?  
[U04323](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04323) What are the crucial points of the body?  
[U04324](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04324) How does it emerge from the gates?  
[U04325](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04325) What are the characteristics of appearing in the object?  
[U04326](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04326) As what does it appear there? [N-186](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-186)  
[U04327](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04327) Having seen, by what is it decisively established?  
[U04328](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04328) Having arrived, what is the measure of completeness?  
[U04329](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04329) By what is the nature of phenomena of cause and result accomplished?  
[U04330](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04330) What is the nature of phenomena that clears in its own place?  
[U04331](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04331) What are the definitive phrases of the nature of phenomena?  
[U04332](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04332) What are its crucial points?  
[U04333](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04333) How is this crucial point of the time of arising held?  
[U04334](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04334) What is the nature of phenomena of the intermediate state like?  
[U04335](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04335) How do its characteristics appear?  
[U04336](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04336) Speak of these for the benefit of sentient beings.’  
[U04337](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04337) Then, [the words of] the teacher Vajradhara,  
[U04338](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04338) from the space of pure intrinsic nature—  
[U04339](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04339) these elaborations of words and phrases—  
[U04340](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04340) arose in the Ground free from expression. [N-186](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-186)  
[U04341](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04341) ‘Ema! Marvelous wonder!  
[U04342](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04342) I shall teach an expression of wonder  
[U04343](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04343) unheard of in other fields.  

[U04344](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04344) [Source heading: First subject of inquiry.]  
[U04345](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04345) The nature of phenomena of abiding is as follows:  
[U04346](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04346) abiding in its fundamental disposition, it is free from virtue and wrongdoing;  
[U04347](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04347) abiding as cause and result, it is free from dependence;  
[U04348](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04348) abiding through conditions, the elements are pure—  
[U04349](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04349) it does not shift or change. [N-187](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-187)  

[U04350](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04350) [Source heading: Second subject of inquiry.]  
[U04351](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04351) The nature of phenomena of the intervals of movement is like this:  
[U04352](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04352) I shall explain it fully as a great wonder.  
[U04353](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04353) Wind, according to the sequence of a day,  
[U04354](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04354) is reckoned as twenty thousand, one thousand,  
[U04355](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04355) and six hundred;  
[U04356](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04356) the winds of one day are thereby complete.  
[U04357](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04357) According to the particular distinctions,  
[U04358](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04358) for the young there are sixty extra;  
[U04359](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04359) for the old there is a shortfall.  
[U04360](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04360) This is through the activity of the elemental vessel. [N-188](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-188)  
[U04361](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04361) Among sixty movements of wind,  
[U04362](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04362) the subtle movement of the nature of phenomena is held through reckoning.  
[U04363](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04363) From two such groups it is coarse;  
[U04364](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04364) when these are gathered as three, it is gross.  
[U04365](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04365) Subtract three from that: it is even. [N-188](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-188)  
[U04366](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04366) In brief, within twenty movements,  
[U04367](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04367) there is one evenness of the nature of phenomena.  

[U04368](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04368) [Source heading: Third subject of inquiry.]  
[U04369](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04369) The nature of phenomena of time is as follows:  
[U04370](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04370) outer, inner, secret,  
[U04371](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04371) and suchness—four.  
[U04372](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04372) The outer is the second [month] of autumn,  
[U04373](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04373) showing the abiding of the nature of phenomena of time.  
[U04374](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04374) The inner is the time when dawn breaks.  
[U04375](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04375) The secret is sleep and dreams.  
[U04376](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04376) Suchness is held through wind.  
[U04377](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04377) When the time is reached, it is complete. [N-188](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-188)  

[U04378](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04378) [Source heading: Fourth subject of inquiry.]  
[U04379](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04379) The nature of phenomena of buddhas:  
[U04380](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04380) undivided, not dividing, even—  
[U04381](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04381) how could words, phrases, and names exist there?  
[U04382](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04382) The basis of dependence is self-purified, and delusion is purified.  
[U04383](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04383) It is not known by the names samsara and nirvana.  
[U04384](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04384) Not having come about or arisen, it does not cease.  
[U04385](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04385) All-arising primordial knowing is not any [thing]. [N-187](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-187)  
[U04386](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04386) There is no taking and no liberation.  
[U04387](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04387) In a moment free from causes and conditions,  
[U04388](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04388) buddhas and sentient beings appear as a foundation,  
[U04389](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04389) free from all multiplicity and parts. [N-187](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-187)  

[U04390](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04390) [Source heading: Fifth subject of inquiry.]  
[U04391](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04391) As for deluded sentient beings:  
[U04392](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04392) embodiments and primordial knowing are in their own manner,  
[U04393](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04393) genuine, fresh, and letting be—  
[U04394](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04394) abiding solely in ‘gyi nar’ [unresolved expression],  
[U04395](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04395) intrinsic nature at the faculties' objects. [N-189](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-189)  
[U04396](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04396) Here their own object is shown;  
[U04397](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04397) without entering into surrounding conditions,  
[U04398](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04398) in unobstructedness and penetration,  
[U04399](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04399) from the appearance aspects of object and knowing,  
[U04400](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04400) each is directly liberated from its own Ground. [N-189](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-189)  
[U04401](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04401) The desirable qualities are self-complete.  
[U04402](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04402) In the respective crucial points of diverse movement,  
[U04403](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04403) knowing one, one abides liberated.  
[U04404](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04404) Thus the nature of phenomena is realized,  
[U04405](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04405) free from parts.  

[U04406](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04406) [Source heading: Sixth subject of inquiry.]  
[U04407](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04407) The crucial points of the body are as follows:  
[U04408](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04408) the general [body], the heart, and the channels—  
[U04409](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04409) the pure, uncontrived nature of phenomena abides there. [N-190](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-190)  
[U04410](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04410) Throughout the bodies of all sentient beings,  
[U04411](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04411) the nature of phenomena pervades in the manner of wind.  
[U04412](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04412) Subtle, light, moving, and separating:  
[U04413](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04413) through these four ways of functioning,  
[U04414](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04414) know the yoga of the four times.  
[U04415](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04415) Within the heart it abides as embodiment.  
[U04416](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04416) The embodiments of radiance have five aspects;  
[U04417](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04417) they hold the characteristics of their respective families.  
[U04418](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04418) The six embodiments of rays  
[U04419](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04419) gather the phenomena of intrinsic nature's appearance.  
[U04420](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04420) Through the three aspects of pure embodiment,  
[U04421](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04421) the characteristics of passing beyond sorrow are held.  
[U04422](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04422) Through the eight aspect-embodiments,  
[U04423](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04423) the doing of completing grounds and paths is done. [N-190](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-190)  
[U04424](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04424) Within the channels are spheres.  
[U04425](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04425) Through three aspects of entering spheres,  
[U04426](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04426) the seeds connecting samsara and nirvana are planted.  
[U04427](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04427) Through the five arising spheres,  
[U04428](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04428) the mandala of yogic experience is established.  
[U04429](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04429) Through the six arraying spheres,  
[U04430](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04430) the manifold nature of phenomena is gathered into one.  
[U04431](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04431) Through reliance on each of these,  
[U04432](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04432) all phenomena have the identity of evenness. [N-190](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-190)  

[U04433](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04433) [Source heading: Seventh reply.]  
[U04434](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04434) As for how it emerges from the gates:  
[U04435](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04435) impelled by entering wind,  
[U04436](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04436) the four lamps become four activities.  
[U04437](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04437) Moved by pervading wind,  
[U04438](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04438) vajra chains [arise] from awareness itself.  
[U04439](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04439) Gathered by ripening wind,  
[U04440](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04440) they appear as embodiments in fivefold pairs. [N-191](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-191)  
[U04441](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04441) From the eyes, the two winds of apprehended object and apprehending subject:  
[U04442](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04442) through the right, the apprehended aspect, appearance increases;  
[U04443](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04443) through the left, the apprehending aspect, colors are complete. [N-191](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-191)  
[U04444](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04444) At the ears, from pervading and entering winds,  
[U04445](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04445) the right, through pervasion, displays sounds;  
[U04446](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04446) the left, through the activity of entering wind,  
[U04447](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04447) holds each respective [sound].  
[U04448](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04448) The wind of complete pervasion at the crown,  
[U04449](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04449) subtle and without generation,  
[U04450](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04450) also holds the embodiments of awareness. [N-191](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-191)  

[U04451](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04451) [Source heading: Eighth reply.]  
[U04452](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04452) The characteristics of appearing in the object:  
[U04453](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04453) from space, clear blue and unstirred,  
[U04454](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04454) light, colors, and shapes  
[U04455](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04455) display the self-appearance of the five primordial knowings.  
[U04456](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04456) From the lamp of pure basic space,  
[U04457](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04457) spheres, embodiments, and purified delusory appearance—  
[U04458](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04458) intrinsic nature itself and essence itself—  
[U04459](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04459) appear nondually, without joining or separating. [N-191](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-191)  
[U04460](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04460) At this time the characteristic of exhaustion  
[U04461](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04461) is just like space, free from doing.  

[U04462](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04462) [Source heading: Ninth reply.]  
[U04463](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04463) As for by what it is seen:  
[U04464](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04464) it is seen through familiarity and the crucial points.  
[U04465](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04465) Familiarity starts with the preliminaries;  
[U04466](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04466) applying the crucial points of body and voice,  
[U04467](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04467) one sees the forms of pure self-appearance,  
[U04468](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04468) and all delusion subsides.  
[U04469](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04469) Through the body at the crucial point, seeing occurs.  
[U04470](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04470) Through the lion posture of the dharma embodiment,  
[U04471](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04471) free from all fear of delusion,  
[U04472](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04472) one sees with the vajra eye.  
[U04473](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04473) Through the posture of the complete enjoyment embodiment,  
[U04474](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04474) relying on the reclining elephant,  
[U04475](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04475) one fully enjoys the nature of phenomena  
[U04476](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04476) and sees with the lotus eye.  
[U04477](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04477) Through the posture of the emanation embodiment,  
[U04478](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04478) relying on the crouching sage,  
[U04479](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04479) the nature of phenomena emanates of itself,  
[U04480](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04480) and one sees with the dharma eye. [N-192](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-192)  
[U04481](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04481) Furthermore, through channels and bodily exercises,  
[U04482](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04482) one sees the five primordial knowings of intrinsic nature's appearance  
[U04483](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04483) with the water-bubble eyes. [N-192](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-192)  

[U04484](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04484) [Source heading: Tenth reply.]  
[U04485](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04485) Having seen, one applies the crucial point.  
[U04486](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04486) Through methods of guiding appearance,  
[U04487](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04487) the appearances of the four elements reach the crucial point.  
[U04488](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04488) By reckoning the connections of the eight winds' activities,  
[U04489](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04489) manifold differentiating conceptualization reaches the crucial point.  
[U04490](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04490) For the body: compressing, shaking out expressiveness,  
[U04491](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04491) and the crucial points of striking, extending, and gathering the limbs;  
[U04492](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04492) through subtlety, straightening, and massage—  
[U04493](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04493) in flesh, blood, and bone. [N-192](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-192)  

[U04494](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04494) [Source heading: Eleventh reply.]  
[U04495](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04495) Having thus arrived, the measures of completeness are:  
[U04496](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04496) cessation of what appears directly before one;  
[U04497](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04497) introducing appearance into earth and stone;  
[U04498](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04498) one's own knowing entering anything;  
[U04499](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04499) through that entry, moving insentient things;  
[U04500](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04500) taking the measure of wind's movement; [N-193](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-193)  
[U04501](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04501) seeing the body's minute particles arises;  
[U04502](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04502) whatever appears is complete as form embodiment;  
[U04503](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04503) there, father and mother embrace;  
[U04504](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04504) fivefold fathers and mothers join;  
[U04505](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04505) all are within a round encircling band.  
[U04506](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04506) The yogin's body too is clear light;  
[U04507](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04507) going, coming, and abiding—  
[U04508](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04508) all are awareness reaching its measure. [N-194](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-194)  

[U04509](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04509) [Source heading: Twelfth reply.]  
[U04510](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04510) The manner of reaching the measure also becomes like this:  
[U04511](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04511) free from the particles of each body,  
[U04512](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04512) windows of light appear in the intervening space.  
[U04513](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04513) From these, rays shaped like hooks,  
[U04514](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04514) subtle and entwining, hold appearance.  
[U04515](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04515) The body's material weight self-ceases:  
[U04516](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04516) a stainless body of light with unimpeded penetration.  
[U04517](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04517) At its center is the sign of A,  
[U04518](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04518) and rays from the brow curl measure one armspan.  
[U04519](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04519) The topknot appears held back by wind. [Source variant: ‘lifted.’] [N-194](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-194)  
[U04520](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04520) From the fingers of the peaceful embodiments,  
[U04521](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04521) garlands of light gather densely.  
[U04522](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04522) There is activity of ordinary mind without sound.  
[U04523](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04523) From the palace of the skull in the head,  
[U04524](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04524) Herukas manifestly arise.  
[U04525](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04525) At this time one's body is brought to its measure:  
[U04526](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04526) the three embodiments, with unimpeded penetration, gathered into one. [N-194](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-194)  

[U04527](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04527) [Source heading: Thirteenth reply.]  
[U04528](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04528) At this time ordinary mind is clear;  
[U04529](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04529) through the six higher knowings,  
[U04530](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04530) phenomena that are far away or measurable  
[U04531](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04531) are known in an instant.  
[U04532](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04532) Immeasurable buddha fields  
[U04533](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04533) and likewise embodiments appear to ordinary mind.  
[U04534](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04534) In empty ordinary mind, stains are exhausted.  
[U04535](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04535) Intrinsic nature does not move; emptiness and clarity are even.  
[U04536](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04536) Unmixed, complete in evenness, it pervades basic space.  
[U04537](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04537) Appearance and existence are clear, free from multiplicity.  
[U04538](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04538) Without parts, there is no elaboration. [N-195](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-195)  

[U04539](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04539) [Source heading: Fourteenth reply.]  
[U04540](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04540) At the times of such appearances,  
[U04541](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04541) the connection between body and ordinary mind is severed.  
[U04542](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04542) Even in the body whose contaminated [elements] are exhausted,  
[U04543](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04543) ordinary mind, characterized by clarity, emerges outward.  
[U04544](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04544) In the manner of a shooting star passing across,  
[U04545](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04545) it is clearly seen in the element of space. [N-195](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-195)  
[U04546](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04546) At the initial times of this,  
[U04547](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04547) even in separation from wind,  
[U04548](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04548) through effort and the activity of pure wind,  
[U04549](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04549) it arises from being projected outside the body.  
[U04550](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04550) Through the two aspects of what sees,  
[U04551](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04551) the body is pure clear light;  
[U04552](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04552) ordinary mind is like proliferating sparks,  
[U04553](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04553) not abiding in one place, a Ground of its own clarity.  
[U04554](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04554) It arises in the manner of two appearance aspects.  
[U04555](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04555) Through this, the connection between body and ordinary mind is severed,  
[U04556](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04556) and one does not return to the three realms. [N-195](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-195)  

[U04557](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04557) [Source heading: Fifteenth reply.]  
[U04558](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04558) The nature of phenomena of cause and result:  
[U04559](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04559) when the source of coarse conceptual thought has ceased,  
[U04560](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04560) in the continuum of self-purified mindfulness,  
[U04561](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04561) dependent connections and wind itself are completely pure.  
[U04562](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04562) It is the nature of phenomena arising from wind. [N-196](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-196)  
[U04563](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04563) In intrinsic nature, pure from the beginning,  
[U04564](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04564) the numbers one and many are exhausted.  
[U04565](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04565) There are no embodiments or primordial knowing;  
[U04566](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04566) having reached the limit where phenomena are exhausted,  
[U04567](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04567) this is held to be the nature of phenomena of the result. [N-196](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-196)  

[U04568](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04568) [Source heading: Sixteenth reply.]  
[U04569](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04569) The nature of phenomena that clears in its own place:  
[U04570](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04570) when the elements clear, material weight is purified;  
[U04571](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04571) particles, subtle and coarse, cease at their limit,  
[U04572](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04572) and not even a mere part abides.  
[U04573](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04573) When afflictions clear, delusion clears.  
[U04574](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04574) Free from conceptual thought, clinging does not abide;  
[U04575](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04575) neither dormancy nor manifest arising—  
[U04576](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04576) not even a particle's portion abides.  
[U04577](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04577) Through the clearing of subtle and coarse in this way,  
[U04578](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04578) the five aggregates gradually become subtle.  
[U04579](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04579) When the body of the four elements is exhausted,  
[U04580](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04580) all is embodiment complete in primordial knowing.  
[U04581](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04581) Deep absorption free from conceptualization abides of itself.  
[U04582](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04582) Beyond contrived phenomena,  
[U04583](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04583) it does not abide even in the phrase ‘phenomena.’ [N-196](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-196)  

[U04584](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04584) [Source heading: Seventeenth reply.]  
[U04585](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04585) The definitive phrase ‘nature of phenomena’ is thus:  
[U04586](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04586) ‘phenomena’ means gathering and assembling,  
[U04587](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04587) doing doings and holding characteristics,  
[U04588](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04588) showing self-appearance  
[U04589](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04589) and holding the respective classes.  
[U04590](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04590) ‘Nature’ means no contrivance;  
[U04591](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04591) abiding in letting be, it genuinely pervades.  
[U04592](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04592) Fresh, genuine, and in its original course,  
[U04593](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04593) the number of words, phrases, and names is exhausted. [N-197](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-197)  

[U04594](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04594) [Source heading: Eighteenth reply.]  
[U04595](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04595) The nature of phenomena of the crucial points is as follows:  
[U04596](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04596) the elements' abodes, one's body,  
[U04597](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04597) objects, gates, knowing, wind,  
[U04598](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04598) channels, spheres, and subsidiary aspects—  
[U04599](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04599) each respective entry abides pervasively.  
[U04600](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04600) Through transforming their respective preliminaries  
[U04601](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04601) and the abiding of the main practice,  
[U04602](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04602) in the nature of phenomena free from conceptual thought,  
[U04603](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04603) intrinsic nature abides and movement is exhausted.  
[U04604](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04604) From the object in which exhaustion and appearance are nondual,  
[U04605](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04605) through intrinsic abiding and contrived [practice],  
[U04606](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04606) the basic space of phenomena also appears through purity.  
[U04607](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04607) Through this, buddhahood comes through the crucial points. [N-197](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-197)  

[U04608](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04608) [Source heading: Nineteenth reply.]  
[U04609](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04609) The crucial point of the time of arising is like this:  
[U04610](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04610) Ema! I shall explain a great wonder.  
[U04611](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04611) This nature of phenomena has eight times of arising,  
[U04612](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04612) and just as many times of abiding.  
[U04613](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04613) Also at four distinctive times,  
[U04614](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04614) when the yogin's ordinary mind abides,  
[U04615](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04615) there is evenness without joining or separating.  
[U04616](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04616) Knowing the crucial point of pure intrinsic nature,  
[U04617](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04617) one knows phenomena without anything being done.  
[U04618](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04618) Time is held through the number of transitions;  
[U04619](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04619) through holding it, there is purity without delusion. [N-197](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-197)  

[U04620](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04620) [Source heading: Twentieth reply, in full.]  
[U04621](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04621) The nature of phenomena of the intermediate state is like this:  
[U04622](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04622) I shall explain the most secret nature of phenomena.  
[U04623](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04623) Distinguished, there are four aspects;  
[U04624](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04624) here I shall explain the intermediate state of the nature of phenomena.  
[U04625](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04625) When released from the moment of death,  
[U04626](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04626) consciousness dissolves into space.  
[U04627](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04627) When space dissolves into clear light,  
[U04628](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04628) all subtle and coarse appearances cease.  
[U04629](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04629) A body of light endowed with all faculties  
[U04630](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04630) becomes like a shadow from the body. [N-198](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-198)  
[U04631](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04631) At that time, when light appears in fragments,  
[U04632](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04632) the adept holds the spreading of appearance.  
[U04633](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04633) Through the spreading of moving wind,  
[U04634](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04634) light and rays of five colors  
[U04635](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04635) appear in uncertain rainbow patterns.  
[U04636](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04636) From entering and ripening winds,  
[U04637](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04637) the four directions, intermediate directions, above and below are established.  
[U04638](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04638) As the directions' lights gather there,  
[U04639](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04639) embodiments of the five families also manifest.  
[U04640](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04640) Blue, white, yellow, red, and green  
[U04641](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04641) appear with their respective objects.  
[U04642](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04642) Knowing how to spread and hold them,  
[U04643](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04643) one completes the buddhas' qualities  
[U04644](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04644) and does not enter the three realms. [N-198](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-198)  

[U04645](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04645) [Source heading: Twenty-first reply.]  
[U04646](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04646) Their characteristics are like this:  
[U04647](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04647) free from dependence on coarse elements,  
[U04648](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04648) spheres blaze as rays in the light.  
[U04649](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04649) Five connected spheres are single embodiments;  
[U04650](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04650) from half-embodiments, forms become complete.  
[U04651](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04651) From embodiments with half-forms, there are fivefold pairs.  
[U04652](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04652) As each cluster becomes complete,  
[U04653](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04653) there are groups of five, ten, and a hundred;  
[U04654](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04654) clusters of a thousand and a hundred thousand  
[U04655](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04655) appear pure in the intrinsic nature of what is envisaged. [N-198](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-198)  
[U04656](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04656) Innumerable, beyond what expression can encompass—  
[U04657](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04657) when one oneself knows their intrinsic nature,  
[U04658](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04658) one attains the three embodiments gathered into one.  
[U04659](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04659) From this attainment there is no return.  
[U04660](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04660) It comes from making this clear. [N-199](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-199)  
[U04661](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04661) Furthermore, the essence of the nature of phenomena  
[U04662](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04662) is established as essence from intrinsic nature itself.  
[U04663](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04663) Since its manner of appearance is not fixed as one,  
[U04664](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04664) the paths of entry also differ.  
[U04665](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04665) Through identity, knowing, and seeing liberation,  
[U04666](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04666) through entering, seeing, and familiarity—  
[U04667](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04667) from recognition, there is one's own identity;  
[U04668](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04668) through trust, one reaches it;  
[U04669](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04669) through decision, one enters confidence.  
[U04670](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04670) Through these three certainties,  
[U04671](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04671) essence being complete, the continuum ceases. [N-199](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-199)  
[U04672](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04672) Furthermore, I shall explain the intrinsic nature of the nature of phenomena.  
[U04673](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04673) Not fixed as one thing of this kind,  
[U04674](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04674) it appears however it is designated.  
[U04675](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04675) From the basis for assigning diverse names,  
[U04676](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04676) it appears as elaborations of many words and phrases.  
[U04677](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04677) Names are uncertain and diverse;  
[U04678](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04678) meaning too is empty through mere designation.  
[U04679](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04679) Since emptiness is not established as anything,  
[U04680](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04680) its intrinsic nature appears pure. [N-199](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-199)  
[U04681](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04681) Furthermore, I shall explain the compassionate responsiveness of the nature of phenomena.  
[U04682](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04682) Since the domains of those to be trained are diverse,  
[U04683](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04683) the embodiments that train them are likewise.  
[U04684](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04684) Through the transformations of classes and faculties,  
[U04685](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04685) conceptual thoughts of samsara and nirvana differ.  
[U04686](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04686) When each is left in its own object,  
[U04687](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04687) this is enlightened intent of the uncontrived meaning.  
[U04688](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04688) Furthermore, I shall explain the appearance of the nature of phenomena.  
[U04689](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04689) In impure delusion,  
[U04690](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04690) without contriving the gates of the faculties,  
[U04691](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04691) the crucial point is leaving [them] as they are;  
[U04692](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04692) not altering that is the pith instruction. [N-200](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-200)  
[U04693](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04693) In appearance without an apprehended object,  
[U04694](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04694) ordinary mind arises without an apprehending subject.  
[U04695](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04695) It is seen by eyes without activity;  
[U04696](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04696) drawn along a path without a nature of phenomena;  
[U04697](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04697) carried to a Ground without view and cultivation;  
[U04698](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04698) gathered into a result with nothing to do, free from effort.  
[U04699](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04699) Here the limits of appearance do not abide. [N-200](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-200)  
[U04700](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04700) Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of awareness.  
[U04701](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04701) Through naked seeing in vajra chains,  
[U04702](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04702) the mindfulness of moving differentiating conceptualization ceases.  
[U04703](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04703) Like going in space along the birds' path,  
[U04704](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04704) there is nowhere to go from the basic space of phenomena.  
[U04705](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04705) With no other, awareness-appearance being completely pure,  
[U04706](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04706) the causes and conditions of delusive appearance are exhausted.  
[U04707](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04707) Since basic space and awareness are inseparable,  
[U04708](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04708) the connected appearance of vajra chains is shown.  
[U04709](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04709) The essence of awareness is vajra chains:  
[U04710](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04710) in past, future, or present,  
[U04711](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04711) no one has made them.  
[U04712](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04712) Thus being unconditioned  
[U04713](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04713) is the intrinsic nature of vajra chains.  
[U04714](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04714) All differentiating conceptualization being pure,  
[U04715](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04715) there is buddhahood without delusion. [N-200](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-200)  
[U04716](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04716) Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of the continuum.  
[U04717](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04717) The true nature of phenomena penetrates beyond words;  
[U04718](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04718) its own essence is without letters.  
[U04719](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04719) The lamp-light of self-aware primordial knowing blazes.  
[U04720](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04720) With the nature of phenomena pure, auspiciousness is beautiful.  
[U04721](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04721) The view, free from fear, completes the lion's expressiveness;  
[U04722](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04722) with self-awareness ripened, completeness arises naturally;  
[U04723](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04723) vajra chains connect, a garland of pearls;  
[U04724](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04724) appearances of experience form an array of jeweled inlay;  
[U04725](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04725) self-abiding emptiness is the mirror of the core;  
[U04726](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04726) deviations are cut of themselves: the mirror of awakened mind.  
[U04727](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04727) Not arising from another, it is self-arising itself.  
[U04728](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04728) Because it arises naturally, there is self-liberation.  
[U04729](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04729) One in basic space, there are also six expanses.  
[U04730](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04730) With qualities complete, precious things are heaped.  
[U04731](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04731) Self-awareness being taught, it is adorned with pointing out.  
[U04732](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04732) With appearance pure, sun and moon are joined.  
[U04733](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04733) Because awareness ripens, embodiment-relics blaze.  
[U04734](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04734) All of these arise from the Ground. [N-201](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-201)  
[U04735](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04735) Thus, because the nature of phenomena is inexpressible,  
[U04736](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04736) it arises as the foundation of conventional phrases.  
[U04737](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04737) The sequences of expressed phrases being completely pure,  
[U04738](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04738) one thus abides in the inexpressible.  
[U04739](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04739) Therefore the limits of phrase-sequences are exhausted.  
[U04740](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04740) Furthermore, I shall explain the nature of phenomena of liberation.  
[U04741](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04741) Liberated through the crucial points, striving and effort are exhausted.  
[U04742](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04742) Liberated from the beginning, there is no need to repeat.  
[U04743](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04743) Because it is self-liberation, there is no antidote.  
[U04744](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04744) Because it is naked liberation, it vanishes where it is seen.  
[U04745](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04745) Completely liberated, intrinsic nature is pure.  
[U04746](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04746) Liberated through time, there is no need for familiarity.  
[U04747](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04747) Liberated through intrinsic nature, it is uncontrived.  
[U04748](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04748) ‘Liberated’ is merely a convention.  
[U04749](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04749) Whose phenomena are realization and non-realization?  
[U04750](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04750) For whom there is ‘liberated,’ what is the object of focus? [N-202](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-202)  
[U04751](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04751) For whom could entry into the three realms occur?  
[U04752](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04752) This is the nature of phenomena liberated from the limits of existence.  
[U04753](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04753) Furthermore, the nature of phenomena free from doing:  
[U04754](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04754) with no doing, there is liberation;  
[U04755](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04755) with no arising, there is no abiding.  
[U04756](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04756) With no going, coming and circling are exhausted.  
[U04757](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04757) There is neither one nor two of what is done or arisen.  
[U04758](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04758) There is not even mere conventional delusion.  
[U04759](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04759) It is empty of entry into activities of existence and nonexistence.  
[U04760](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04760) From the beginning, the result is free from the conceptual mind,  
[U04761](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04761) beyond all speaking, thinking, and expression. [N-203](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-203)  
[U04762](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04762) Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:  
[U04763](alignment/chapter-04.md#u04763) the fourth chapter, the Array of the Nature of Phenomena, teaching the root of ordinary mind's appearance.  

<a id="part-5"></a>

## Chapter 5

[U04764](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04764) Then, through completely pure intrinsic nature,  
[U04765](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04765) from the inexpressible dharma embodiment,  
[U04766](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04766) from the space of the nature of phenomena, free from conceptual elaborations,  
[U04767](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04767) from the inexpressible foundation,  
[U04768](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04768) phrases of meaning in a naturally arisen array  
[U04769](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04769) arose like light proliferating from the teacher's tongue,  
[U04770](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04770) without being made by anyone. [N-204](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-204)  
[U04771](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04771) The Lord of Gods supplicated:  
[U04772](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04772) ‘Kyema! Blessed one, lord without delusion,  
[U04773](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04773) by whom will the Array of View and Cultivation  
[U04774](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04774) be held?  
[U04775](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04775) What is the natural state established in its own disposition?  
[U04776](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04776) How should one view it?  
[U04777](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04777) Distinguish the boundary between viewing phrases and meaning.  
[U04778](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04778) What are the essence and intrinsic nature of view?  
[U04779](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04779) What are the divisions of expressed phrases and names?  
[U04780](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04780) What is that called cultivation like?  
[U04781](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04781) What are its activity and understanding?  
[U04782](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04782) What cultivation abides without deviation?  
[U04783](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04783) How do cultivation's characteristics appear?  
[U04784](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04784) What are the faults of not cultivating?  
[U04785](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04785) I wish to hear these.  
[U04786](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04786) Vajra-holder, speak to me.’  
[U04787](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04787) Then, from uncontrived space,  
[U04788](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04788) the one bearing thirty-two marks  
[U04789](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04789) spoke the Array of View and Cultivation thus:  
[U04790](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04790) ‘Lord of Gods, listen well.  
[U04791](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04791) Whoever doubts this  
[U04792](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04792) obscures the embodiments of the buddhas.  

[U04793](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04793) [Source heading: First reply.]  
[U04794](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04794) Those who hold view and cultivation  
[U04795](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04795) have excellent faith and diligence,  
[U04796](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04796) are able to give and respect the lama,  
[U04797](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04797) possess disciplined observance and avoid wrongdoing;  
[U04798](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04798) unchanging and utterly stable, they hold it.  
[U04799](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04799) Alternatively, one whose own impetus is quiet,  
[U04800](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04800) letting be, with a simple person's relaxation,  
[U04801](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04801) whose realization is small and whose intrinsic nature is quiet,  
[U04802](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04802) who is free from doings of body and voice,  
[U04803](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04803) and possesses discerning knowing, will hold it. [N-205](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-205)  

[U04804](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04804) [Source heading: Second reply.]  
[U04805](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04805) The natural state established in its own disposition  
[U04806](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04806) also has seven manners of abiding:  
[U04807](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04807) spontaneous presence from the side of diversity;  
[U04808](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04808) indeterminacy from the aspect of the extent of change;  
[U04809](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04809) certainty through not changing;  
[U04810](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04810) appearance's expressiveness transforming into anything;  
[U04811](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04811) all arising, together with affirmation;  
[U04812](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04812) primordial purity because delusion is purified;  
[U04813](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04813) and the variegation held to be diverse manners of appearance. [N-206](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-206)  
[U04814](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04814) Through such distinctions in what is asserted,  
[U04815](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04815) because the Great Perfection appears as anything,  
[U04816](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04816) although differing assertions are numerous,  
[U04817](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04817) in the view of abiding in its own disposition  
[U04818](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04818) there is no one, no two, and freedom from anything.  
[U04819](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04819) Even taking life and taking what is not given  
[U04820](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04820) arise from me and are my phenomena. [N-207](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-207)  
[U04821](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04821) View, cultivation, and activity too  
[U04822](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04822) are beyond the extremes of existence, nonexistence, and emptiness.  
[U04823](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04823) In brief, they are distinguished through characteristics.  

[U04824](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04824) [Source heading: Third reply.]  
[U04825](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04825) There are two ways of viewing this:  
[U04826](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04826) viewing phrases and viewing meaning.  
[U04827](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04827) Viewing phrases has seven stages;  
[U04828](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04828) their respective assertions are constructed by the conceptual mind:  
[U04829](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04829) freedom from doing and absence of striving;  
[U04830](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04830) gradual entry and drawing out distinctions of self and other;  
[U04831](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04831) taking life, and freedom from virtue and wrongdoing;  
[U04832](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04832) nothing done and nothing arisen;  
[U04833](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04833) unaltered appearance, self-purified;  
[U04834](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04834) and afflictions held to be liberated in intrinsic nature. [N-207](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-207)  
[U04835](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04835) Meaning is viewed in direct perception of the nature of phenomena,  
[U04836](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04836) not through discerning knowing,  
[U04837](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04837) but as the very domain of the faculties.  
[U04838](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04838) It is not decisively reached through phrases.  
[U04839](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04839) In the identity of seeing the three crucial points,  
[U04840](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04840) because the three phrases are explained as crucial points,  
[U04841](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04841) there are no sharp or dull faculties.  
[U04842](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04842) Because seeing the nature of phenomena purifies,  
[U04843](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04843) there is no good or bad in activities.  
[U04844](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04844) The nature of phenomena is empty and without divisions;  
[U04845](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04845) virtue and wrongdoing, both, are removed. [N-207](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-207)  
[U04846](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04846) Without seeing self-awareness directly,  
[U04847](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04847) the assertions of the nine stages do not liberate.  
[U04848](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04848) When the nature of phenomena is exhausted and its limit reached,  
[U04849](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04849) the three embodiments are the appearance of the path.  

[U04850](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04850) [Source heading: Fourth reply.]  
[U04851](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04851) The boundary between the views of phrases and meaning:  
[U04852](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04852) phrases are causes of expression;  
[U04853](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04853) whatever expresses or produces an emanation  
[U04854](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04854) is mental examination that thinks about it.  
[U04855](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04855) Meaning is oneself viewing oneself.  
[U04856](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04856) Through the three measures of direct perception,  
[U04857](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04857) one engages directly with vajra chains.  
[U04858](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04858) Through naked seeing and naked liberation,  
[U04859](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04859) whatever one sees is meaning. [N-208](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-208)  

[U04860](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04860) [Source heading: Fifth reply.]  
[U04861](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04861) The essence of view is seeing,  
[U04862](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04862) unchanged by phrases of mental examination.  
[U04863](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04863) Neither authoritative transmission nor the lama's pith instructions  
[U04864](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04864) shows it by any [means]. [N-208](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-208)  

[U04865](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04865) [Source heading: Sixth reply.]  
[U04866](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04866) The intrinsic nature of view pervades environment and inhabitants;  
[U04867](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04867) nothing is unpervaded, so anything is liberated.  
[U04868](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04868) In liberated phenomena, differentiated grasping is absent.  
[U04869](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04869) Whatever appears is one's own nature of phenomena;  
[U04870](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04870) there is no separate contrived nature of phenomena.  
[U04871](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04871) However one acts, non-conceptuality arises.  
[U04872](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04872) There is self-appearance without rejecting and accepting.  

[U04873](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04873) [Source heading: Seventh reply.]  
[U04874](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04874) The divisions of expressed phrases and names  
[U04875](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04875) are outer, inner, secret,  
[U04876](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04876) and unsurpassed—the four views.  
[U04877](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04877) The outer is viewing bearers of properties:  
[U04878](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04878) in the respective assertions of the eight stages,  
[U04879](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04879) mental examination and grasping are maintained. [N-209](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-209)  
[U04880](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04880) The inner: viewing the nature of phenomena,  
[U04881](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04881) one sees essence and intrinsic nature as illusion.  
[U04882](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04882) The secret: viewing self-awareness,  
[U04883](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04883) one sees its uninterrupted connection with vajra chains.  
[U04884](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04884) Suchness is basic space and awareness. [N-209](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-209)  

[U04885](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04885) [Source heading: Eighth reply.]  
[U04886](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04886) What is called cultivation is ordinary mind abiding,  
[U04887](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04887) cutting outer and inner movement,  
[U04888](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04888) and ceasing apprehended object and apprehending subject.  
[U04889](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04889) What arises from intrinsic nature's own disposition  
[U04890](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04890) is to be called meditative stabilization.  
[U04891](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04891) Wind, channels, spheres,  
[U04892](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04892) the body's crucial points, the eyes, and emptiness—  
[U04893](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04893) these are held to be cultivation.  
[U04894](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04894) The light and colors of letters,  
[U04895](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04895) and the emanations' proliferating and gathering,  
[U04896](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04896) are explained as deep absorption.  
[U04897](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04897) Uncontrived, resting of itself, is the natural state.  
[U04898](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04898) Because there is no change from that, however [it occurs],  
[U04899](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04899) it is the buddhas' enlightened intent. [N-210](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-210)  

[U04900](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04900) [Source heading: Ninth reply.]  
[U04901](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04901) Its activity and understanding:  
[U04902](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04902) through activity, clinging to objects is cut,  
[U04903](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04903) afflictions and the like are rejected,  
[U04904](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04904) and the root of samsara is drawn out.  
[U04905](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04905) Through understanding, the nature of ordinary mind is held,  
[U04906](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04906) made to abide, and wind is exhausted;  
[U04907](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04907) the path of the empty nature of phenomena is found,  
[U04908](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04908) and there is ground-appearance of pure intrinsic nature.  

[U04909](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04909) [Source heading: Tenth reply.]  
[U04910](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04910) Cultivation that abides without deviation:  
[U04911](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04911) the faculties' gates are clear,  
[U04912](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04912) and the mental faculty's conceptual thought does not move.  
[U04913](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04913) The mental faculty that examines knowing itself  
[U04914](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04914) does not escape outward.  
[U04915](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04915) It is not a clarity marked by divisions;  
[U04916](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04916) it has not gone into an emptiness cut off separately;  
[U04917](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04917) it is not a continuous succession of bliss;  
[U04918](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04918) it has not become a stagnant pool of equanimity. [N-211](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-211)  
[U04919](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04919) Resting in clear mindfulness and awareness;  
[U04920](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04920) training's contrary aspects having become the path;  
[U04921](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04921) an apprehending aspect with a continuous inward ease;  
[U04922](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04922) an apprehending aspect clear in its manner of parts;  
[U04923](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04923) and casting out colors and syllables—  
[U04924](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04924) when one has not fallen to the side of any of these,  
[U04925](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04925) this is cultivation abiding without deviation. [N-211](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-211)  

[U04926](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04926) [Source heading: Eleventh reply.]  
[U04927](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04927) The characteristics and manner of appearance of cultivation:  
[U04928](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04928) its characteristic is empty, clear awareness;  
[U04929](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04929) its intrinsic nature is mixed with nothing;  
[U04930](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04930) the limits of apprehended object and apprehending subject are exhausted;  
[U04931](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04931) and the nature of phenomena is pure in its own identity.  
[U04932](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04932) Its manner of appearance is not delusion.  
[U04933](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04933) In the intrinsic nature of pure causes and conditions,  
[U04934](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04934) things and absence of things are self-purified;  
[U04935](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04935) clinging to the objects of the five gates is purified;  
[U04936](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04936) the order of the elements is purified;  
[U04937](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04937) and the increasing appearance of pure primordial knowing is shown.  
[U04938](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04938) There is the nature of phenomena of exhaustion. [N-212](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-212)  

[U04939](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04939) [Source heading: Twelfth reply.]  
[U04940](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04940) The faults of not cultivating are:  
[U04941](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04941) the characteristics of samsara manifest;  
[U04942](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04942) self and other, objects and knowing,  
[U04943](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04943) a view bound to phrases,  
[U04944](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04944) and objects bound to focus—  
[U04945](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04945) afflictions bind [one] as a self.  
[U04946](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04946) The path of buddhahood is also lost;  
[U04947](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04947) the intrinsic nature of the result is not known;  
[U04948](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04948) there is no Ground of the evenness of all phenomena;  
[U04949](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04949) self-awareness is bound in the three realms;  
[U04950](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04950) and one falls into what is accompanied by conceptual thought. [N-212](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-212)  

[U04951](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04951) [Source heading: Below this, the condensed meaning is explained.]  
[U04952](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04952) Furthermore, I shall explain the wheel of the nature of phenomena.  
[U04953](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04953) Through the view of abiding, all phenomena are realized.  
[U04954](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04954) With intrinsic nature pure, samsara and nirvana are distinguished.  
[U04955](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04955) With nothing done, there is arising.  
[U04956](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04956) Brought onto the true path, it pervades.  
[U04957](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04957) Completely arisen, the nature of phenomena is empty.  
[U04958](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04958) With no doing, non-arising is complete.  
[U04959](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04959) Samsara being purified, there is passing beyond sorrow.  
[U04960](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04960) Furthermore, the view of pure seeing:  
[U04961](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04961) the great completeness of self-purified absence of things.  
[U04962](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04962) There is no need to make offerings to deities;  
[U04963](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04963) there is no mantra recitation or austerity;  
[U04964](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04964) no incense, lamps, or food offerings to deities;  
[U04965](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04965) no gathering offerings or offerings to the lama;  
[U04966](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04966) no praise or burnt offerings;  
[U04967](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04967) no scope of activity of the six perfections.  
[U04968](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04968) Thus, with no Ground or root whatsoever:  
[U04969](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04969) taking life, taking what is not given, and unchaste activity— [N-213](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-213)  
[U04970](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04970) there is no view, no cultivation, and no activity.  
[U04971](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04971) With nothing to view or enact, what assertion of cultivation is there?  
[U04972](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04972) Ordinary mind's assertions of emptiness and clarity cease.  
[U04973](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04973) Likewise, phenomena and the phenomena of buddhahood are absent. [N-213](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-213)  
[U04974](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04974) Furthermore, the view of intrinsic nature:  
[U04975](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04975) the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature, without things.  
[U04976](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04976) Earth, water, fire, and wind—the elements—are manifestly buddha.  
[U04977](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04977) Without contrivance, there is great self-liberation itself.  
[U04978](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04978) Desire, hatred, dull confusion, and awakening—four. [N-214](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-214)  
[U04979](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04979) The activity of differentiating conceptualization and karma is confidence in realization.  
[U04980](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04980) Without rejecting samsara, there is the buddhas' enlightened intent.  
[U04981](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04981) Body, voice, and mental faculty being refined, the three embodiments are complete.  
[U04982](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04982) Ordinary mind, ignorance, and delusion are the Ground of the nature of phenomena.  
[U04983](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04983) Karma, habitual tendencies, and clinging are crucial points of view. [N-214](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-214)  
[U04984](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04984) Furthermore, the contrived view:  
[U04985](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04985) for a buddha that has not arisen from phenomena,  
[U04986](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04986) a result complete without being generated by a cause,  
[U04987](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04987) one takes a body not destroyed by conditions.  
[U04988](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04988) With tenets not arising from authoritative transmission,  
[U04989](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04989) and pith instructions not relying on the continuum,  
[U04990](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04990) one cuts the root of passing beyond sorrow,  
[U04991](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04991) and makes the result of samsara ripen.  
[U04992](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04992) An untrue, impure Ground—  
[U04993](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04993) in the foundation of the six afflictions,  
[U04994](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04994) the seed of awakening is emptied out.  
[U04995](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04995) Forms of the three lower destinies ripen,  
[U04996](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04996) and the three embodiments too are ‘lag bcangs’ [unresolved]. [N-215](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-215)  
[U04997](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04997) Furthermore, I shall explain the view of what is expressed.  
[U04998](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04998) If life is not cut, one does not become buddha. [N-216](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-216)  
[U04999](alignment/chapter-05.md#u04999) If the windows are not closed,  
[U05000](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05000) the darkness itself is not cleared. [N-216](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-216)  
[U05001](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05001) The teacher of the buddhas,  
[U05002](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05002) at the king of Dharma in hell,  
[U05003](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05003) leads through Dharma to the three lower destinies.  
[U05004](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05004) Through practicing wrongdoing, virtue is attained. [N-217](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-217)  
[U05005](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05005) Through viewing, [one enters] samsara in the three realms;  
[U05006](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05006) through cultivating, one gathers all the hosts of afflictions;  
[U05007](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05007) through enacting activity, [one enters] deviation and obscuration;  
[U05008](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05008) the result is the very cause of samsara. [N-217](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-217)  
[U05009](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05009) Furthermore, I shall explain the connection of symbolic meaning and phrases.  
[U05010](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05010) In the midst of an expanse of the conflagration of an age,  
[U05011](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05011) a person, without being burned,  
[U05012](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05012) expounds the Dharma of the scriptural collections from his mouth,  
[U05013](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05013) while in action cutting another's life—  
[U05014](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05014) and his virtuous practice increases. How strange! [N-217](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-217)  
[U05015](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05015) Inside a spacious iron house without a door,  
[U05016](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05016) in the midst of an expanse of lightless darkness,  
[U05017](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05017) although sun, moon, and clear light are there,  
[U05018](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05018) no one sees them.  
[U05019](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05019) The objects of each one's faculties are clear,  
[U05020](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05020) yet there is no seeing. How strange! [N-217](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-217)  
[U05021](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05021) Furthermore, I shall explain the view that apprehends objects.  
[U05022](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05022) Emptying the Ground of samsara in the three realms,  
[U05023](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05023) one sweeps away passing beyond sorrow.  
[U05024](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05024) Cutting the root of ignorance and samsara,  
[U05025](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05025) one uproots the result of the three embodiments.  
[U05026](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05026) Putting on the armor of darkness, one extinguishes the sun;  
[U05027](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05027) the clear means of seeing is covered by darkness.  
[U05028](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05028) Giving an edge its sharp temper, not even one [thing] is cut.  
[U05029](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05029) With great breadth and an empty center, not even one [thing] fits.  
[U05030](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05030) Gathering diversity, one is not found.  
[U05031](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05031) Conceptual thought at the extremes of existence and nonexistence is cleared. [N-218](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-218)  
[U05032](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05032) Furthermore, I shall explain the view of desiring ordinary mind.  
[U05033](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05033) Striking all, not even one is cut;  
[U05034](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05034) striking one, all are to be killed.  
[U05035](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05035) A hundred people in iron fetters—not one is freed;  
[U05036](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05036) holding one, all are freed.  
[U05037](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05037) Through freedom, the path of liberation above is exhausted.  
[U05038](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05038) The cause of entering activity ceases. [N-218](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-218)  
[U05039](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05039) Furthermore, I shall explain the wheel of activity.  
[U05040](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05040) Since there is no arising, there is no self;  
[U05041](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05041) neither taking nor casting away is the seal of embodiment.  
[U05042](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05042) Do not search for elements; do not reject what exists.  
[U05043](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05043) Arouse desire, but do not become attached.  
[U05044](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05044) Having become attached, let go.  
[U05045](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05045) Do not disparage another's body or set [your own] above it.  
[U05046](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05046) Do not interrupt the flow of mantra and gestures.  
[U05047](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05047) Do not reject tenets; do not reject Dharma.  
[U05048](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05048) Do not disparage companions or the lama. [N-219](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-219)  
[U05049](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05049) Furthermore, I shall explain pledges and vows.  
[U05050](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05050) Lamas and buddhas are supports for offerings;  
[U05051](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05051) to Dharma, the community, chosen deities,  
[U05052](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05052) and vajra ḍākinīs,  
[U05053](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05053) do not cut the flow of gathering offerings and other offerings.  
[U05054](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05054) On seeing a lama endowed with the characteristics,  
[U05055](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05055) make praise and offerings complete.  
[U05056](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05056) Do not disparage women; do not become angry.  
[U05057](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05057) Do not disparage the aggregates; rely on the chosen deity.  
[U05058](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05058) Relying on mandalas, mantras, deities,  
[U05059](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05059) and gestures is authentic.  
[U05060](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05060) Do not break up gatherings of the faithful;  
[U05061](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05061) do not disturb the ordinary minds of those possessing virtue.  
[U05062](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05062) Possessing vows, abide in the pledges. [N-219](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-219)  
[U05063](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05063) Furthermore, the stages of training:  
[U05064](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05064) complete the body's activity,  
[U05065](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05065) and thoroughly cut speech.  
[U05066](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05066) Let ordinary mind abide, and examine and investigate it.  
[U05067](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05067) In the stages of progressively entering activities,  
[U05068](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05068) know the measures and characteristics.  
[U05069](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05069) Constantly, through ointments and pills,  
[U05070](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05070) introduce the channels into a net of spheres. [N-220](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-220)  
[U05071](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05071) Garlands, necklaces, and half-[necklaces] too,  
[U05072](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05072) beautiful and encircling, are to be connected.  
[U05073](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05073) The doing of activity is clear and without things.  
[U05074](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05074) Take the foremost of supreme accomplishments;  
[U05075](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05075) make the wheel of adornments at the six places. [N-220](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-220)  
[U05076](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05076) Furthermore, reliance and entry:  
[U05077](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05077) relying on the lama ceases the flow of samsara;  
[U05078](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05078) relying on empowerment ripens body and voice;  
[U05079](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05079) relying on pledges brings accomplishments;  
[U05080](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05080) relying on activity trains the body.  
[U05081](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05081) Whatever arises from these objects of reliance  
[U05082](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05082) is itself pledges and vows.  
[U05083](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05083) Activities of entry have eight aspects;  
[U05084](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05084) bodily entry is fivefold;  
[U05085](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05085) voice has eight aspects;  
[U05086](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05086) ordinary mind's entry is thirty-twofold.  
[U05087](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05087) The special functioning of body and ordinary mind  
[U05088](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05088) is the distinction between karma and primordial knowing. [N-220](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-220)  
[U05089](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05089) Furthermore, the abiding-wheel of cultivation:  
[U05090](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05090) through training the body with diverse bodily exercises,  
[U05091](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05091) ordinary mind comes to abide.  
[U05092](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05092) Through three applications, soothe the channels;  
[U05093](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05093) draw ordinary mind in gently.  
[U05094](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05094) Through applications of signs and skillful craft—[Source variant: ‘craft of signs.’]  
[U05095](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05095) through the distinct methods for body and ordinary mind,  
[U05096](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05096) make the yogin's ordinary mind abide. [N-220](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-220)  
[U05097](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05097) Furthermore, cut the flow of deluded clinging.  
[U05098](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05098) Gather the light of sun and moon; draw in the ocean.  
[U05099](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05099) Weigh the Mount Merus of a three-thousandfold world on a balance.  
[U05100](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05100) Reverse the Ground of ignorance: self-awareness. [N-221](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-221)  
[U05101](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05101) Draw samsara out entirely; block the path of passing beyond sorrow.  
[U05102](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05102) Sweep away phenomena and persons.  
[U05103](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05103) The result is self-complete, the Ground of the nature of phenomena;  
[U05104](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05104) doing and acting self-cease.  
[U05105](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05105) Draw out from the root craving that desires another.  
[U05106](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05106) Delusion concerning phenomena makes a primordial basis. [Source variant: ‘upper basis.’] [N-221](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-221)  
[U05107](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05107) That itself is the object liberated in its own place.  
[U05108](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05108) The three realms made the path, there is no buddha.  
[U05109](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05109) For whom could the teacher's teaching exist? It does not.  
[U05110](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05110) All phenomena are without a Ground; for whom could a root exist?  
[U05111](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05111) Ordinary mind free from existence and without desire,  
[U05112](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05112) uncontrived in intrinsic nature, is beyond expression.  
[U05113](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05113) Deluded conceptual thought of doing and acting is exhausted.  
[U05114](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05114) There are no phenomena, no buddhas, and no sentient beings.  
[U05115](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05115) There is no I, no self, no apprehended object and no apprehending subject. [N-221](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-221)  
[U05116](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05116) Furthermore, I shall explain the one taste of the dharma embodiment.
