[U05117](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05117) It is not expressed in phrases;  
[U05118](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05118) there is not even mere abiding called ordinary mind.  
[U05119](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05119) Because it is absent, in the appearance of emptiness  
[U05120](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05120) the source of mindfulness and knowing is exhausted.  
[U05121](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05121) In the nature of phenomena in which nothing is done,  
[U05122](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05122) there is no arising. Such great wonder!  
[U05123](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05123) In the dharma embodiment without arising,  
[U05124](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05124) there is no going. Such great wonder!  
[U05125](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05125) In awareness with nowhere to circle,  
[U05126](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05126) there is the nature of phenomena without arising.  
[U05127](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05127) Furthermore, I shall explain gathering and evenness.  
[U05128](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05128) Rejecting multiplicity, one is free from convention.  
[U05129](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05129) One is not established; intrinsic nature is pure.  
[U05130](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05130) Intrinsic nature being clear, meaning is accomplished.  
[U05131](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05131) Self-purity and the foundation,  
[U05132](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05132) and the foundation mixed with samsara—[Source variant: ‘foundation of reliance.’]  
[U05133](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05133) hollow and without a core:  
[U05134](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05134) called ‘ya ma rla,’ beyond indication. [N-222](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-222)  
[U05135](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05135) Furthermore, I shall explain the delusion of intrinsic nature.  
[U05136](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05136) Time and faculties,  
[U05137](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05137) distinctions, entry, and respective activities;  
[U05138](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05138) aggregates, elements, and sense bases—  
[U05139](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05139) in an instant when causes and conditions coincide,  
[U05140](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05140) like jewels and masses of cloud,  
[U05141](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05141) they circle like a body and its shadow.  
[U05142](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05142) Through different dependencies upon the basis of delusion,  
[U05143](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05143) the activities of sentient beings are diverse. [N-222](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-222)  
[U05144](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05144) Furthermore, I shall explain pure appearance.  
[U05145](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05145) The vision of the direct perception of the nature of phenomena  
[U05146](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05146) definitely emerges through the faculties' gates  
[U05147](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05147) and is clear in cloudless space.  
[U05148](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05148) The vision of increasing experience:  
[U05149](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05149) the colors of primordial knowing come forth,  
[U05150](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05150) standing upright and arising as spokes,  
[U05151](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05151) as diverse spheres and embodiments,  
[U05152](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05152) clear in the domain of coarse appearance.  
[U05153](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05153) The vision of awareness reaching its limit:  
[U05154](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05154) the complete enjoyment embodiment's marks and signs are clear;  
[U05155](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05155) from rainbows of indeterminate colors,  
[U05156](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05156) the five families appear as fathers and mothers.  
[U05157](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05157) The vision of the exhaustion of nature of phenomena:  
[U05158](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05158) appearances of experience are emptied out,  
[U05159](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05159) the body is exhausted, and the objects of the faculties too are exhausted;  
[U05160](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05160) the assemblage of realizations and delusion is self-liberated, [N-223](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-223)  
[U05161](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05161) and there is freedom from expressed phrases.  
[U05162](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05162) Furthermore, I shall explain the sounds and signs of the elements.  
[U05163](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05163) When fire's sound crackles,  
[U05164](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05164) and sparks wave and proliferate,  
[U05165](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05165) the yogin does not accomplish the aim.  
[U05166](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05166) When it rises openly, blazing without interruption,  
[U05167](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05167) slow and long, with a sharp tip,  
[U05168](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05168) the yogin accomplishes a great aim. [N-224](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-224)  
[U05169](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05169) I shall also explain the colors.  
[U05170](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05170) When it is red with a black tip,  
[U05171](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05171) and pieces break off toward oneself,  
[U05172](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05172) obstacles occur.  
[U05173](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05173) White and clear, like a lotus;  
[U05174](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05174) sharp and of diverse colors;  
[U05175](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05175) wheels, parasols, weapon-points;  
[U05176](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05176) red, clear, limpid rays proliferating;  
[U05177](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05177) vajras, victory banners, bells,  
[U05178](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05178) checkerboard patterns, tents and houses,  
[U05179](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05179) tiered stupas, and so forth:  
[U05180](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05180) for the yogin, there is great completeness. [N-224](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-224)  
[U05181](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05181) Having directly perceived the meaning,  
[U05182](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05182) one passes beyond the three realms.  
[U05183](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05183) Furthermore, the subsidiary aspects of the elements:  
[U05184](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05184) water resounds with diverse sounds  
[U05185](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05185) and shows many white designs;  
[U05186](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05186) the yogin excellently accomplishes the aim.  
[U05187](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05187) Earth, through bouncing and ringing,  
[U05188](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05188) leaping at the edges and rolling, brings accomplishment.  
[U05189](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05189) Wind, through whistling and pressing,  
[U05190](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05190) and through being blue-green and billowing, does likewise. [N-224](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-224)  
[U05191](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05191) If one has not met with such signs,  
[U05192](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05192) the adept repeats the rite and undertakes it again.  
[U05193](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05193) Through three, five, or seven times,  
[U05194](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05194) it will be accomplished; there is no doubt about this.  
[U05195](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05195) Accord definitely with the respective signs. [N-224](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-224)  
[U05196](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05196) Thus, from the Great All-Penetrating Word, Root of All Phenomena:  
[U05197](alignment/chapter-05.md#u05197) the fifth chapter, the Array of View and Cultivation, teaching the root of abiding.  

<a id="part-6"></a>

## Chapter 6

[U05198](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05198) Then, although all phenomena are inexpressible,  
[U05199](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05199) from the many deluded conceptual thoughts of sentient beings,  
[U05200](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05200) also as a foundation for words, phrases, and conventions,  
[U05201](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05201) from uncontrived space,  
[U05202](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05202) phrases arise: the teacher abides in distinction. [N-225](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-225)  
[U05203](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05203) To the buddha arisen from the lotus,  
[U05204](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05204) Maker of Joy supplicated thus:  
[U05205](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05205) ‘Kye kye! You who abide in the lotus's center,  
[U05206](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05206) here in the mandala of space,  
[U05207](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05207) sixty-four groups of a hundred thousand  
[U05208](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05208) drops of light appear.  
[U05209](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05209) What is the cause of the seventeen utmost refinements?  
[U05210](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05210) What is the dharma embodiment like?  
[U05211](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05211) To what is complete enjoyment embodiment applied?  
[U05212](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05212) How many emanation embodiments are there?  
[U05213](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05213) What are the characteristics of embodiment?  
[U05214](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05214) How does it abide?  
[U05215](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05215) What are the embodiments of intrinsic nature and the nature of phenomena?  
[U05216](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05216) Blessed one, speak to me.’  
[U05217](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05217) Then the blessed Vajradhara  
[U05218](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05218) rose from the lotus's center  
[U05219](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05219) and made the gesture of the sky-soarer.  
[U05220](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05220) Merely by doing this,  
[U05221](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05221) from the space of the nature of phenomena's self-appearance,  
[U05222](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05222) from inexpressible intrinsic nature,  
[U05223](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05223) as expressed phrases, these words  
[U05224](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05224) arose naturally, without being made. [N-225](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-225)  

[U05225](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05225) [Source heading: First reply.]  
[U05226](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05226) ‘Kye kye! Lord of Gods, listen.  
[U05227](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05227) I shall truly explain the sixty-four  
[U05228](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05228) groups of a hundred thousand white lights.  
[U05229](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05229) From the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature,  
[U05230](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05230) distinctions of phrases proliferate;  
[U05231](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05231) from three divisions, there are nine expanses.  
[U05232](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05232) When phrases are gathered, there are ślokas:  
[U05233](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05233) sixty-four groups of a hundred thousand,  
[U05234](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05234) with thirty-five thousand chapters  
[U05235](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05235) and twenty-one thousand textual sections.  
[U05236](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05236) What is expressed equals the bounds of space  
[U05237](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05237) and cuts the conceptual thoughts of beings to be trained. [N-226](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-226)  
[U05238](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05238) Here, when the subject matter is closely condensed,  
[U05239](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05239) there are a hundred and eighty fixings,  
[U05240](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05240) a thousand and a half crucial junctures,  
[U05241](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05241) three thousand gatherings of great crucial points,  
[U05242](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05242) four hundred thousand deviations and obscurations,  
[U05243](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05243) and twenty thousand named continua.  
[U05244](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05244) The sequences of expressed phrases are clearly arrayed. [N-226](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-226)  

[U05245](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05245) [Source heading: Second reply.]  
[U05246](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05246) I shall explain the seventeen utmost refinements of light:  
[U05247](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05247) Self-Arising and Self-Liberation,  
[U05248](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05248) Naturally Arisen Itself and Complete at the Root,  
[U05249](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05249) Endowed with Beauty and Jeweled Array,  
[U05250](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05250) Garland of Pearls and Without Letters— [N-227](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-227)  
[U05251](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05251) Mirror of the Core and Mirror of Awakened Mind,  
[U05252](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05252) Union of Sun and Moon, Adorned with Pointing Out,  
[U05253](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05253) Blazing Embodiment-Relics, Heap of Jewels,  
[U05254](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05254) Blazing Lamp, and Six Great Expanses.  
[U05255](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05255) Thus these sixteen sequences  
[U05256](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05256) definitely proliferate from Thal Gyur  
[U05257](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05257) and appear to sentient beings to be trained.  
[U05258](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05258) By holding the teaching in which vital content is gathered,  
[U05259](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05259) one liberates from samsara for as long as ages exist,  
[U05260](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05260) and complete buddhahood manifests. [N-228](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-228)  
[U05261](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05261) The dharma embodiment: speech and awakened mind,  
[U05262](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05262) qualities, and enlightened activity.  
[U05263](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05263) Embodiment is emptiness and clarity,  
[U05264](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05264) an intrinsic nature without signs.  
[U05265](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05265) Speech is free from words, phrases, and names,  
[U05266](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05266) free from expression and discussion.  
[U05267](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05267) Awakened mind, without thinking or movement,  
[U05268](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05268) is beyond proliferation and activity.  
[U05269](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05269) Its quality is neither coming back nor returning;  
[U05270](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05270) basic space and primordial knowing pervade,  
[U05271](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05271) and intrinsic nature is uncontrived.  
[U05272](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05272) Enlightened activity has not come about, arisen, or been done;  
[U05273](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05273) it comes about of its own force and does not cease.  
[U05274](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05274) Not done, there is no doing. [N-229](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-229)  

[U05275](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05275) [Source heading: Fourth reply.]  
[U05276](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05276) The complete enjoyment embodiment too has embodiment, speech, awakened mind,  
[U05277](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05277) qualities, and enlightened activity—five.  
[U05278](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05278) Embodiment appears but has no intrinsic nature.  
[U05279](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05279) Clarity and that which makes clear are pervaded by awareness.  
[U05280](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05280) Speech is naturally arisen and self-appearance,  
[U05281](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05281) a wheel of the aspects of proliferating and gathering.  
[U05282](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05282) Awakened mind is seeing without a flow,  
[U05283](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05283) the expressiveness of knowing, and enlightened intent. [N-229](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-229)  
[U05284](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05284) Qualities: the marks and signs are complete;  
[U05285](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05285) clear enlightened intent and higher knowing are complete.  
[U05286](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05286) Enlightened activity: from the wheel of Dharma,  
[U05287](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05287) outer, inner, and secret [teachings] unfold,  
[U05288](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05288) and for the buddhas of the respective families  
[U05289](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05289) and for bodhisattvas too,  
[U05290](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05290) they unfold from the naturally arisen tongue,  
[U05291](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05291) showing the retinue through their own essence. [N-229](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-229)  

[U05292](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05292) [Source heading: Fifth reply.]  
[U05293](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05293) The emanation embodiment too has embodiment, speech, awakened mind,  
[U05294](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05294) qualities, and enlightened activity—five in each case.  
[U05295](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05295) Embodiment, with marks and signs,  
[U05296](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05296) emanates bodies for whatever beings to be trained appear.  
[U05297](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05297) Speech has excellent phrase-composition:  
[U05298](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05298) the six aspects of Brahmā's voice.  
[U05299](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05299) Awakened mind, through primordial knowing of objects of knowledge,  
[U05300](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05300) abides in enlightened intent for the two benefits, one's own and others'.  
[U05301](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05301) Qualities are completeness of knowing.  
[U05302](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05302) Enlightened activity completes without remainder  
[U05303](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05303) the outer, inner, and secret deeds,  
[U05304](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05304) abiding in five perfections. [N-230](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-230)  

[U05305](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05305) [Source heading: Sixth reply.]  
[U05306](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05306) Embodiment's characteristics, essence,  
[U05307](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05307) definitive wording, and divisions;  
[U05308](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05308) the respective fields and beings to be trained;  
[U05309](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05309) the activities of differentiation and the measure of arising—  
[U05310](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05310) through distinctions in characteristics and understanding:  
[U05311](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05311) characteristics are established from phenomena,  
[U05312](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05312) established from desirable qualities,  
[U05313](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05313) and established from diverse methods.  
[U05314](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05314) Understanding: intrinsic nature is pure;  
[U05315](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05315) the respective domains of appearance are shown;  
[U05316](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05316) and, endowed with form, the faculties are complete. [N-230](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-230)  

[U05317](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05317) [Source heading: Seventh reply.]  
[U05318](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05318) Embodiment's manner of abiding has three aspects:  
[U05319](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05319) because it is liberated from phenomena of signs,  
[U05320](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05320) it is not a form marked by divisions;  
[U05321](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05321) because the intrinsic nature of clarity is complete,  
[U05322](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05322) it appears but has no intrinsic nature;  
[U05323](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05323) to train the continua of those to be trained,  
[U05324](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05324) it abides unmoving in great equipoise.  
[U05325](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05325) Unmoving and clear,  
[U05326](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05326) it appears as the aspect of awareness and knowing:  
[U05327](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05327) even, without partiality, and undivided. [N-230](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-230)  

[U05328](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05328) [Source heading: Eighth reply.]  
[U05329](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05329) The intrinsic nature and nature of phenomena of embodiment:  
[U05330](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05330) intrinsic nature is emptiness and clarity,  
[U05331](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05331) the unceasing manifestation of knowing and awareness.  
[U05332](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05332) The nature of phenomena of embodiment is complete;  
[U05333](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05333) there is no return, going, or coming,  
[U05334](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05334) and no speaking, thinking, or expression.  
[U05335](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05335) Furthermore, accomplishing embodiments:  
[U05336](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05336) through the distinctions taught above,  
[U05337](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05337) in one's own ordinary mind, the seed of the dharma embodiment,  
[U05338](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05338) apply the crucial point of direct perception,  
[U05339](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05339) and become constantly familiar with it.  
[U05340](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05340) This is accomplishing the dharma embodiment.  
[U05341](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05341) Train in desirable qualities, the seed of the complete enjoyment embodiment,  
[U05342](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05342) and become familiar with them:  
[U05343](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05343) this is accomplishing complete enjoyment.  
[U05344](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05344) As for the seed of the emanation embodiment, the four elements:  
[U05345](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05345) train in their word and meaning;  
[U05346](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05346) through familiarity, the emanation embodiment is accomplished. [N-231](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-231)  
[U05347](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05347) Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of fire's activities.  
[U05348](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05348) Distinguished through colors:  
[U05349](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05349) white, clear, and gentle accomplishes pacifying;  
[U05350](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05350) yellow, subtle, and long accomplishes increasing;  
[U05351](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05351) red, limpid, and proliferating is the activity of power;  
[U05352](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05352) blue-green, black, and short accomplishes the fierce.  
[U05353](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05353) When it is variegated, broken, and whirling,  
[U05354](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05354) distinctions of various activities are accomplished.  
[U05355](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05355) When it is extremely subtle, there is falling.  
[U05356](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05356) When broken and fierce, obstacles arise.  
[U05357](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05357) When spreading tongues of fire have smoke,  
[U05358](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05358) pledges deteriorate. [N-232](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-232)  
[U05359](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05359) Distinctions through sound:  
[U05360](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05360) three successive lhab lhab sounds and many spar spar sounds  
[U05361](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05361) accomplish diverse [activities].  
[U05362](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05362) Fierceness and crackling [accomplish] the fierce;  
[U05363](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05363) roaring and dense [sound accomplish] pacifying and increasing, the two;  
[U05364](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05364) resounding and ringing accomplish the supreme. [N-232](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-232)  
[U05365](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05365) Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of water's activities.  
[U05366](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05366) The definitive signs of colors:  
[U05367](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05367) through the four beginning with white,  
[U05368](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05368) pacifying and the rest are definitely accomplished.  
[U05369](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05369) When brown-black, rough, and whirling,  
[U05370](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05370) the yogin does not accomplish the aim.  
[U05371](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05371) When white and clear, rising and billowing,  
[U05372](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05372) the supreme accomplishment is definitely attained.  
[U05373](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05373) Through sounds that resound and ring,  
[U05374](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05374) whoosh, roar, and are dense,  
[U05375](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05375) food, clothing, and the result are accomplished.  
[U05376](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05376) Through sir, sil, and lhab sounds,  
[U05377](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05377) the bliss of each of the three embodiments is attained. [N-232](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-232)  
[U05378](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05378) Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of earth's activities.  
[U05379](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05379) Know white, red, and the other colors from the preceding account.  
[U05380](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05380) Indeterminate change does not accomplish the aim.  
[U05381](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05381) Clarity and shining accomplish the supreme.  
[U05382](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05382) Its sounds are shag and sdig— [N-232](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-232)  
[U05383](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05383) Furthermore, I shall explain the scope of wind's activities.  
[U05384](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05384) White, red, and the other colors are as above.  
[U05385](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05385) Blackness and turbulence do not accomplish the aim.  
[U05386](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05386) Variegated blazing accomplishes the supreme.  
[U05387](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05387) Its sounds are roaring, muffled, and diverse;  
[U05388](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05388) ringing and diverse [sounds] bring supreme attainment. [N-232](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-232)  
[U05389](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05389) Furthermore, I shall explain signs and intrinsic nature.  
[U05390](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05390) After the preceding signs arise,  
[U05391](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05391) after three months and six months,  
[U05392](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05392) through the two, one knows the sounds of the insentient and the aware.  
[U05393](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05393) After ten months and eighteen months,  
[U05394](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05394) each respective sound and the voices of the six classes  
[U05395](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05395) are known just as they are uttered. [N-233](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-233)  
[U05396](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05396) Furthermore, the stages of the result:  
[U05397](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05397) the dharma embodiment is through the activity of ordinary mind;  
[U05398](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05398) through complete enjoyment embodiment, [it] is attained;  
[U05399](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05399) the emanation embodiment is through the body.  
[U05400](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05400) Essence itself is through equal portions. [N-233](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-233)  
[U05401](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05401) The body: the elements are pure.  
[U05402](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05402) Voice is joined to āli and kāli.  
[U05403](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05403) Ordinary mind, without things, is unimpeded penetration.  
[U05404](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05404) The gathering of the three is great bliss.  
[U05405](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05405) Furthermore, this is to be fully explained:  
[U05406](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05406) through thoroughly knowing embodiment's characteristics,  
[U05407](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05407) all delusion ceases at one time.  
[U05408](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05408) Through fully knowing embodiment's intrinsic nature,  
[U05409](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05409) the boundary between clarity and emptiness is held.  
[U05410](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05410) Through knowing essence, there is purification into emptiness.  
[U05411](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05411) Through knowing ‘dang’ [reading unresolved], non-conceptuality arises. [N-234](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-234)  
[U05412](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05412) Knowing the great identity,  
[U05413](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05413) one cuts the root of samsara in the three realms.  
[U05414](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05414) Through these, confidence in self-liberation  
[U05415](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05415) is certainly attained without anything being done.  
[U05416](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05416) Ema! Such wondrous marvel!  
[U05417](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05417) In space in which nothing is done,  
[U05418](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05418) uncontrived phrases arise;  
[U05419](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05419) unthought, naturally arisen, they are understood by the conceptual mind.  
[U05420](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05420) Having understood thus, through knowing and realization,  
[U05421](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05421) [one trusts] that, as the root of all the scriptural collections,  
[U05422](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05422) this king of continua, Thal Gyur itself,  
[U05423](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05423) this most secret, unsurpassed teaching,  
[U05424](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05424) is indeed this. [N-235](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-235)  
[U05425](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05425) All the retinues appearing through intrinsic nature,  
[U05426](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05426) having aroused an utterly delighted ordinary mind,  
[U05427](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05427) read and preserve this continuum,  
[U05428](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05428) write it and hold it;  
[U05429](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05429) holding and preserving it at the throat and crown,  
[U05430](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05430) they engage in honoring all buddhas.  
[U05431](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05431) Having accepted and pledged this,  
[U05432](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05432) they praise the teacher, the blessed buddha—  
[U05433](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05433) his embodiment complete in qualities. [N-235](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-235)  
[U05434](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05434) Then all those fully assembled,  
[U05435](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05435) with ordinary minds of joy, delight, and gladness arisen,  
[U05436](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05436) [behold] space, self-purified and clear,  
[U05437](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05437) and inexpressible phrases self-arising.  
[U05438](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05438) Rejoicing and finding bliss,  
[U05439](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05439) they become even with freedom from conceptual elaborations  
[U05440](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05440) in the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature. [N-235](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-235)  
[U05441](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05441) Of all phenomena as the single unique sphere,  
[U05442](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05442) having attained the enlightened intent of equal taste,  
[U05443](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05443) [to] the lotus-arisen blessed one,  
[U05444](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05444) they say, ‘Excellent! Excellent! Excellent!  
[U05445](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05445) You have ripened us as the three embodiments;  
[U05446](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05446) we supplicate and rejoice.’ [N-235](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-235)  
[U05447](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05447) This is called the Array of Embodiments,  
[U05448](alignment/chapter-06.md#u05448) the sixth chapter.  

<a id="part-7"></a>

## Closing material

[U05449](alignment/closing-material.md#u05449) Causing Precious Things to Arise:  
[U05450](alignment/closing-material.md#u05450) the continuum called the Great All-Penetrating Word, Taught as the Root of All Phenomena,  
[U05451](alignment/closing-material.md#u05451) specially arrayed from the sixty-four hundred-thousand ślokas of the Great Perfection of intrinsic nature, is complete. [N-236](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-236)  
[U05452](alignment/closing-material.md#u05452) Seal! Seal! Seal!  
[U05453](alignment/closing-material.md#u05453) Seal! Seal! Seal!  
[U05454](alignment/closing-material.md#u05454) Seal! Seal! Seal!  
[U05455](alignment/closing-material.md#u05455) This king of continua  
[U05456](alignment/closing-material.md#u05456) is not available to just anyone, except exceptional beings.  
[U05457](alignment/closing-material.md#u05457) Glorious queen guarding mantra, dark-brown Wrathful Queen, Copper-Faced One: protect it! [N-237](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-237)  
[U05458](alignment/closing-material.md#u05458) Samaya.  
[U05459](alignment/closing-material.md#u05459) So phag.  
[U05460](alignment/closing-material.md#u05460) Ag tham. [N-237](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-237)  
[U05461](alignment/closing-material.md#u05461) Glorious Black Protector, I entrust it to you.  
[U05462](alignment/closing-material.md#u05462) If it is given to an unfit vessel, impose the penalty of the pledge. [N-237](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-237)  
[U05463](alignment/closing-material.md#u05463) Samaya.  
[U05464](alignment/closing-material.md#u05464) Seal! Seal! Seal!  
[U05465](alignment/closing-material.md#u05465) The Thal Gyur continuum has seven mother-and-child [texts]. [N-238](REVIEW-NOTES.md#n-238)  
[U05466](alignment/closing-material.md#u05466) Virtue! Virtue! Virtue!
